### [1] [The Frustrometer: Detecting User Frustration in Data Visualization Tasks using Biomarkers and Interaction Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13687)
*Johannes Ellemose,Sophia Wanner,Djordje Slijepčević,Laura Cesar,Vanessa Leung,Wolfgang Aigner,Niklas Elmqvist*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出Frustrometer，通过融合生理信号（心率、皮电、眼动、瞳孔、头部方向）和交互信号（鼠标、键盘）构建CNN分类器，在可视化分析任务中实时预测用户是否陷入困境。研究发现：自主生理信号（心率、皮电）和头部方向对困惑检测价值有限，而鼠标移动和眼动数据包含主要的预测信号，其中鼠标移动在部分参与者中表现尤为突出，提示轻量级方案即可实现实时困惑检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可视化研究主要解决了“如何”在用户卡顿或沮丧时提供帮助（如交互式引导、上下文帮助），但尚未解决“何时”触发帮助这一关键问题——过早触发会打断用户思路，过晚则用户已误入歧途。

Method: 设计Frustrometer系统：1）在控制实验中收集14名参与者在两个交互式可视化仪表盘上执行分析任务时的多模态数据（眼动、瞳孔、皮电、心率、头部方向、鼠标、键盘）；2）参与者自评表现，同时人工标注任务中陷入困境的时刻；3）构建卷积神经网络分类器进行实时困境预测。

Result: 自主生理信号（心率、皮电）和头部方向对困惑检测贡献有限；鼠标移动和眼动数据携带了主要的预测信号，其中鼠标移动对部分参与者具有强相关性，表明轻量级仪器即可实现有效的实时困惑检测。

Conclusion: 研究结果为复杂可视化任务的适应性引导系统设计提供了依据，建议采用多模态方法进行困惑和困境检测。

Abstract: Visualization research has largely solved \textit{how} to help a stuck or frustrated user -- through interactive onboarding, contextual help, and active guidance. The unsolved problem is \textit{when}: trigger help too eagerly and you break the user's train of thought; wait too long and they have already gone astray. We present the \textsc{Frustrometer}, a series of experiments to predict user stuckness and frustration by fusing physiological and interaction signals. The Frustrometer consists of a convolutional neural network classifier, that in real-time estimates whether user are stuck in their task or not. We collected data from a controlled study where 14 participants performed analytical tasks on two interactive visualization dashboards while we captured eye movement, pupil dilation, galvanic skin response, heart-rate, head orientation, mouse dynamics, and keyboard events. In addition participants assessed their own performance, while we annotated when during the tasks the participants were stuck. Our results reveal that autonomous physiological responses such as heart-rate and galvanic skin response provide limited insights into the frustration level of the user. Similarly, head orientations are not easily correlated with the frustrations felt by the user during visual analysis tasks. Mouse movements and gaze data conversely carry the majority of predictive signal, with mouse movements alone having a strong correlation for some participants, suggesting that lightweight instrumentation may suffice for real-time frustration detection. We end the paper by discussing how these findings can inform the design of adaptive guidance systems for complex visualization tasks that takes a multimodal approach to frustration and stuckness detection.

</details>


### [2] [Rethinking the UI of GenUI: A Tale of Two Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13843)
*Xiang `Anthony' Chen,Savvas Dimitrios Petridis,Tian Deng,Humad Bari,Ruofei Du,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究对比了两种GenUI设计方法：传统的非结构化、深度优先、高保真生成方式与作者提出的结构化输入、广度优先、低保真生成方式。通过对24名UX设计师和产品经理的对比实验，揭示了两种方法各自的优缺点，并提出了GenUI及类似AI创意支持工具的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GenUI工具沿袭了对话式大模型的惯例（非结构化提示、深度优先、高保真），但这些方式是否能有效支持早期从0到1的设计探索尚不明确，因此需要重新审视并提出新的设计范式。

Method: 提出了一种对比性设计方法（结构化输入、广度优先探索、低保真生成），并与现有GenUI工具进行对照实验，招募24名UX设计师和产品经理进行小型设计探索练习。

Result: 结构化输入能揭示关键设计维度但增加工作量和入门门槛；广度优先工作流能展现更多可能性但预览多屏UX想法仍然困难；专业人士更偏好高保真度，因为它符合实践且GenAI提高了保真度期望。

Conclusion: 总结了GenUI及类似AI驱动的创意支持工具的设计启示，指出在早期设计探索中需要平衡结构化与非结构化输入、广度与深度探索、低保真与高保真之间的权衡。

Abstract: GenUI is an emergent class of AI tools that use large models to generate UI mock-ups based on users' high-level descriptions, promising to democratize UX design exploration for a broader audience. Most GenUI designs to date tend to inherit the conventions of conversational large models, such as ChatGPT and Gemini, where a user describes their design needs primarily via an unstructured prompt, and the tool then takes a depth-first approach, delving into the design right away and producing a high-fidelity prototype. In this research, we rethink how well this unstructured, depth-first, and high-fidelity GenUI design can support early-stage, 0-to-1 design exploration. To probe this question, we propose a contrastive design with structured input, breadth-first exploration, and low-fidelity generation. We then conducted a comparison study with 24 UX designers and product managers who conducted mini design exploration exercises using an existing GenUI tool and our contrastive GenUI tool. Findings reveal participants' perceived benefits and trade-offs of the two GenUI designs: structured input surfaces key facets but requires more work, raising entry barriers to start exploration; breadth-first workflow reveals more possibilities, but previewing UX ideas spanning many screens remains hard; and though low fidelity has value, professionals favor high fidelity because it fits practice and GenAI heightens fidelity expectations. We conclude with design implications for GenUI and similar AI-powered creativity support tools.

</details>


### [3] [SpheriCity: Designing Trustworthy Conversational AI for Sustainability Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13854)
*Ahmed Qayyum,Madison Werner,Kathryn Youngblood,Jenna R. Jambeck,Tahiya Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SpheriCity 是一个以溯源为先导的对话式原型系统，旨在帮助可持续发展专家从城市循环性评估报告中可信地进行知识梳理。系统通过透明溯源、结构化合成和交互支架，支持跨文档比较与探索性查询。六位专家的形成性评估表明，透明溯源、上下文解释、可解释性以及与专家工作流程的对齐，显著影响专家对系统可信度和有用性的判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市循环性评估报告虽包含丰富的材料、基础设施和政策干预信息，但其篇幅冗长、结构异构，使得跨文档综合与比较对循环经济领域的从业者和学者而言十分困难。现有大语言模型虽能加速知识获取，但其不透明的推理过程、幻觉和缺乏来源透明度在高风险的可持续发展背景下带来信任与可解释性风险，亟需验证。

Method: 构建了一个溯源优先的对话式代理系统 SpheriCity，强调证据可追溯性、结构化合成和交互支架，以支持探索性查询和跨文档综合。随后邀请六位可持续发展专家，使用涵盖跨城市比较、政策总结和推荐导向任务的代表性查询，对系统进行了形成性专家评估。

Result: 透明溯源、上下文解释、可解释性以及与专家工作流程的对齐，是影响专家对系统信任度和有用性判断的关键因素。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献了：（1）一个面向可持续发展知识梳理的对话式原型系统；（2）一个面向高风险知识领域中 AI 响应的专家评估框架；（3）关于溯源、不确定性沟通和工作流程整合如何影响专家用户对 AI 辅助可持续发展决策支持信任度的设计洞见。

Abstract: We present SpheriCity, an expert-grounded conversational prototype designed to support trustworthy knowledge sensemaking from sustainability reports. City-level circularity assessment reports contain rich information about materials, infrastructure, and policy interventions, yet their length and heterogeneous structure make cross-document synthesis and comparison difficult for practitioners and researchers working on circular economy initiatives. While large language models (LLM) promise faster knowledge access and synthesis, their opaque reasoning, hallucinations, and lack of source transparency introduce risks for trust and interpretability, and require verification in high-stakes sustainability contexts. SpheriCity addresses these challenges through a provenance-first conversational agent that foregrounds evidence traceability, structured synthesis, and interaction scaffolds to support exploratory querying and cross-document synthesis across sustainability reports. We conducted a formative expert review with six sustainability experts using representative queries spanning cross-city comparison, policy summarization, and recommendation-oriented tasks. Experts evaluated responses across dimensions and provided qualitative reflections on the system's usefulness for sustainability knowledge work. Our results reveal that transparent sourcing, contextual explanation, interpretability, and alignment with expert workflow strongly shape expert trust and judgments of system usefulness. This work contributes (1) a conversational prototype for sustainability knowledge sensemaking, (2) an expert-grounded evaluation framework for assessing AI responses in high-stakes knowledge domains, and (3) design insights into how provenance, uncertainty communication, and integration in workflow influence expert users' trust in AI assistance for sustainability decision support.

</details>


### [4] [The Silent Cost of Artificial Intelligence Assistance: A Theory of Autonomy Surrender, the Recovery Mechanism, and the Restoration of Human Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13962)
*Ancuta Margondai,Julie Rader,Emma Rader,Sara Willox,Mustapha Mouloua*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文基于HIAG框架，提出了一个可测量的自主性让渡理论模型，描述了人类在AI辅助下逐步丧失自主性的过程，包括静默成本、让渡阈值和恢复机制三个核心机制，并预言了偏好反转这一终端状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与人类决策环境的整合带来了一个未被充分理论化的代价——人类自主性的逐步让渡。现有研究对此缺乏系统性理论框架和量化手段。

Method: 基于HIAG框架构建理论模型，提出三个相互作用机制：AI辅助的静默成本、让渡阈值、恢复机制，并预测偏好反转作为终端状态。

Result: 识别出自主性让渡是可测量的累积过程；提出认知带宽消耗是自主性让渡的驱动因素；揭示偏好反转状态下功能依赖被体验为偏好，使问题从设计领域扩展至文化与政治领域。

Conclusion: AI系统设计必须纳入结构化的恢复机制作为人类重新进入决策回路的主动路径；偏好反转将自主性恢复从设计问题转变为文化与政治问题；对AI系统设计、治理框架和人因研究提出启示。

Abstract: The integration of artificial intelligence into human decision-making environments has introduced a previously undertheorized cost: the gradual surrender of human autonomy in exchange for access to information and computational assistance. Building on the Human Identity and Autonomy Gap (HIAG) framework, this paper advances a theoretical model of autonomy surrender as a measurable, cumulative process driven by cognitive bandwidth depletion. The model proposes three interacting mechanisms: the silent cost of AI assistance, in which autonomy is transferred incrementally and without awareness; the surrender threshold, beyond which reclaiming autonomous function becomes cognitively and psychologically difficult; and the recovery mechanism, which establishes the design obligation and the ethical responsibility accompanying deliberate human re-assumption of control. The paper argues that human re-entry into the decision loop is not a passive option but an active cognitive event requiring intentional bandwidth restoration. The design of AI systems must incorporate structured re-entry pathways, here termed recovery mechanisms, that preserve human agency while appropriately distributing responsibility. The model further predicts a terminal state, here termed preference inversion, in which functional dependence on AI assistance is experienced not as a deficit but as a preference, transforming the restoration of autonomy from a design problem into a cultural and political one. Implications are drawn for AI system design, governance frameworks, and human factors research.

</details>


### [5] [Visible Adoption, Untracked Contribution: GitHub Evidence of the Accountability Gap Across Three Cohorts of an HCI Prototyping Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14054)
*Maria Teresa Parreira,Pranav Prabhat Sinha,Hauke Sandhaus,Wendy Ju*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过对2022、2023、2025三个学年同一门研究生HCI原型设计课程中203个GitHub仓库（23,065次提交）的纵向分析，追踪学生GenAI采用情况的变化。研究区分了AI问责的两层：披露（声明使用了AI工具）和归因（将特定产出或任务归功于AI工具）。结果显示，工具披露率从0%增长到66%，但明确归因仍是少数实践，两者差距揭示了问责缺失。到2025年，AI已嵌入课程模板和学生构建的设备中。研究认为，基于披露的问责框架在"氛围编码"时代已不足够，问题不是学生隐瞒AI使用，而是为偶发、可识别行为构建的规范无法捕捉持续、环境化的共同创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GenAI工具快速普及的背景下，现有的AI问责框架（如简单披露是否使用AI）已不足以理解学生在课程中与AI的真实协作模式。本研究旨在揭示从偶发使用到AI基础设施化的转变过程中，问责机制的缺口。

Method: 纵向观察性案例研究，对2022、2023、2025三个学年同一门研究生HCI原型设计课程进行跨队列比较，计算分析203个GitHub仓库和23,065次学生提交，并基于对2023队列的已有定性研究，区分了"披露"和"归因"两个问责层次。

Result: 工具披露率从2022年的0%增长到2025年的66%；明确贡献归因始终是少数实践；披露与归因之间存在显著差距；到2025年，AI已从外部工具转变为嵌入课程基础设施的组件。

Conclusion: 基于披露的问责框架在"氛围编码"（vibe-coding）时代已不足够——真正的问题不是学生隐藏AI使用，而是为偶发、可识别行为设计的规范无法捕捉持续、环境化的共同创作。研究呼吁建立真正的"共同思考"问责机制。

Abstract: This paper presents a longitudinal, observational case study of how student GenAI adoption shifted across three cohorts (Fall 2022, 2023, and 2025) of the same graduate-level HCI prototyping course, using computational analysis of 203 GitHub repositories with student activity and 23,065 student commits. Building on a prior qualitative study of the 2023 cohort, we distinguish two levels of AI accountability trace: disclosure (naming that an AI tool was used) and attribution (crediting a specific artifact or task to an AI tool). We find that tool disclosure grew from 0% to 66% of repositories across the three cohorts, while explicit contribution attribution remains a minority practice, and the gap between the two reveals where accountability is missing even among students who disclose. By 2025, AI is infrastructure embedded in course templates and student-built devices: students increasingly name the tools they used, but rarely specify what those tools contributed. We argue that disclosure-based frameworks are insufficient for the vibe-coding era. The failure is not that students conceal AI use; it is that a norm built for episodic, identifiable acts cannot capture continuous, ambient co-creation. We offer this case study as grounding for the workshop's conversation about what genuine co-thinking accountability looks like.

</details>


### [6] [Thinking Outside the [Chat]Box: Bridging Computer Science and Industrial Design for Cognitive-Inclusive Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14306)
*Virginia Francisco,Daniel Guasch,Raquel Hervás*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过跨学科协同设计挑战，比较计算机科学与工业设计学生为智力障碍人群设计的生成式AI交互界面，提出了超越传统聊天框的双层支架框架，包括结构支架（可预测性、导航性、信任）与体验支架（节奏、注意力引导、多模态），为认知无障碍的GenAI交互拓展了设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GenAI界面主要局限于聊天框交互，对智力障碍（ID）用户存在高认知负荷、提示撰写困难、信息过载及可靠性评估机制缺乏等问题。亟需探索替代性交互模型以提升认知无障碍性。

Method: 开展跨学科协同设计挑战，由计算机科学和工业设计两个学生团队基于相同功能需求集（提示支架、结构化输出、GUI精炼、透明度、个性化）开发界面概念，通过比较分析整合两大领域的互补贡献。

Result: 两类团队在基础需求上达成共识（初始校准、主动提示、响应片段直接操作）。计算机科学团队侧重于结构支架（可靠性指标、显式来源、长对话上下文管理）；工业设计团队侧重于体验支架（分步响应流、聚焦模式、助手式集成）。最终提炼出双层支架框架。

Conclusion: 双层支架框架超越了以聊天为中心的模式，拓展了认知无障碍GenAI交互的设计空间。未来工作需开展专家精炼、技术可行性验证以及与ID用户的实证评估。

Abstract: Current Generative AI (GenAI) interfaces remain largely constrained to chatbox interaction, which can impose high cognitive demands on users and create substantial barriers for people with intellectual disabilities (ID), including prompt formulation difficulties, response overload, and limited mechanisms to assess information reliability. To explore alternative interaction models for cognitive accessibility, we conducted a cross-disciplinary co-design challenge in which two student cohorts (Computer Science and Industrial Design) developed interface concepts from the same set of functional requirements (e.g., prompt scaffolding, structured output, GUI-based refinement, transparency, and personalization). Comparing the resulting proposals reveals both convergence on foundational requirements (notably initial calibration, proactive prompting, and direct manipulation of response fragments) and complementary contributions that outline a multi-layered support system. Computer Science teams primarily produced structural scaffolding, emphasizing predictability, navigability, and trust through mechanisms such as reliability indicators, explicit sources, and context management for long conversations. Industrial Design teams emphasized experiential scaffolding, focusing on pacing, attention guidance, multimodality, and proactive agency, including step-by-step response flows, focus modes, and assistant-like integrations. We synthesize these findings into a dual-layer scaffolding framework that expands the design space for cognitively accessible GenAI interaction beyond chat-centric models and motivates future work on expert refinement, technical feasibility, and empirical validation with users with ID.

</details>


### [7] [Friction in AI-Assisted Clinical Decision-Making: A Case Study on The Role of Questions and 'What-if' Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14406)
*Simon WS Fischer,Hanna Schraffenberger,Miranda L. van Hooff,Serge Thill,Pim Haselager*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过临床专家访谈，评估了两种摩擦形式（数据驱动问题和"what-if"分析）在决策支持系统中的作用，发现问题虽未促进反思但可作提醒，"what-if"分析有助于改善患者护理，系统被视为有潜力的培训工具，并提出了工作实践中摩擦设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对临床决策中过度依赖决策支持系统的问题，探索通过增加摩擦（friction）促进认知参与和反思的方法，以缓解对AI的过度依赖。

Method: 基于临床实践中使用的决策支持系统复刻原型，通过情境访谈（n=7）收集临床医生对数据驱动问题和"what-if"分析两种摩擦形式的反馈。

Result: 数据驱动问题对促进反思性思维帮助不大，但可作为提醒；"what-if"假设分析被认为有助于改善患者护理；系统被视为有潜力的新手培训工具。

Conclusion: 基于临床医生反馈，提出了工作实践中摩擦设计的具体建议，为促进反思以缓解AI过度依赖的人机交互研究做出了贡献。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making is augmented by decision-support systems (DSSs). To counter overreliance on DSSs, several methods have been proposed that create friction in order to promote cognitive engagement and reflection. In this paper, we investigate how two such forms of friction, namely data-driven questions and `what-if' analysis, are perceived by medical experts. For a real-world decision task, we replicated a DSS used in clinical practice and gathered clinicians' feedback on a prototype through in-situ interviews (n=7). Our findings suggest that while the questions were perceived as unhelpful for reflective thinking, they could serve as reminders to consider relevant information. Furthermore, inspecting `what-if' hypotheticals was found useful for potentially improving patient care. Clinicians saw our prototype as a promising training tool for novice clinicians. From the clinicians' feedback, we make recommendations for designing friction in work practices. Our work contributes to human-AI interaction research, which aims to encourage reflection to mitigate AI overreliance.

</details>


### [8] [Fabula: Building a Narrative Storytelling Sidekick with the Writers' Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14411)
*Piotr Mirowski,Ben Wedin,Reinald Kim Amplayo,Rich Galt,Duncan Williams,Rida Qadri,Jaume Sanchez-Elias,Erin Drake-Kajioka,Sian Gooding,Lucia Lopez-Rivilla,Joao G. M. Araujo,Lion Schulz,Satinder Baveja,Shakir Mohamed,Edward Grefenstette,Laura Rimell,Richard Evans*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Fabula是一款基于叙事学理论的交互式写作应用，通过分层故事结构（场景/节拍）和基于语言模型的自动评估器辅助作家创作，经42名专家和大量用户测试，探讨了AI评估、UI设计、叙事理论适用性和迭代创作对写作质量与创造力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为小说作家设计一款利用详细叙事计划和通用叙事学理论的交互式写作工具，弥补现有写作辅助工具在理论基础和结构支持方面的不足。

Method: 采用参与式AI方法：通过设计访谈和写作环节（42名专家）、大规模内部和外部测试进行迭代评估；使用基于语言模型的自动评估器（经人类专家偏好优化）；将Fabula作为文化探针进行对抗性设计。

Result: 验证了四个关键设计选择：语言模型自动评估器可提升故事质量；用户期望同时展示详细叙事计划和故事脚本；叙事学假设在本地化叙事传统中需调整且适用于编剧/剧作家；对故事计划的收敛迭代支持作家创造力。

Conclusion: Fabula展示了将叙事学理论与AI技术结合支持创意写作的可行性，同时揭示了跨文化叙事传统差异和对抗性设计在写作反馈与交互叙事中的潜力。

Abstract: We design and evaluate Fabula, an interactive app for fiction writers. Fabula uses detailed narrative plans informed by general narratological theory. Stories are structured hierarchically into scenes and beats that can be (re)generated and revised at script and story plan level. Using participatory AI, we critically evaluate and improve Fabula with casual and published writers, via design interviews and writing sessions with 42 experts, and large-scale internal and external testing. We interrogate our design choices: (1) whether a language model-based auto-evaluator, optimized on human experts' preferences, can improve story quality, (2) whether users want UI that exposes the detailed narrative plan alongside the story script, (3) to what extent our narratology assumptions fit localised storytelling traditions and serve screenwriters or playwrights, and (4) whether convergent iteration over the story plan supports writers' creativity. Building on critical feedback and concerns, we use Fabula as a cultural probe in adversarial design, and identify potentials for writing feedback and for interactive storytelling.

</details>


### [9] [The Self-Aware Body: A User-Centered Framework for Designing Therapeutic Sonic Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14664)
*Prithvi Ravi Kantan,Sofia Dahl,Erika G. Spaich*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于设计治疗性声音交互技术的框架，聚焦于运动声化（将身体运动实时转换为声音作为康复反馈）。框架包含三个贡献：将设计任务重新概念化为校准声音变异性以适应感知可供性和临床需求；基于专业音频混音工作流的实用设计平台；以及以用户为中心的开发方法论。通过HearWalk系统实例说明，并探讨了LLM和AI工具在设计各阶段的作用与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管运动声化技术在运动康复中展现出有效性，但尚未被临床实践系统采用，原因可能在于缺乏标准化的开发方法论以及临床利益相关者视角未能充分整合到交互设计中。

Method: 框架包含三个相互关联的贡献：(1) 概念重构——将设计任务视为校准声音变异性以适应听者的感知可供性和临床情境需求；(2) 实践设计平台——借鉴专业音频混音工作流，建立结构化的信号流架构，支持快速迭代探索；(3) 以用户为中心的开发方法论——源自医疗干预科学，将设计决策建立在与临床医生和患者的互动基础上。

Result: 通过HearWalk生物反馈系统（用于偏瘫步态康复）展示了该框架的实际应用。探讨了大型语言模型和AI工具在设计各阶段的有效辅助作用，同时指出人类临床和专业感知经验不可替代的领域。

Conclusion: 该框架通过标准化设计方法论和整合临床利益相关者视角，有望推动运动声化技术从研究走向临床实践的系统性应用。AI工具可辅助部分设计流程，但人类临床和感知专业知识的核心地位不可替代。

Abstract: This chapter presents a framework for designing therapeutic sonic interaction technologies, with a focus on movement sonification: the real-time conversion of bodily motion into sound that serves as feedback during motor rehabilitation. Despite growing evidence for their effectiveness, technologies implementing movement sonification are yet to be systematically adopted as part of clinical practice, potentially due to a lack of standardized development methodologies as well as inadequate integration of clinical stakeholder perspectives into interaction design. The framework addresses these barriers through three interconnected contributions. The first is a conceptual reframing of the design task as the calibration of sonic variability to the perceptual affordances of the listener and the demands of the clinical context. The second is a practical design platform inspired by professional audio mixing workflows, which imposes a structured and learnable signal-flow architecture on the interaction design process and enables rapid iterative exploration. The third is a user-centered development methodology adapted from healthcare intervention science, which grounds design decisions in engagement with the clinicians and patients who will use the resulting systems. The HearWalk biofeedback system for hemiparetic gait rehabilitation illustrates the framework, and the chapter concludes by examining where large language models and AI tools can meaningfully assist each stage of this design process, as well as where human clinical and perceptual expertise remains irreplaceable.

</details>

### [10] [Learning Dynamic Swing-Up of an Inverted Pendulum using Remote Magnetic Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13915)
*Viacheslav Sydora,Jasan Zughaibi,Denis von Arx,Quentin Boehler,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用临床级Navion电磁导航系统，首次实现了磁驱动倒立摆的摆动控制。该方法结合轨迹优化、时变LQR和迭代学习控制（ILC），在六次迭代内成功完成摆动任务。实验表明ILC能有效补偿电磁驱动中的不确定性，具有临床应用的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电磁导航系统（eMNS）研究多依赖准静态控制，动态控制虽已展现优势，但远离平衡态的轨迹跟踪问题仍未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 结合轨迹优化（考虑eMNS内部动力学）、时变线性二次型调节器（LQR）状态反馈和迭代学习控制（ILC），利用先前试验数据和系统动态模型逐步优化前馈指令。

Result: 单独使用LQR失败，但ILC在六次迭代内成功实现摆动控制。实验后分析表明，学得的ILC校正量与高保真磁场模型校准预测的扭矩偏差高度吻合。

Conclusion: 学习和自适应方法是应对电磁驱动中不确定性的有效工具，可推广至导管、导丝等磁驱动器械的临床场景。

Abstract: Electromagnetic Navigation Systems (eMNS) have gained considerable attention for minimally invasive surgery and targeted drug delivery. While most of the literature relies on quasi-static control of these systems, recent work has demonstrated the benefits of dynamic approaches. However, trajectory tracking far from equilibrium states remains largely unaddressed. We close this gap by demonstrating the first swing-up of a magnetically actuated inverted pendulum using the clinically-ready Navion eMNS. Although the inverted pendulum is not clinically relevant in itself, the proposed method utilizes torques and forces as control objectives, making it applicable to other magnetically actuated devices such as catheters and guidewires. Our approach combines trajectory optimization that accounts for internal eMNS dynamics with time-varying Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) state feedback and Iterative Learning Control (ILC), which leverages previous trial data and the system's dynamic model to progressively refine the feedforward command. While LQR alone fails due to the complex phenomena of magnetic actuation, ILC enables successful swing-up within six iterations. Furthermore, post-experimental analysis reveals that the learned ILC correction closely matches the torque discrepancy predicted by high-fidelity magnetic field model calibration, suggesting learning and adaptation as a promising tool to deal with uncertainties in electromagnetic actuation arising, e.g., from patient-specific physiological motion patterns and field model calibration inaccuracies.

</details>


### [11] [Occupancy-Grounded Room Segmentation for Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13727)
*Carlos Cueto Zumaya,Iacopo Catalano,Jorge Peña-Queralta,Wallace Moreira Bessa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于占用分解的 occupancy-grounded 3D场景图构建方法，将房间节点锚定在可追踪的自由空间区域上，赋予房间显式的多边形足迹，在Matterport3D数据集上的评估表明该方法比基于地点连通性的基线方法能恢复更多的房间实例，但精度较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图中的房间层构建方法依赖不同的空间基板（如地点聚类、墙面、分割输出），导致房间节点缺乏统一的几何评价标准。

Method: 提出 occupancy-grounded 管道，通过占用分解追踪自由空间区域，将房间节点锚定在具有显式多边形足迹的自由空间上。

Result: 在12个Matterport3D场景上评估，相比Hydra基线方法，该方法恢复了更多房间实例，但精度较低；两种方法在墙面精确的房间边界上均存在挑战。

Conclusion: 占用锚定方法能更好地恢复房间实例，但精确的房间边界仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs (3DSGs) for indoor robots organize geometric and semantic information across spatial scales, with a room layer that connects object-level perception to room-scale reasoning. Existing systems construct this layer from different spatial substrates (\eg{} place clusters, wall planes, or segmentation outputs), and as a result, room nodes are not evaluated on a common geometric criterion. We present an occupancy-grounded 3DSG pipeline in which room nodes are anchored to tracked free-space regions derived from occupancy decomposition, giving each room an explicit polygonal footprint. We evaluate the pipeline on 12 Matterport3D scenes by matching predicted room polygons to annotated room instances and compare against Hydra, a representative state-of-the-art place-connectivity baseline. The results show that occupancy-grounded anchoring recovers substantially more room instances than place-connectivity construction, at the cost of lower precision, and that wall-accurate room boundaries remain an open problem for both methods. Code is available at https://github.com/crcz25/OccuSG.

</details>


### [12] [Scalable Dynamic Tactile Sensing Enabled by Passive and Flexible Acoustic Waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13746)
*Guimin Long,Changhong Linghu,Chuanping Liu,Ke Xu,Xingjian Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深亚波长声波导的被动分布式触觉传感范式，利用弹性膜帽Helmholtz谐振器与弹簧增强微管组成的封闭网络，实现柔性弯曲下不变声传输。稀疏麦克风阵列（4个）即可完成64节点的高精度触觉定位（4mm分辨率，>99%准确率）与低频信号波形重建，并展示了从指尖阵列到触觉手套和大面积皮肤的多种应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉传感技术在扩展到大面积阵列时面临灵敏度、鲁棒性、柔顺性之间的权衡，且布线复杂度和成本较高，缺乏一种可扩展、柔性、低成本的大面积动态触觉传感方案。

Method: 采用深亚波长声波导的被动分布式范式：弹性膜帽Helmholtz谐振器通过弹簧增强微管互联形成封闭网络，在宏观弯曲下保持声传输不变性；稀疏嵌入麦克风（4个）结合快速连续小波变换和轻量神经网络实现实时定位与波形重建。

Result: 实现了4mm最高空间分辨率、>99%准确率（4麦克风64节点阵列）；5.5ms内完成推理；成功检测从单根头发接触、5mg颗粒撞击、动脉脉搏波到羽毛触摸和手指接触等多种刺激。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种可扩展、柔性、低成本的触觉传感新范式，适用于下一代人机界面。

Abstract: Artificial dynamic tactile sensing requires sensitivity, robustness, and compliance, yet existing technologies face trade-offs when scaling to large-area arrays, compounded by wiring complexity and cost. Here, we report a passive distributed paradigm using deep sub-wavelength acoustic waveguides that decouples performance from structural flexibility. Elastic-membrane-capped Helmholtz resonators interconnected by spring-reinforced microtubes form an enclosed network with invariant acoustic transmission under macroscopic bending. By sparsely embedding microphones, the system achieves real-time localization (4 mm highest spatial resolution; >99% accuracy in a 4 microphones 64-node sensing array) and waveform reconstruction of low-frequency signals (<100 Hz). Fast Continuous Wavelet Transform and a lightweight neural network enable inference within 5.5 ms. We demonstrate conformable prototypes-fingertip arrays, a tactile glove, and large-area skins-detecting stimuli from single-hair contact to 5-mg particle impacts, arterial pulse waves, feather touches, and finger contact. This establishes a scalable, flexible, low-cost paradigm for next-generation human-machine interfaces.

</details>


### [13] [$μ_0$: A Scalable 3D Interaction-Trace World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13769)
*Seungjae Lee,Yoonkyo Jung,Jusuk Lee,Jonghun Shin,Amir Hossein Shahidzadeh,Yao-Chih Lee,H. Jin Kim,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 μ₀，一种基于3D轨迹的可扩展世界模型。通过预测交互点的平滑3D轨迹而非像素或直接动作，结合TraceExtract系统自动从视频中提取3D监督，实现了与动作监督预训练的VLA模型竞争的性能，证明3D轨迹是跨本体操作的可扩展、可迁移表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型面临两难：像素级视频模型虽提供广泛视觉先验但计算成本高，直接动作模型依赖具体本体的动作标签难以扩展。需要一种既具备可扩展性又无需本体特定标签的紧凑中间表示。

Method: 提出μ₀世界模型，核心是预测物体、工具、手部及接触区域等显著交互点的平滑3D轨迹。配套TraceExtract系统通过选关键点、构建全局对齐轨迹、关联层次化语言描述自动提取3D监督。模型结合预训练视觉-语言骨干网络与模块化轨迹专家，轨迹查询通过B样条控制点表示并预测未来轨迹。

Result: μ₀在2D和3D轨迹预测上均优于基线方法（包括轨迹预测模型和token化VLM）。冻结的μ₀可搭配下游动作专家，形成的轨迹条件策略在性能上与使用动作监督预训练的VLA模型（如π₀）竞争。

Conclusion: 3D轨迹是一种可扩展、可迁移的跨本体操作表示，无需动作标签即可学习物理世界动力学，并有效支持下游机器人控制任务。

Abstract: World models that capture how actions induce physical change enable scalable robot learning without reliance on embodiment-specific action labels. Pixel-space video models provide broad visual priors but expend model capacity on dense appearance reconstruction, while direct action models require embodiment-specific labels that hinder scalability. We present $μ_0$, a scalable world model based on 3D traces. Rather than predicting dense pixels or directly modeling actions, $μ_0$ forecasts smooth 3D trajectories for salient interaction points such as objects, tools, hands, and contact regions, yielding a compact, embodiment-agnostic motion interface. To enable training from diverse video sources, our TraceExtract system automatically extracts 3D supervision by selecting keypoints, constructing globally aligned traces, and associating motion segments with hierarchical language captions. This TraceExtract supervision pretrains $μ_0$ by combining a pretrained vision-language backbone with a modular trace expert, which represents each query via B-spline control points and predicts future traces. Experiments show that $μ_0$ outperforms baselines in both 2D and 3D trace prediction, including trace prediction models and tokenized VLM methods. Because $μ_0$ is frozen and reusable, it can be paired with action experts for downstream robot embodiments. Despite action-free pretraining, the resulting trace-conditioned policies achieve performance competitive with VLA models pretrained with action supervision, such as $π_0$. These results establish 3D traces as a scalable and transferable representation for cross-embodiment manipulation.

</details>


### [14] [Semidefinite Relaxations for Collision-Free Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14063)
*Bernhard Paus Graesdal,Alexandre Amice,Pablo A. Parrilo,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次对无碰撞运动规划的半定松弛进行了理论分析。提出了一种基于多项式曲线的半定松弛方法，证明了该松弛等价于在高维空间中求解一个相关运动规划问题的全局最优解，并给出了松弛紧致性的充要条件。同时，该松弛具有对称性约简特性，使得半定锥尺寸与多项式次数呈线性关系，与环境维度无关。相比于SNOPT和IPOPT等非线性规划方法，求解速度快10-100倍，方差更低，并能可靠找到局部最优路径。在RRT规划器中作为凸转向函数用于最小加加速度四旋翼规划取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无碰撞运动规划本质上是非凸的，传统非线性规划方法求解困难且效率低。虽然半定松弛在机器人运动规划中被使用，但缺乏系统的理论分析，不清楚松弛何时紧、为何有效。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为半定松弛在无碰撞运动规划中的应用提供理论基础。

Method: 将无碰撞运动规划问题建模为多项式曲线上的非凸优化问题（点机器人通过球型障碍物，最小化平方导数代价），然后提出自然的半定松弛。通过理论分析揭示松弛的几何解释（等价于高维空间中的运动规划问题）和对称性约简特性（锥大小仅与多项式次数线性相关，与维度无关）。

Result: 半定松弛求解速度比SNOPT和IPOPT快10-100倍，求解时间方差显著更低，能可靠找到原始问题的局部最优路径。在RRT规划器中作为凸转向函数，成功实现了C^4连续的最小加加速度四旋翼轨迹规划。

Conclusion: 半定松弛为无碰撞运动规划提供了一种高效且理论上可解释的求解方案。其几何解释给出了松弛紧致性的充要条件和直觉理解，对称性约简使其计算复杂度不随环境维度增长。该方法在实际机器人规划任务中表现出色，可作为凸基元嵌入更复杂的规划框架中。

Abstract: We study semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. We focus on a point robot moving from start to goal through spherical obstacles in $\mathbb{R}^n$, subject to path continuity constraints and squared derivative costs; a setting that is conceptually simple yet captures the hardness of collision-free motion planning. We formulate this problem exactly as a nonconvex problem over polynomial curves, and present a natural semidefinite relaxation. We contribute two key theoretical insights; to our knowledge this is the first theoretical analysis of semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. First, we show that solving the convex relaxation is equivalent to solving, to global optimality, a related motion planning problem in a potentially higher-dimensional space. This geometric interpretation yields necessary and sufficient conditions for tightness, and a clear intuition for when the relaxation is loose. Second, we show that the relaxation admits a symmetry reduction that makes it significantly smaller than one might expect, with positive semidefinite cone sizes that scale linearly with the polynomial degree and are independent of the ambient dimension. The resulting relaxation is 10 to 100 times faster than direct nonlinear programming transcriptions solved with SNOPT and IPOPT, exhibits significantly lower variance in solve times, and reliably finds a locally optimal path for the original problem. We demonstrate its effectiveness as a convex steering function in an RRT planner for minimum-snap quadrotor planning with $C^4$ continuous trajectories.

</details>


### [15] [FlowMo-WM: A World Model with Object Momentum and Hidden Ambient Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13817)
*Yitao Jiang,Luyang Zhao,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FlowMo-WM，一种端到端可训练的视觉世界模型，通过将图像-动作历史分解为短期潜在状态（物体运动）和长期上下文（隐藏漂移），在无直接流场监督下提升水下车艇等惯性主导场景的长时程预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型多假设运动由即时控制主导，但水下/水面车辆等实际物体在惯性及隐藏环境漂移（水流、风）下持续运动，现有模型难以应对此类场景。

Method: 提出FlowMo-WM，端到端训练，将图像-动作历史分解为：1) 短期潜在状态（总结物体中心运动）；2) 长期上下文（总结缓慢变化的外源影响）。通过零上下文残差过渡分离动作条件基础动力学与上下文相关漂移效应，在潜在rollout中实现。

Result: 在含多种隐藏流、扰动和随机车辆动力学的模拟水面车辆环境中，FlowMo-WM在长时程rollout精度上优于代表性动作条件潜在世界模型。

Conclusion: 预测时上下文消融实验（置零或打乱上下文）表明环境上下文对隐藏漂移下的稳定预测至关重要；冻结线性探针表明学习到的因子编码了可解释信息。

Abstract: World models in robot learning predict future states from visual observations and actions, enabling agents to reason about the consequences of their controls. However, many action-conditioned models are evaluated in settings where motion is dominated by immediate control, whereas aquatic surface vehicles and other real-world objects continue moving under inertia and are displaced by hidden ambient drift, such as water currents or wind. We propose FlowMo-WM, an end-to-end trainable visual world model that infers object-centric motion state and a predictive long-history context associated with hidden drift from image-action histories without direct supervision of flow fields. FlowMo-WM factorizes image-action history into a short-history latent state, trained to summarize object-centric motion, and a longer-history context, trained to summarize slowly varying exogenous influences. A zero-context residual transition separates action-conditioned base dynamics from context-dependent drift effects during latent rollout. In simulated aquatic surface-vehicle environments with diverse hidden flows, disturbances, and randomized vehicle dynamics, FlowMo-WM improves long-horizon rollout accuracy over representative action-conditioned latent world models. Prediction-time context ablations, in which the inferred context is zeroed or shuffled during rollout, show that the ambient context is important for stable prediction under hidden drift, while frozen linear probes characterize information encoded in the learned factors.

</details>


### [16] [Robustness without Wrinkles: Parallel Simulation and Robust MPC for Certified Deformable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14188)
*Wei-Chen Li,Jeffrey Fang,Sasanka Polisetti,Yuexi Song,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CORD-SLS是一种基于GPU并行可微模拟器的实时安全变形体操作控制方法，通过输出反馈鲁棒MPC和共形预测校准实现绳索与布料的高速、安全操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在变形体操作中难以同时满足实时性、安全性和鲁棒性，尤其是面对间断接触、模型不确定性和感知噪声时缺乏有效的约束满足机制。

Method: 提出GPU并行可微模拟器（含接触平滑），支持基于梯度的快速规划；设计实时输出反馈鲁棒MPC算法，利用共形预测校准视觉反馈和感知误差边界，构建可达管以实现高概率安全控制；该模拟器还可加速基于模型的强化学习训练神经操作策略。

Result: 在绳索和布料的高维接触丰富操作任务中（避障、布线、折叠、展平等），CORD-SLS实现了毫秒级规划，在安全性、速度和任务成功率上超越基线方法。

Conclusion: GPU并行可微模拟器结合鲁棒MPC与共形预测校准，为变形体操作提供了兼具实时性与安全性的有效框架。

Abstract: We present CORD-SLS, a real-time control method for safe deformable object manipulation, with a focus on ropes and cloth. At its core is a GPU-parallel differentiable simulator with contact smoothing which enables efficient gradient-based planning through intermittent contact. To robustly satisfy constraints under model and sensing uncertainty, we develop a real-time, GPU-parallel output-feedback robust model predictive control (MPC) algorithm that plans with this simulator. We further show that the simulator accelerates model-based RL for training neural manipulation policies. To improve real-world robustness, we use conformal prediction to calibrate visual-feedback and perception-error bounds for MPC, producing reachable tubes that enable high-probability safe control. We evaluate CORD-SLS on high-dimensional, contact-rich rope and cloth manipulation tasks in simulation and hardware, including obstacle avoidance, routing, folding, and smoothing. Across settings, CORD-SLS achieves millisecond-speed planning, exceeding baselines in safety, speed, and task success.

</details>


### [17] [Multi-Agent Embodied Autonomous Driving: From V2X Information Exchange to Shared World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13840)
*Senkang Hu,Zhengru Fang,Yihang Tao,Zihan Fang,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了自动驾驶从单一车辆智能向多智能体具身系统（MAEAD）的转变，以共享世界模型（SWMs）为核心框架，回顾了380余篇涵盖V2X通信、协同感知、交互认知、协同规划、端到端协同驾驶以及闭环仿真验证的文献，指出当前研究集中在仿真与离线评估、缺乏可验证的实时安全保障，并提出了未来在可验证共享状态维护、意图与计划对齐、以及通信/延迟/部署约束下的安全协调行动等关键研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶正从孤立车辆智能向多智能体具身系统演进，需要车辆间共享感知、推断意图并在不确定性下协调行动，但目前缺乏对这一转变的系统性审视，尤其是以共享世界模型为统一视角的理论框架与实证综述。

Method: 以"共享世界模型"为组织主线，系统检索并分析了380余篇相关文献，覆盖V2X通信、协同感知、智能体间认知、协同规划、端到端协同驾驶以及闭环仿真与数据引擎六大子领域，归纳了从观测对齐到状态共享、意图感知交互、再到协调行动的技术路径。

Result: 现有研究主要集中在仿真环境、标准化基准和离线协议下进行评估；基于基础模型的协同方法在开放交通场景下缺乏可验证的实时安全保证；识别出可验证共享状态维护、鲁棒的意图与计划对齐、以及在通信/延迟/部署约束下的安全协调行动三大关键研究空白。

Conclusion: 提出了多智能体具身自动驾驶（MAEAD）的研究优先级：可验证的共享状态维护、鲁棒的意图与计划对齐、以及约束下的安全协调行动，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is shifting from isolated vehicle intelligence toward multi-agent embodied systems that share perception, infer intent, and coordinate action under uncertainty. This survey examines this transition through the lens of Shared World Models (SWMs): predictive cross-agent representations maintained across vehicles, infrastructure, and other traffic participants. We review more than 380 publications spanning vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication, collaborative perception, inter-agent cognition, cooperative planning, end-to-end cooperative driving, and simulation and data engines for closed-loop validation. The organizing question is how exchanged observations become aligned state, intent-aware interaction, and coordinated downstream action. Across the surveyed literature, evaluation remains concentrated in simulation, curated benchmarks, and offline protocols. Foundation-model-based coordination also lacks verified real-time safety guarantees in open traffic. These gaps motivate key research priorities for multi-agent embodied autonomous driving (MAEAD): verifiable shared-state maintenance, robust intent and plan alignment, and safe coordinated action under communication, latency, and deployment constraints.

</details>


### [18] [Short-Horizon Position Accuracy of Single-Track Models: Implications for Motion Planning of Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14216)
*Aron J. Aertssen,Lars A. T. H. van Alen,Igo J. M. Besselink,Rudolf G. M. Huisman,René M. J. G. van de Molengraft*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种单轨车辆模型在多种驾驶工况下的短时域位置精度，通过实测数据标定模型参数，旨在为模型预测控制中的模型选择提供权衡依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆模型在运动规划中的位置精度缺乏系统的实测验证，而位置精度直接影响轨迹可行性和安全性，因此需要基于真实测量数据对模型的位置精度进行量化评估。

Method: 通过专用实验对测试车辆进行参数标定，然后在多种驾驶工况下比较三种单轨车辆模型的短时域位置精度与实测数据。

Result: 未在摘要中给出具体数值结果，但指出目标是揭示模型复杂度、参数化质量与位置精度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 不存在单一最优模型，应根据MPC应用需求在模型复杂度、参数化质量和位置精度之间做出权衡选择。

Abstract: Accurate and computationally efficient vehicle models are essential for motion planning of autonomous vehicles, where positional accuracy directly affects trajectory feasibility and safety. However, the positional accuracy has not been systematically evaluated against real measurements. Therefore, this paper compares the short-horizon positional accuracy of three single-track vehicle models against vehicle measurements across various driving maneuvers. Model parameters are identified through dedicated experiments with the instrumented test vehicle. Rather than identifying a single best model, this work aims to provide insight into the trade-offs between model complexity, parameterization quality, and positional accuracy for informed model selection in Model Predictive Control applications.

</details>


### [19] [Efficient Domain-Adaptive Policy Learning via Kernel Representation with Application to Quadrotor Control under Non-Stationary Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13842)
*Hongyu Zhou,Mingtian Tan,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于核表示的高效域自适应策略学习算法，利用随机傅里叶特征对未知扰动进行可微核逼近，通过离线可微仿真训练策略（仅需50秒），并在部署时通过在线最小二乘估计实时更新核系数和带宽参数，实现对非平稳环境的快速自适应。在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务（Crazyflie硬件平台）上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域自适应策略学习面临环境表示的两难：既要足够表达能力强以建模复杂的sim-to-real差距，又要计算高效以支持部署时的快速在线自适应。现有方法难以同时满足这两个要求。

Method: 1) 使用基于随机傅里叶特征的可微核近似对未知扰动进行建模；2) 离线阶段随机采样核系数和带宽参数生成多样化扰动分布，通过可微仿真及解析梯度优化控制策略，训练仅需50秒（RTX 4090）；3) 部署阶段通过在线最小二乘估计实时更新核系数和带宽，实现策略对非平稳环境的实时自适应。

Result: 在高保真数值仿真以及Crazyflie硬件平台上，针对多种扰动（气动效应、风扰、地面效应、负载变化）的四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中，该方法实现了有效的域自适应控制。

Conclusion: 基于随机傅里叶特征的核表示方法能够同时满足离线训练的充分表达能力和在线自适应的高效计算需求，为域自适应策略学习提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for efficient domain-adaptive policy learning via kernel representations. Learning domain-adaptive policies is challenging since it requires an environment representation that is both sufficiently expressive to model complex sim-to-real gaps during offline training, and computationally efficient enough to support rapid online adaptation during deployment. For instance, a quadrotor may encounter time-varying, non-stationary disturbances, such as sudden gusts of wind, payload shifts, or transitions between distinct flight regimes with and without ground effects. To address these challenges, we model unknown disturbances using a differentiable kernel approximation based on random Fourier features. During the offline training phase, we randomly sample kernel coefficients and bandwidth parameters to generate a rich diversity of disturbance profiles. We then optimize the control policy via differentiable simulation with analytical gradients, a process that takes only 50 seconds of training time on an RTX 4090 GPU. During hardware deployment, the policy adapts to non-stationary environments in real time by updating both the kernel coefficients and bandwidth through online least-squares estimation. We evaluate our method on quadrotor trajectory tracking tasks across high-fidelity numerical simulations and hardware experiments using Crazyflie, subjected to various disturbances, including complex aerodynamic effects, wind, ground effects, and payload fluctuations.

</details>


### [20] [Output-Level Regularization Eliminates the Seed Lottery in Single-GPU VLA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13856)
*Jeffrin Sam,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-JEPA微调存在“种子彩票”问题：相同代码、数据和架构下，不同随机种子会导致性能从91-94%骤降至65.2%，且无任何警告。原因在于输出坍缩——动作预测器学到产生几乎相同的输出。权重级正则化（L2、EWC）对此无效，而输出级正则化（VICReg、Dropout、半学习率）可彻底消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示视觉-语言-动作模型微调中一个隐藏的严重问题：相同训练配置下随机种子差异会导致性能大幅下降且无任何预警信号，研究者对此问题缺乏认知和解决方案。

Method: 通过在LIBERO基准上对VLA-JEPA进行13次不同随机种子的微调实验，系统比较了7种正则化方法（包括权重级的L2、EWC和输出级的VICReg、Dropout、半学习率），利用雅可比零空间理论分析了输出坍缩的机制。

Result: 基线实验中13次运行有1次性能从91-94%坍缩至65.2%（29个百分点的差距）；输出级正则化方法（VICReg、Dropout、半学习率）在所有21次测试中均未出现坍缩（0/21），而权重级方法（L2、EWC）无法消除该问题。

Conclusion: 输出坍缩是导致“种子彩票”问题的根本原因，权重级正则化在结构上无法检测此问题，而输出级正则化方法能有效消除灾难性种子；最简单的解决方案是调整优化器配置中的学习率。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a vision-language-action model (VLA-JEPA) on a single GPU should be simple: load a pretrained checkpoint, run training, deploy. There is a hidden danger. Run the same fine-tuning code thirteen times -- same data, same architecture, different random seed -- and twelve runs produce a robot succeeding 91--94% of the time, while one run silently degrades to 65.2%: a 29 pp gap with no error message, no warning, and no way to predict which seed will fail. We call this the seed lottery. We trace the cause to output collapse: the action predictor quietly learns to produce nearly identical outputs regardless of what the robot sees. Existing weight-level methods (L2, EWC) are structurally blind to this collapse -- they penalize weight changes, but collapse occurs in directions weights can move freely without affecting outputs, a gap we formalize via the Jacobian null-space. Across 7 methods x up to 13 seeds x 3 LIBERO benchmarks, three output-level regularizers -- VICReg (n=12 seeds), Dropout (n=4), and a halved learning rate (n=5) -- each eliminate every catastrophic seed (0/21 combined collapses vs. 1/13 Baseline; F(12,11)=28.7, p<0.001), while weight-level methods (L2, EWC) preserve the lottery. The simplest fix is changing one number in your optimizer config.

</details>


### [21] [ForestBack: Breadcrumb-Based Pedestrian Dead Reckoning for Infrastructure-Free Return Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14421)
*Aueaphum Aueawatthanaphisut,Chanakan Chaipan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForestBack 是一种基于面包屑式行人航位推算（PDR）的无基础设施行人返回导航框架，通过加速度步态检测、自适应步长估计、磁力计辅助航向估计、气压高度校正和双向面包屑路径重建，在无 GPS/Wi-Fi/蓝牙等外部定位基础设施的 GPS 拒止环境中，将传统 PDR 的 RMSE 从 1.129m 降低至 0.965m（提升 15.76%），最终位置误差从 1.781m 降至 1.388m，转弯事件检测一致性达 99.90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 GPS 拒止环境中，可靠的行人返回导航仍是一个重要挑战。现有方法依赖 GPS、Wi-Fi、蓝牙信标或预装基础设施，在无外部定位基础设施的场景下难以实现可靠的原路返回导航。

Method: ForestBack 框架基于面包屑式行人航位推算（PDR），集成加速度步态检测、自适应步长估计、磁力计辅助航向估计、气压高度校正以及双向面包屑路径重建，记录用户行走路径为可逆面包屑节点序列，生成反向路径引导。

Result: 基于室内障碍物回避路线（5个检查点、36次步行试验、42,474个时间序列样本）的评估表明：与传统 PDR 相比，ForestBack 将平均 RMSE 从 1.129m 降至 0.965m（提升 15.76%），最终位置误差从 1.781m 降至 1.388m，转弯事件检测一致性达 99.90%。

Conclusion: ForestBack 在障碍物回避场景下显著改善了轨迹重建和路径保持的返回导航性能。所发布的数据集和分析笔记支持无基础设施 PDR 返回导航系统的可重复性评估和未来基准测试。

Abstract: Reliable return navigation remains an important challenge in GPS-denied environments where external positioning infrastructure may be unavailable or unreliable. This paper presents ForestBack, an infrastructure-free pedestrian return navigation framework based on breadcrumb-based pedestrian dead reckoning (PDR). The system records a user's walking route as a sequence of reversible breadcrumb nodes and generates reverse-path guidance without requiring GPS, Wi-Fi, Bluetooth beacons, or pre-installed infrastructure. ForestBack integrates acceleration-based step detection, adaptive step-length estimation, magnetometer-assisted heading estimation, barometric-altitude correction, and bidirectional breadcrumb path reconstruction. The system was evaluated using an indoor obstacle-avoidance route with five checkpoints, where the user navigated around a central obstacle. A dataset of 36 walking trials and 42,474 time-series samples was used for evaluation, including IMU signals, magnetometer readings, barometric variables, turn-event labels, ground-truth trajectories, baseline PDR outputs, proposed ForestBack outputs, and power-related measurements. Experimental results show that ForestBack reduced the mean RMSE from 1.129 m to 0.965 m compared with traditional PDR, corresponding to a 15.76% improvement. The mean final-position error was reduced from 1.781 m to 1.388 m, while turn-event detection consistency reached approximately 99.90%. These results indicate that ForestBack improves trajectory reconstruction and route-preserving return guidance in obstacle-avoidance scenarios. The released dataset and analysis notebook support reproducibility and future benchmarking of infrastructure-free PDR-based return navigation systems.

</details>


### [22] [ContactWorld: What Matters in Vision-Tactile World Models for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13877)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Pokuang Zhou,Kaidi Zhang,Adeesh Desai,Temitope Amosa,Davood Soleymanzadeh,Jiuzhou Lei,Minghui Zheng,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ContactWorld基准，系统研究了接触式操作中视觉-触觉世界模型的表示属性。发现空间结构化且时间连续的表示（如点云）能显著提升规划性能（成功率从约20%提升至32%），而触觉感知的有效性关键取决于跨模态表示兼容性。结合点云与触觉力场表示可进一步提升至36.1%，且触觉在长时域规划中尤为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要世界模型从多模态感官观测中推理复杂接触动力学，但目前尚不清楚何种表示属性能够从根本上支持接触场景下稳定的长时域规划。

Method: 构建ContactWorld基准，包含12项接触丰富操作任务（插入、拆卸、拧螺丝、探索性交互等），系统对比不同视觉-触觉表示（腕部视图、前视图、点云、触觉力场等）在规划任务中的性能表现。

Result: 空间结构化且时间连续的表示（点云）将平均规划成功率从20.7%（腕部视图）和22.0%（前视图）提升至32.1%；结合点云与触觉力场表示进一步提升至36.1%；触觉感知在长时域规划目标下重要性显著增加。

Conclusion: 表示结构、跨模态兼容性和长时域鲁棒性是视觉-触觉世界模型用于接触式机器人操作的关键属性，点云与触觉力场表示的组合是目前最强的方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires world models to reason over complex contact dynamics from multimodal sensory observations. However, it remains unclear which representation properties fundamentally support stable long-horizon planning in contact-rich settings. In this paper, we present ContactWorld, a benchmark and systematic empirical study of vision-tactile world models spanning 12 contact-rich manipulation tasks, including insertion, disassembly, screwing, and exploratory interaction. Across extensive experiments, we find that representations that are both spatially structured and temporally continuous consistently achieve the strongest planning performance. In particular, point-cloud observations improve average planning success rates from 20.7% with wrist-view observations and 22.0% with front-view observations to 32.1%. We further find that the effectiveness of tactile sensing depends critically on cross-modal representation compatibility rather than modality scaling alone. Combining point-cloud observations with tactile force-field representations, which preserve richer spatial structure and interaction dynamics, further improves performance to 36.1%, yielding the strongest overall planning performance across all evaluated tasks. Moreover, tactile sensing becomes increasingly important under long-horizon planning objectives, where compounding prediction errors and contact uncertainty accumulate over time. Together, these findings highlight the importance of representation structure, multimodal compatibility, and long-horizon robustness in vision-tactile world models for contact-rich robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [23] [Impedance MPC with Disturbance Estimation for Dexterous Hand Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14606)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种与执行器无关的阻抗模型预测控制（Impedance MPC）框架，用于灵巧手指的精确轨迹跟踪与安全柔顺接触控制。通过代数前馈将肌腱传动简化为常系数双积分器，结合在线QP优化（500 Hz）与编码器增强型卡尔曼扰动观测器，在液压手指实验和16自由度LEAP Hand仿真中均实现了远超经典阻抗控制的精度与抗扰性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手需要在精确轨迹跟踪与安全柔顺接触之间取得平衡，而固定增益控制器无法同时满足这两个相互矛盾的目标。

Method: 1) 基于恒定A_d偏移自由架构的阻抗MPC框架；2) 代数前馈将肌腱传动统一简化为常系数双积分器；3) 离线预计算QP代价逆矩阵，在线运行10步滚动时域QP（500 Hz），强制执行接触力、驱动限制和加加速度硬约束；4) 编码器增强型卡尔曼扰动状态估计器消除稳态误差。

Result: 液压手指实验：RMS 0.5 mrad，稳态误差0.1 mrad，1.5 Nm接触下峰值偏差6.6 mrad，分别比经典阻抗好183倍、1500倍和23倍。LEAP Hand仿真：2.5 N抓取载荷扰动下0.7 s内恢复。

Conclusion: 所提出的Impedance MPC框架在精度、刚度和抗扰能力上显著优于经典阻抗控制，且具有执行器无关性和架构可扩展性，适用于高动态灵巧操作场景。

Abstract: Dexterous hands must simultaneously track precise finger trajectories and maintain safe, compliant contact -- objectives in tension for any fixed-gain controller. We present an actuator-agnostic Impedance Model Predictive Control (Impedance MPC) framework for dexterous fingers, instantiating the constant-$A_d$ offset-free architecture established for physical human-robot interaction (pHRI); its stability, recursive-feasibility, and input-to-state-stability guarantees are inherited by preserving the architectural assumptions. An algebraic feedforward reduces the tendon transmission -- hydraulic, cable, pneumatic, twisted-string, or series-elastic -- to a constant-coefficient double integrator, so the QP cost inverse is precomputed offline and a 10-step receding-horizon quadratic program runs at 500\,Hz while enforcing hard constraints on contact force (ISO/TS 15066), actuation limits, and jerk. An encoder-only augmented-Kalman disturbance state drives steady-state error to zero under any constant contact load. On a hydraulically actuated finger -- the worked example platform, adding pressure and cavitation constraints -- the 500\,Hz Kalman MPC attains 0.5\,mrad RMS, 0.1\,mrad steady-state, and 6.6\,mrad peak deflection under 1.5\,Nm contact: 183$\times$, 1500$\times$, and 23$\times$ better than classical impedance. The realized first-move stiffness (18$\to$323\,Nm/rad with update rate) is independently verified. The architecture scales to a 16-DOF LEAP Hand MuJoCo simulation, recovering from 2.5\,N grasp-load disturbances within 0.7\,s.

</details>


### [24] [AnyGoal: Vision-Language Guided Multi-Agent Exploration for Training-Free Lifelong Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13878)
*MoniJesu James,Marcelino Julio Fernando,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyGoal是一种无需训练的多机器人导航架构，以视觉语言模型（VLM）为核心驱动基于前沿的探索，通过共享的2D高斯贝叶斯价值图（BVM）协调智能体，在GOAT-Bench未见分割集上实现52.4%的子任务成功率（Subtask SR）和12.7%的SPL，相比Modular GOAT提升27.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端导航策略在跨分布场景中性能急剧下降；模块化管道受限于封闭集目标检测的召回率；3D快照记忆系统积累稠密且视角相关的表示，维护成本高昂。

Method: 提出AnyGoal架构：1) VLM为核心进行前沿探索；2) 共享2D高斯贝叶斯价值图（BVM），通过深度锥掩码进行精度加权融合，跨子任务终身累积证据；3) 前沿排序结合VLM softmax与贝叶斯UCB项；4) 贪婪分配器结合空间分离惩罚与承诺滞后来分配智能体。

Result: 双智能体系统在GOAT-Bench未见分割集（360个episode，2,669个子任务）上达到52.4% Subtask SR和12.7% SPL；单智能体AnyGoal达41.9% Subtask SR；消融实验表明开放词汇检测器将主要失败模式从探索转向目标验证。

Conclusion: AnyGoal通过基于VLM和贝叶斯价值图的决策架构，在严格物理条件下实现了GOAT-Bench新的最优性能，且性能提升源于架构设计而非模型规模。

Abstract: End-to-end navigation policies trained on large simulation corpora degrade sharply when transferred to out-of-distribution scenes, categories, or goal modalities. Modular pipelines such as Modular GOAT are bottlenecked by closed-set object detection recall, while 3D snapshot-memory systems (e.g. 3D-Mem) accumulate dense, view-dependent representations that are heavy to maintain. We present AnyGoal, a training-free multi-robot architecture that places a Vision-Language Model (VLM) at the core of frontier-based exploration and coordinates agents through a shared 2D Gaussian Bayesian Value Map (BVM). The BVM maintains a per-pixel (mu, sigma^2) posterior over goal relevance, updated via precision-weighted fusion of VLM scores through a depth-cone mask, and is never reset between subtasks, yielding lifelong evidence accumulation. Frontiers are ranked by a convex blend of a VLM-as-judge softmax and a Bayesian UCB term on the BVM. A greedy allocator with spatial-separation penalty and commitment hysteresis distributes frontiers across agents without a centralized controller. On the full GOAT-Bench val unseen split (360 episodes, 2,669 subtasks), our dual-agent system achieves 52.4% Subtask SR at 12.7% SPL--state of the art under the strict physical regime (discrete 0.25 m steps, no teleportation, 42 deg HFOV) and a +27.5 pp improvement over Modular GOAT (24.9%). Single-agent AnyGoal achieves 41.9% Subtask SR, showing gains arise from the decision architecture. A four-way perception ablation shows that open-vocabulary detectors shift the dominant failure mode from exploration to goal verification.

</details>


### [25] [Whole-Body Impedance Model Predictive Control for Safe Physical Human--Robot Interaction on Floating-Base Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14617)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于浮动基座机器人的三层阻抗MPC架构：质心MPC规划接触力，优先级WBC实现平衡，残差零空间通过卡尔曼增强的QP预测和抑制人机交互扰动，实现零稳态误差和1kHz以上的实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浮动基座机器人的全身控制框架要么将整个关节空间分配给 locomotion，要么依赖固定增益阻抗反馈，在持续物理人机交互下会累积稳态误差。

Method: 三层架构：1) 质心MPC（500ms horizon规划接触力）；2) 优先级WBC层（通过接触一致零空间投影将平衡分解为关节力矩）；3) 残差零空间由滚动时域QP控制（使用卡尔曼增强状态预测和抑制pHRI扰动）。采用接触一致的反馈线性化将臂端执行器模型简化为双积分器，支持离线预计算QP成本，实现≥1kHz运行。协方差膨胀协议保证接触模式切换时扰动估计的连续性。

Result: 在17自由度双足机器人和Unitree G1人形机器人上进行了仿真验证。阻抗等价定理表明无限时域极限恢复经典任务空间阻抗律，有效质量、阻尼和刚度自适应于姿态和接触配置。

Conclusion: 所提方法在持续物理人机交互下实现零稳态误差，同时保持1kHz以上的实时运行能力，解决了固定增益阻抗反馈的局限性。

Abstract: Floating-base robots must balance under rigid contact constraints while interacting safely with humans. Existing whole-body control~(WBC) frameworks allocate the full joint space to locomotion or rely on fixed-gain impedance feedback that accumulates steady-state error under sustained physical human--robot interaction~(pHRI) forces. This paper extends the authors' fixed-base two-layer Impedance MPC to floating-base platforms through a three-level architecture: a centroidal MPC plans contact forces over a 500\,ms horizon; a priority-driven WBC layer resolves balance into joint torques through contact-consistent null-space projection; and the residual null space is governed by a receding-horizon quadratic program~(QP) that predicts and rejects pHRI disturbances using a Kalman-augmented state. A contact-consistent feedback linearization reduces the arm end-effector plant to a double integrator with a \emph{constant} state matrix within each contact mode, enabling offline precomputation of the QP cost and ${\geq}1$\,kHz operation. A covariance-inflation protocol preserves the disturbance estimate across contact-mode switches, guaranteeing zero steady-state error under bounded constant pHRI loads, and an Impedance Equivalence Theorem shows the infinite-horizon limit recovers a classical task-space impedance law whose effective mass, damping, and stiffness adapt to posture and contact configuration. Simulations on a 17-DOF biped and the Unitree G1 humanoid validate the design.

</details>


### [26] [Guided Diffusion with Distilled Vision-Language Reliability for Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13883)
*Ivan Valuev,Iana Zhura,Valerii Serpiva,Didar Seyidov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可靠性感知的扩散规划器，通过轻量级网络蒸馏视觉语言模型的开放词汇推理能力生成场景级可靠性热力图，并结合可微分的两阶段ESDF成本函数，使无人机在玻璃、镜子等不可靠区域也能安全导航，在仿真和实物实验中障碍物违反率从40.3%降至9.6%，平均可靠性从0.588提升至0.925。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机自主导航将感知、建图与规划分离，导致误差传播、延迟累积且需针对环境重新调参。端到端生成模型虽消除了接口，但在训练数据上无法识别不可靠观测（如玻璃、镜子、过曝光表面），将退化区域当作有效证据进行规划，存在严重故障模式。

Method: 1. 设计轻量级网络蒸馏视觉语言模型（VLM）的开放词汇推理能力，在实时规划预算内生成场景级可靠性热力图；2. 提出基于扩散模型的轨迹生成器，以观测与可靠性热力图共同作为条件；3. 引入可微分的两阶段ESDF成本函数，将深度传感器中的物理障碍与高不可靠区域的虚拟障碍同等对待，引导去噪过程；4. 无需针对新环境重新训练即可泛化。

Result: 在仿真与真实四旋翼飞行器实验中：障碍物违反率从40.3%降至9.6%；穿越区域的平均可靠性从0.588提升至0.925；消融实验显示去除可靠性项后平均可靠性从0.898降至0.783；蒸馏框架相比完整VLM运行速度提升达2倍。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的扩散规划器能有效解决端到端生成模型在不可靠观测下的规划失败问题，显著提升无人机导航安全性，且蒸馏策略保证了实时性，具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV navigation is conventionally solved by pipelines that separate perception, mapping, and planning into distinct stages, which propagates errors, accumulates latency, and requires environment-specific retuning. End-to-end generative models remove these interfaces by mapping raw observations directly to trajectories, but inherit a subtle failure mode: trained on clean data, they cannot recognise when an observation is unreliable, and treat degraded regions such as glass, mirrors, and overexposed surfaces as valid evidence for planning. We present a reliability-aware diffusion planner for 3D UAV navigation. It conditions trajectory generation on the observation together with a scene-level reliability heatmap that marks where perception cannot be trusted, produced by a lightweight network that distils the open-vocabulary reasoning of a vision-language model within the real-time planning budget. To generalise to unseen environments without retraining, we steer the denoising process with a differentiable two-stage ESDF cost that treats physical obstacles from depth and virtual obstacles from highly unreliable regions on equal footing. In simulation and on a real quadrotor, our planner produces markedly safer trajectories than a state-of-the-art diffusion baseline, reducing the obstacle-violation rate from 40.3% to 9.6% and raising the mean reliability of traversed regions from 0.588 to 0.925. Ablating the reliability term alone drops mean reliability from 0.898 to 0.783, confirming it as the decisive component, while distillation runs the framework up to 2 times faster than the full vision-language model.

</details>


### [27] [PhysVLA: Towards Physically-Grounded VLA for Embodied Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13886)
*Namai Chandra,Shriram Damodaran,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysVLA是一个无需重新训练的即插即用推理框架，通过双重物理校正（相位感知有限状态机+选择性欧拉-拉格朗日门）弥补VLA模型的物理规则缺失，在多种VLA骨干网络上实现高达17%的成功率提升和19%的稳定性提升，并成功迁移至物理机器人平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型仅拟合行为演示数据，缺乏对刚体动力学和接触约束等基本物理原理的显式建模，导致时间平滑策略虽能改善轨迹质量却引入短期记忆无法解决的额外失败。

Method: PhysVLA采用即插即用的推理时框架，拦截预测动作并应用双层校正：(i) 相位感知有限状态机，将任务结构化为接近、抓取、运输和放置四个离散段；(ii) 选择性欧拉-拉格朗日门，仅在动力学检测到不一致时激活，实现刚性体动力学约束。

Result: 在LIBERO-Spatial基准上对四种VLA骨干（OpenVLA、OpenVLA-OFT、Force-VLA、Generalist-VLA）评估，成功率绝对提升最高17%，稳定性提升最高19%，轨迹效率提升最高15%，轨迹急动度鲁棒性提升最高10倍。在真实Agilex Piper机械臂拾放任务中成功率提升最高50%。

Conclusion: 物理感知可作为可组合的、与骨干无关的运行时模块嵌入VLA模型，在不需重新训练、微调或权重访问的情况下显著提升机器人操作的物理一致性和任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at mapping visual inputs and natural language instructions directly to robotic control policies. However, because they are trained primarily to fit behavioural demonstration data, they do not explicitly enforce fundamental physical principles such as rigid-body dynamics or contact constraints. This exposes a critical physics gap: standard temporal smoothing applied on top of single-step or chunked VLAs trades trajectory quality for added failures that short-term memory cannot resolve. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysVLA (Physics-VLA), a plug-and-play, inference-time framework designed to wrap any frozen VLA backbone without retraining, fine-tuning, or weight access, with less than 1 ms of overhead per control step. PhysVLA intercepts the predicted control action, captures only the simulator or system state, and applies a dual-layered correction: (i) a phase-aware finite-state machine that structures discrete task segments (approach, grasp, transport, and place), and (ii) a selective Euler-Lagrange gate that activates only when a dynamics oracle detects kinodynamic inconsistency. Evaluated across OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, Force-VLA, and Generalist-VLA on LIBERO-Spatial with a 7-DoF Franka Panda, the framework delivers absolute success rate increases of up to 17% and stability increases of up to 19% with no per-task regressions, improves trajectory efficiency by up to 15% across all four backbones, and shows up to a 10x improvement in trajectory jerk robustness on a Robosuite Lift cross-simulator sweep. We further validate the framework on a real Agilex Piper arm with a pick-and-place task, confirming that PhysVLA transfers to physical hardware without retraining, with success-rate improvements of up to 50%, establishing physical awareness as a composable, backbone-agnostic runtime module.

</details>


### [28] [An Attention-based Model for Robust Forecasting with Missing Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13970)
*Zhitian Zhang,Wenjie Zi,Yunduz Rakhmangulova,Saghar Irandoust,Hossein Hajimirsadeghi,Thibaut Durand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CVAE和Transformer架构的多模态模型，能够在训练和推理阶段处理缺失模态，并在机器人轨迹预测和操作预测任务中优于现有融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人系统常因传感器数据不完整而面临模态缺失问题，现有注意力模型虽能处理多模态但大多假设所有模态在训练和推理时均可用，限制了机器人感知与决策的实用性。

Method: 将模型构建为条件变分自编码器（CVAE），结合基于Transformer的注意力机制，学习统一的固定维度表示，即使部分模态缺失也能训练，近似所有模态的鲁棒表示。

Result: 在人类轨迹预测和机器人操作预测两个任务的五个多模态数据集上，所提模型能从不完整数据中有效学习，性能优于以往多模态融合方法。

Conclusion: 所提模型在缺失模态场景下具有鲁棒性和有效性，拓展了多模态学习在机器人领域的实际应用范围。

Abstract: Learning with missing modalities is a fundamental challenge in multimodal robot learning, as real-world robotic systems often operate in environments with incomplete sensor data. Attention-based models are appealing for processing multimodal data because they can handle multiple modalities with a single backbone network. However, most multimodal models assume that all modalities are available during both training and inference, limiting their applicability in robotic perception and decision-making. In this paper, we introduce a multimodal model designed to handle missing modalities during both training and inference. The model is formulated as a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) and incorporates a transformer-based architecture that leverages attention mechanisms to learn a unified, fixed-dimensional representation, even when some modalities are missing. We show that our proposed model can be trained with missing modalities while approximating a robust representation of all modalities. We evaluate our approach on five multimodal datasets across two robot learning tasks: human trajectory prediction and robot manipulation forecasting. Experimental results demonstrate that our model effectively learns from incomplete data and is superior to prior multimodal fusion approaches.

</details>


### [29] [SplatlessDF: Continuous Distance Field Mapping with Non-Splatting Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13990)
*Monisha Mushtary Uttsha,Lan Wu,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SplatlessDF，一种基于高斯溅射的连续距离场建图框架，通过空间视角而非光度视角参数化各向异性高斯元素，实现距离和梯度的空间域查询，可独立或与2DGS联合用于机器人导航和表面建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯溅射方法主要用于高质量重建和渲染，但缺乏对空间距离场的连续建模能力，难以直接支持机器人导航等需要距离和梯度查询的下游任务。

Method: 基于高斯溅射原理，从空间视角参数化各向异性高斯元素，直接优化可微距离场；支持独立DF建模和与2DGS联合的DF-渲染统一框架。

Result: 独立公式能高效准确地查询距离和梯度；联合公式在改进渲染几何的同时建模连续距离场。

Conclusion: GS风格表示不仅适用于表面建模和渲染，还能为机器人导航提供合适的建图表示。

Abstract: Recent Gaussian splatting (GS) methods have shown that scenes can be represented efficiently with optimisable Gaussians for high-quality reconstruction and rendering. In this paper, building on this principle, we introduce SplatlessDF, a continuous distance field (DF) mapping framework that uses anisotropic Gaussian elements from a spatial rather than photometric perspective. SplatlessDF directly parameterises the Gaussians and optimises to recover a differentiable DF, enabling distances and gradients to be queried in the spatial domain for downstream robotic tasks such as navigation. Furthermore, SplatlessDF can be coupled with 2D Gaussian splatting (2DGS), providing a unified framework based solely on Gaussian primitives that can learn continuous DF and surface models and supports photometric rendering. We consider two settings: a standalone DF-only formulation and a joint DF-rendering formulation coupled with 2DGS. Experiments show that the standalone formulation provides efficient and accurate distance and gradient queries, while the joint formulation improves rendering geometry and simultaneously models a continuous DF. These results highlight the potential of GS-style representations not only for surface modelling and rendering but also for mapping representations suited to robotic navigation.

</details>


### [30] [From Attacks to Curricula: Learnability-Guided Adversarial Training for Safe Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14032)
*Yuewen Mei,Tong Nie,Jie Sun,Haotian Shi,Wei Ma,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AlignADV提出了一种可学习性引导的闭环对抗训练框架，通过偏好对齐和动态课程采样机制，将对抗场景转化为可解决且与策略能力匹配的训练课程，在Waymo数据集上降低了碰撞率并减少了40.6%的训练步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练框架是攻击导向的：碰撞驱动的生成器会合成无法解决的极端场景，损害学习效果；启发式采样器忽略策略能力演变，导致样本效率低和收敛延迟。

Method: 1) 将对抗场景生成重构为偏好对齐问题，使用直接偏好优化(DPO)引导生成器产生关键但可解决的场景；2) 引入行为指纹捕获策略演变特征，构建多模态能力预测模型估计策略性能；3) 结合可解决性对齐场景与能力预测，设计动态课程采样机制优先采样针对策略弱点的场景。

Result: 在Waymo开放运动数据集上，AlignADV将训练步数最多减少40.6%，同时降低了碰撞率并提高了正常和对抗交通条件下的路线完成率。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着从攻击导向的场景生成向可学习性引导的策略改进的转变，为更安全高效的自动驾驶训练提供了原则性方向。

Abstract: Closed-loop adversarial training improves autonomous driving safety by exposing policies to rare safety-critical scenarios. Standard pipelines first generate adversarial scenarios and then sample them for policy optimization. However, most existing frameworks remain attack-oriented: collision-driven generators often synthesize unsolvable extreme situations, which can degrade learning, while heuristic samplers ignore the evolving capability of the driving policy, causing sample inefficiency and delayed convergence. We propose AlignADV, a learnability-guided closed-loop adversarial training framework that converts adversarial scenarios into resolvable and capability-aligned curricula. First, we reformulate adversarial scenario generation as a preference alignment problem and employ direct preference optimization to guide the generator toward critical yet resolvable scenarios. Second, we introduce behavioral fingerprints to capture the intrinsic characteristics of the evolving policy and construct a multi-modal capability prediction model that estimates policy performance without expensive closed-loop simulations. By combining resolvability-aligned scenarios with capability predictions, AlignADV develops a dynamic curriculum sampling mechanism that prioritizes scenarios targeting the current policy's vulnerabilities. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate that AlignADV improves convergence efficiency and final performance, reducing training steps by up to 40.6 percent compared with baseline methods while lowering collision rate and improving route completion under both normal and adversarial traffic conditions. These results highlight a shift from attack-oriented scenario generation to learnability-guided policy improvement, offering a principled direction for safer and more efficient autonomous driving training. Project page: https://meiyuewen.github.io/AlignADV/.

</details>


### [31] [ReactSim-Bench: Benchmarking Reactive Behavior World Model Simulation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14058)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Yanlun Peng,Jianing Zhang,Xianda Guo,Zehan Huang,Haoran Liu,Qifeng Li,Shaofeng Zhang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ReactSim-Bench基准，用于评估自动驾驶行为世界模型仿真中的反应能力，通过解耦智能体与AV的控制，使用偏离日志的AV行为来测试智能体的反应，并系统评估了多种主流模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为仿真基准没有直接测量反应能力，通常让模拟器联合控制AV和周围智能体，通过日志相似度或开环预测指标评估真实性，无法衡量智能体对AV行为变化的可行响应。

Method: 解耦智能体与AV的控制，使用AV规划器生成候选行为并通过规则和人工验证筛选出偏离日志的行为作为AV输入；构建2,636个测试场景（三类）；采用碰撞指标、地图指标和运动学可行性指标评估反应的安全性和规则合规性。

Result: 系统评估了基于Transformer、扩散模型和下一token预测的多种SOTA模型，分析了重规划频率对性能的影响。

Conclusion: ReactSim-Bench有效弥补了现有基准在反应能力评估上的空白，实验结果为未来自动驾驶行为世界模型仿真研究提供了重要洞察。

Abstract: Reactive capability is a key property of data-driven behavior world model simulators for autonomous driving simulation systems. With this capability, simulated world agents can respond feasibly to autonomous vehicle (AV) behaviors that differ from the log. However, existing behavior simulation benchmarks do not directly measure reactive capability. They often let the simulator jointly control the AV and surrounding agents and evaluate realism through log similarity or open-loop prediction metrics. In this work, we introduce ReactSim-Bench for evaluating the reactive capability of behavior world model simulation in autonomous driving. We decouple the control of agents and the AV, using AV behaviors that differ from the log and require agents to respond as independent AV inputs. To obtain these AV behaviors, we construct a pipeline that uses an AV planner model to generate candidate behaviors and filters the data using rules and manual verification. Collision metrics, map-based metrics, and kinematic feasibility metrics are used to evaluate the safety and rule compliance of reactive responses. We construct 2,636 test scenarios with three categories and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art models across multiple architectures, including Transformer-based, diffusion-based, and next-token-prediction-based models. We further analyze how replan frequency affects performance and provide insights for future studies.

</details>

Abstract: This paper proposes, an improved version of the 3 in sewer pipe inspection robot equipped with an emergency evacuation mechanism. The coupled propulsion units feature a differential mechanism capable of posture changes via a single wire, enabling adaptation to pipe diameter variations. To traverse obstacles like pipe joints, a control method was devised that detects obstacle contact through current load on the drive wheel motors and slackens the wire. This method was verified through simulated pipe experiments. Load comparisons were made using current waveforms applied to the drive wheels. Our proposed control method significantly improved the step-over capability of the new articulated robots.

Conclusion: N2D Haptic Glove及方向性指尖触觉设备在接触密集型遥操作、沉浸式虚拟现实仿真和机器人示教学习等领域具有广阔应用前景，且硬件软件将完全开源。

Abstract: Humans rely on directional fingertip forces to probe and regulate contact during manipulation, yet most wearable haptic gloves render only vibration or single-axis force, leaving force direction ambiguous. Without directional cues, users must infer contact force from vision alone, often leading to over-pressing, inconsistent control, and reduced precision in robotic teleoperation. We present the N2D Haptic Glove, a multi-finger wearable device that renders planar flexion-extension fingertip forces using capstan-drive transmissions for high-transparency force feedback. Through benchtop validations and a user study involving haptic teleoperation of a robotic arm and hand, we demonstrate that compared to visual-only and single-axis haptic baselines, planar fingertip feedback significantly reduces contact force error during precise manipulation, improves trial-to-trial consistency, and enhances overall user experience in axial probing tasks. These findings establish the N2D Haptic Glove and directional finger-based haptics devices as a promising modality for contact-rich teleoperation, immersive virtual reality simulations, and robot learning from demonstrations. N2D Haptic Glove's hardware and software system will be fully open-sourced at \href{https://ucsdarclab.github.io/n2d-glove/}{this https URL}.

</details>


### [34] [Self-Improving VLA Policies: Selected Diffusion Noise for Spurious-Robust Action Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14084)
*Duc Minh Nguyen,Bao-Ngoc Dao,Tung M. Luu,Binh Gia Nguyen,Vinh Tong,Anji Liu,Vu N. Duong,Dung D. Le,Daniel Sonntag,Trung Le,Ngan Le,Jan Peter,An Thai Le,Minh Nhat Vu,Mathias Niepert,Khoa D. Doan,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Vien Anh Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、仅在测试时使用的扩散噪声选择方法（SDN），通过最大化噪声向量与参考集的分离度来提升VLA策略的鲁棒性和成功率，在仿真和真实世界任务中分别提升了+8%和+10%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽然泛化能力强，但对虚假视觉关联和噪声动作生成敏感，导致在扰动下行为脆弱。作者希望在不修改模型参数的前提下，利用扩散噪声空间作为可控自由度来提升策略的鲁棒性和成功率。

Method: SDN（Selected Diffusion Noise）是一种无需训练、测试时的方法。它动态采样与参考集最大分离的噪声向量，以减少对虚假线索的依赖，同时选择能产生更连贯动作轨迹的候选噪声。该双目标优化旨在物体遮挡等情况下保持稳定行为并减少动作抖动。

Result: 在Google Robot、Widow-X两个仿真基准以及两个真实世界数据集上，对pi_0、Groot-N1.5、Groot-N1.6等多个VLA策略进行评估。SDN在仿真环境中一致提升了+8%的成功率，在真实环境中提升了+10%，同时产生了更平滑、更稳定的动作。

Conclusion: 扩散噪声选择可以作为测试时增强VLA策略的有效且通用机制，无需修改模型参数即可显著提升鲁棒性和成功率。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies enable strong generalization in robotic manipulation, but remain sensitive to spurious visual correlations and noisy action generation, leading to brittle behavior under perturbations. We introduce Selected Diffusion Noise (SDN), a simple, training-free test-time method that improves both robustness and success rate by leveraging the diffusion noise space as a controllable degree of freedom. SDN dynamically samples noise vectors that are maximally separated from a reference set to mitigate reliance on spurious cues, while selecting candidates that yield more coherent action trajectories. This dual objective encourages stable behavior even under object-masked observations and reduces action jitter without modifying model parameters. We evaluate SDN on two simulation benchmarks (Google Robot, Widow-X) and two real-world robotic datasets across multiple VLA policies, including pi_0, Groot-N1.5, and Groot-N1.6. SDN consistently improves success rates by +8% in simulation and +10% in real-world settings, while producing smoother and more stable actions. Our results highlight that diffusion noise selection can serve as an effective and general mechanism for enhancing VLA policies at test time.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化双臂苹果采摘机器人，采用垂直堆叠双臂结构实现单棵树上下区域同时作业。系统集成了大模型感知、安全轨迹规划、线性扫描采摘策略、时序逻辑双臂协调等5项创新。在1738次田间试验中，单次成功率达80.0%，平均单臂周期7.53秒，91.2%的采摘果实保持最高USDA等级，为商业化苹果采摘提供了有前景的方案。
