To this end, we propose On-Policy Co-Distillation (OPCoD), where each student's self-distillation is conditioned on its own correct rollout and feedback from its peer. To make feedback exchange effective, OPCoD uses cognizance-based gating to decide when to give feedback and feedback anchoring to ground feedback in the problem. On Science Q\&A tasks, OPCoD consistently outperforms baselines and achieves Pareto improvement across all evaluated domain pairs and students.

</details>


### [147] [Discovery under Hypothesis Redundancy: A Geometric Theory of Discovery Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14386)
*Li Xia,Baoxun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出搜索压缩假说，指出非局部探索仅在满足谱压缩、正交逃逸和残差信号对齐三个几何条件时才能提升科学发现效率，并通过合成环境、A股因子发现和符号回归基准验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学发现系统面临假说空间饱和问题——新假说不再提供独立信息。亟需理解何时以及为何混合发现系统（结合结构化局部搜索与LLM生成的非局部提议）能有效突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 形式化定义了搜索压缩假说的三个几何条件（谱压缩、正交逃逸、残差信号对齐），推导混合优势的必要条件，并通过受控合成环境、大规模A股因子发现、符号回归基准及公共表格数据操作合理性检验进行实验验证。

Result: 信号植入和有向vs随机实验表明，仅靠新颖性不足：随机正交跳跃扩展覆盖但不提高产出。混合增益集中在弱表示但目标承载的方向上，当假说空间接近满秩时增益消失。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM引导的发现从通用新颖性搜索转化为诊断程序，用于判断何时应进行有向非局部探索。

Abstract: Scientific discovery saturates when new hypotheses cease to provide independent information, even if the nominal hypothesis space remains large. We study hybrid discovery systems that combine structured local search with LLM-generated non-local proposals and pose the Search Compression Hypothesis: non-local exploration helps only when three geometric conditions co-occur: spectral compression, orthogonal escape from the explored span, and residual signal alignment with the target. We formalize these conditions, derive necessary conditions for hybrid advantage, and test the mechanism in controlled synthetic environments, large-scale A-share factor discovery, and symbolic-regression benchmarks; a public tabular operational sanity check tests the associated budget-allocation implication. Signal-planting and directed-versus-random experiments show that novelty alone is insufficient: random orthogonal jumps expand coverage but do not improve yield without predictive alignment. Across compression sweeps, real factor archives, and LLM-SRBench tasks, hybrid gains concentrate in weakly represented but target-bearing directions and vanish as the hypothesis space approaches full rank. The framework turns LLM-guided discovery from generic novelty search into a diagnostic procedure for deciding when directed non-local exploration is warranted.

</details>


### [148] [A Low-Rank Subspace Analysis of LLM Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14388)
*Angira Sharma,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Philip Torr,Anisoara Calinescu,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个诊断框架，用于分析LLM中行为（如拒绝、谄媚）之间的相互作用。研究发现不同行为共享内部表征，干预一个行为会以不对称方式影响其他行为，并通过行为子空间重叠度及与决策子空间的夹角两个几何量来解释这种传播效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对LLM特定行为（如拒绝或谄媚）的干预常常导致其他行为的意外改变，缺乏精准控制的能力，这使得设计可靠的安全控制变得困难。作者希望理解这种副作用的内在机制。

Method: 将行为建模为激活空间中的低秩子空间，通过测量行为子空间之间的重叠度（主角度平均余弦平方）以及每个行为子空间与决策子空间的夹角，来量化干预在不同行为之间的传播效应。在多个指令微调模型（7B-70B）上，针对拒绝、越狱和谄媚等场景进行实验。

Result: 不同行为共享内部表征；干预一个行为会以不对称方式改变其他行为；某些行为作为上游控制点，其干预广泛传播，而另一些行为则更为孤立。干预效果大小与子空间重叠度正相关，且与源行为子空间到决策子空间的距离（角度越小，传播越大）相关。

Conclusion: 行为之间难以独立修改，干预会通过共享表征和不对称交互传播，这对精准行为控制构成挑战。

Abstract: Interventions designed to modify a particular behavior in LLMs, such as refusal or sycophancy, often produce unintended changes in other behaviors. This lack of targeted control makes it difficult to design and implement reliable safety controls. To understand these side-effects, we introduce a diagnostic framework for analyzing interacting behaviors in LLMs. We model behaviors as low-rank subspaces in activation space, and study how interventions influence across behaviors. Across multiple instruction-tuned models (7B-70B) and across refusal, jailbreak, and sycophancy settings, we find that different behaviors share internal representations, and intervening on one behavior alters others in asymmetric ways. Some behaviors act as upstream control points whose interventions propagate broadly across other behaviors, while others remain more isolated. We relate these effects to two geometric quantities: (i) the overlap between behavior subspaces, measured as the average squared cosine of principal angles, and (ii) the angle between each behavior subspace and the decision subspace (capturing the model's final decision e.g., refuse vs. comply). Empirically, intervention effects on other behaviors tend to be larger for behavior pairs with higher subspace overlap, and for source behaviors whose subspaces lie closer (smaller angle) to the decision subspace. These findings highlight a challenge for targeted behavior control: behaviors are difficult to modify independently, as interventions can propagate through shared representations and asymmetric interactions.

</details>


### [149] [Running the Gauntlet: Re-evaluating the Capabilities of Agents Beyond Familiar Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14397)
*Mykola Vysotskyi,Runqi Lin,Grzegorz Biziel,Michal Zakrzewski,Sebastian Montagna,Damian Rynczak,Shreyansh Padarha,Kumail Alhamoud,Zihao Fu,William Lugoloobi,Kai Rawal,Hanna Yershova,Xander Davies,Taras Rumezhak,Guohao Li,Fazl Barez,Baoyuan Wu,Arkadiusz Drohomirecki,Yarin Gal,Chris Russell,Christopher Summerfield,Adam Mahdi,Volodymyr Karpiv,Philip Torr,Adel Bibi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GauntletBench是一个基于Web的基准测试，用于评估智能体系统在挑战性场景中的泛化能力，聚焦时间感知、图形理解和3D推理三种未被充分探索的能力，涵盖5个专业应用共100个视觉密集型任务。实验表明，最先进智能体仅达到19.1%的成功率，而人类非专家可超过80%，揭示了当前智能体与真实复杂场景需求之间的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常基于简单应用任务，仅关注少数能力维度，导致性能饱和，无法探测现代智能体的局限性。需要构建更具挑战性和广度的评估基准。

Method: 构建了一个模块化流水线，包括与开源和闭源智能体框架兼容的环境、受控的Web应用、结构化任务套件以及具有多样化指标的自动评估引擎。包含5个专业应用（视频编辑器、工作流构建器、3D建模器、飞行分析器和电路设计器）共100个视觉密集型任务，聚焦时间感知、图形理解和3D推理三种能力。

Result: 最先进智能体仅达到19.1%的成功率；非专家人类标注者成功率超过80%。

Conclusion: 前沿智能体系统远未达到人类水平性能，在未充分探索的能力和泛化方面存在显著局限，揭示了当前智能体与复杂真实场景需求之间的巨大差距。

Abstract: As agentic systems continue to evolve and are widely deployed in real-world scenarios, there is a growing demand to faithfully evaluate their capabilities. However, current benchmarks are typically built on popular applications with relatively simple tasks and focus on a narrow set of capabilities while overlooking broader dimensions, resulting in saturated performance on modern agents and failing to probe their limitations. To this end, we introduce GauntletBench, a web-based benchmark for evaluating agent generalisation in challenging scenarios, focusing on three underexplored capabilities (temporal perception, graphical understanding, and 3D reasoning), across five less-covered professional applications (Video Editor, Workflow Builder, 3D Modeller, Flight Analyser, and Circuit Designer), each with 20 vision-intensive tasks (100 in total). Our benchmark provides a modular pipeline that comprises an environment compatible with both open- and closed-source agent frameworks, a controlled web-based application, a well-structured task suite, and an automated evaluation engine with diverse metrics. Contrary to widespread expectations, our empirical results reveal that frontier agentic systems remain far from achieving human-level performance. Even the state-of-the-art agent achieves only a 19.1% success rate on our GauntletBench, highlighting the limitations in these overlooked capabilities and generalisation. By comparison, non-expert human annotators achieve over 80% success on our challenging yet feasible tasks, revealing the substantial gap between current agent capabilities and those required for complex real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [150] [A theoretical model for task routing in mixture-of-expert transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14398)
*Yongli Xiang,Vinoth Nandakumar,Yunzhi Yao,Peike Li,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过离散语言模型（句法模板+键值词典）证明了单层MoE Transformer可以形成任务-专家特化，专家规模仅取决于任务内在复杂度，为局部知识回路提供了理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论工作使用连续混合模型分析MoE，无法有效建模自然语言。本文旨在用离散语言模型从理论上解释MoE Transformer中的任务-专家特化现象。

Method: 将结构化知识表示为句法模板和有限键值词典，构造性地证明单层MoE Transformer可以通过专家特化编码知识，查询被路由到特定任务的唯一专家。

Result: 专家规模仅取决于任务内在复杂度（句法模板和事实词典的组合大小）；为MoE模型中局部知识回路的经验发现提供了理论支撑；通过不同MoE损失函数的实验验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 首次用离散模型证明了MoE中任务-专家特化的理论可行性，桥接了经验发现与理论分析之间的差距。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) layers enable the scaling of transformer models while keeping the inference compute fixed. While task-expert specialization has been observed in empirical studies of frontier MoE transformer models, existing theoretical work analyzes this using continuous mixture models that cannot be used to model natural language effectively. An important open question is to \textit{theoretically explain task-expert specialization in transformer MoE models using discrete models of language}. To address this, we represent structured knowledge via syntactic templates and finite key-value dictionaries, and prove formally that a single-layer MoE transformer can encode knowledge by using experts that specialize in the corresponding tasks. Our construction shows how queries are routed to unique, task-specific experts whose size depends solely on the intrinsic complexity of the given task (i.e. the combined size of its syntactic templates and factual dictionary). Our construction provides a theoretical support for empirical results on localized knowledge circuits in MoE models. We support our theoretical findings with experiments evaluating model performance under varying MoE loss functions.

</details>


### [151] [Federated Learning for Feature Generalization with Convex Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14416)
*Dongwon Kim,Donghee Kim,Sung Kuk Shyn,Kwangsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCONST通过线性凸约束自适应调节更新幅度，利用梯度信噪比分析增强特征可迁移性，在异构联邦学习中实现了最优泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端数据异构导致全局模型泛化能力差，局部模型易过拟合本地数据分布，且可迁移特征在聚合过程中被扭曲。

Method: 提出FedCONST方法，基于全局模型参数强度自适应调节更新幅度，使用线性凸约束保证训练稳定性并保留本地学习的泛化能力，通过梯度信噪比（GSNR）分析验证特征可迁移性和鲁棒性。

Result: 对齐了局部与全局目标，缓解了过拟合，在多样化联邦学习环境中取得了最先进的泛化性能。

Conclusion: FedCONST有效提升了联邦学习在异构数据下的泛化能力，达到了当前最优水平。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) often struggles with generalization due to heterogeneous client data. Local models are prone to overfitting their local data distributions, and even transferable features can be distorted during aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose FedCONST, an approach that adaptively modulates update magnitudes based on the parameter strength of the global model. This prevents over-emphasizing well-learned parameters while reinforcing underdeveloped ones. Specifically, FedCONST employs linear convex constraints to ensure training stability and preserve locally learned generalization capabilities during aggregation. A Gradient Signal to Noise Ratio (GSNR) analysis further validates the effectiveness of FedCONST in enhancing feature transferability and robustness. As a result, FedCONST effectively aligns local and global objectives, mitigating overfitting and promoting stronger generalization across diverse FL environments, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [152] [EM-NeSy: Expectation Maximization for Neurosymbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14463)
*Annegret Seibt,Luc De Raedt,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EM-NeSy将概率神经符号学习建模为期望最大化算法实例，通过概率推断计算符号后验分布，仅对神经组件进行梯度更新，实现了对近似推理的自然扩展和计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号模型要求符号组件可微分，这复杂化了近似推理的使用。作者希望提出一种框架，既保留神经符号学习的优势，又能自然地支持近似推理，无需对符号组件进行额外的可微性改造。

Method: 将概率神经符号学习形式化为EM算法：E步通过概率推断计算神经预测符号关于标签的后验分布；M步基于该后验仅对神经组件进行梯度下降更新。在精确推理下可恢复标准端到端梯度方法。

Result: 实验结果表明EM-NeSy具有良好的可扩展性和计算效率。

Conclusion: EM-NeSy框架解锁了EM算法在神经符号学习中的全部潜力，无需额外修改或可微性要求即可自然扩展到近似推理，同时保持与现有端到端方法的一致性。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic (NeSy) models integrate neural networks and symbolic reasoning for robust and interpretable AI. State-of-the-art NeSy models require that the symbolic component is expressed in a differentiable way, often complicating the use of approximate inference. We propose EM-NeSy which casts probabilistic NeSy learning as an instance of the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm. In the expectation step, we compute the posterior over the neurally predicted symbols conditioned on the label via probabilistic inference. In the maximization step, we update the neural parameters based on this posterior using gradient descent only through the neural component. This formulation unlocks the full potential of the EM algorithm for NeSy learning. It allows NeSy to extend naturally to approximate reasoning without any additional modifications or differentiability requirements of the symbolic component. Furthermore, it recovers the standard end-to-end gradient-based NeSy setting under exact inference. Our experimental results demonstrate the scalability and computational efficiency of EM-NeSy.

</details>


### [153] [Recipe-Controlled Decoder Audit for Structural Knowledge-Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14492)
*Xihang Shan,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种**配方控制的解码器审计方法（RCDA）**，用于结构化归纳知识图谱补全。通过在相同训练配方下交换解码器（ComplEx vs DistMult为主），评估解码器对性能的真实贡献。实验表明，在标准KG上解码器差异较小（MRR +0.005~0.012），而在小KG上解码器效应成为主要诊断指标。结论建议：在做出编码器层面的声明前，应先报告配对齐的解码器行、记录小KG来源、并扫描解码器×深度组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGC研究常将性能提升归因于编码器或训练配方的改进，但忽略了**解码器选择**的混淆效应。本文旨在通过受控实验分离解码器的影响，避免误导性归因。

Method: 提出RCDA协议：在同一训练配方下，固定编码器、仅交换解码器（ComplEx与DistMult作为主控对，辅以RotatE/TransE抽查），在7个基准数据集上比较MRR差异，并特别关注小KG的种子可变性和来源可变性。

Result: 1) 标准KG上解码器差异小且一致（+0.005~0.012 MRR），编码器效果变化更大；2) 小KG上解码器效应主导：Kinship中ComplEx稳定优势+0.143 MRR，UMLS结果依赖实验来源；3) 解码器与编码器深度存在交互；4) 在YAGO3-10上ComplEx (L=0, d=128) 达到0.6971 MRR。

Conclusion: 解码器选择在小KG和特定配置下对KGC性能有显著影响，且结果对配方和来源敏感。建议采用标准化的审计协议：报告匹配的解码器行、记录小KG来源、并在声称编码器贡献前扫描解码器×深度空间。

Abstract: We present a recipe-controlled decoder audit (RCDA) for structural transductive knowledge-graph completion (KGC). The audit asks a simple reporting question: before attributing gains to an encoder or training recipe, what changes when the decoder is swapped under the same recipe? Using ComplEx and DistMult as the primary controlled pair, with targeted RotatE/TransE spot-checks, we evaluate seven benchmarks. On five standard KGs, ComplEx-vs-DistMult differences are modest but consistent under our recipe (+0.005 to +0.012 MRR), whereas CompGCN-style encoder effects vary more by dataset. On small KGs, decoder effects become the main diagnostic: Kinship shows a stable ComplEx advantage of +0.143 MRR (6 seeds), while UMLS favours ComplEx by +0.022 MRR in a clean 6-seed server rerun but reverses in an earlier provenance variant. We therefore treat small-KG decoder choice as recipe- and provenance-sensitive rather than as a fixed dataset winner. We further show that decoder choice interacts with encoder depth on WN18RR, and that under our recipe L=0 ComplEx on YAGO3-10 reaches 0.6971 +/- 0.0048 MRR at d=128. The result is a compact audit protocol: report matched decoder rows, log small-KG provenance, and sweep decoder x depth before making encoder-level claims.

</details>


### [154] [PepALD: Macrocyclic Peptide Generation via Autoregressive Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14510)
*Junming Zhang,Siyu Yi,Wei Ju,Zhonghui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PepALD是一个自回归潜扩散基础模型，用于从头设计大环肽，通过结构化化学嵌入、上下文条件扩散、R基团感知环闭合和偏好优化实现高质量生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大环肽是极具前景的胞内靶点治疗候选分子，但设计时需同时控制非天然单体化学、环拓扑、膜渗透性和靶标结合。现有基于SMILES或HELM字符串的生成模型存在原子级序列空间过长或单体化学基础不足的问题。

Method: 提出PepALD模型，包含四个关键技术：(1) 使用结构化化学嵌入表示HELM单体；(2) 在化学信息潜空间中进行上下文条件扩散生成每个残基；(3) 自回归生成过程中预测R基团感知的环闭合；(4) 采用胜者保护扩散适配偏好优化（WDPO）将去噪器与亲和力奖励对齐。

Result: 在多个代表性肽生成基线上的实验表明，PepALD在生成质量和奖励优化性能方面均表现出优势。

Conclusion: PepALD通过将潜扩散与自回归生成相结合，有效解决了大环肽设计中化学基础不足和序列空间过长的问题，为从头设计大环肽提供了新范式。

Abstract: Macrocyclic peptides are promising therapeutic candidates for intracellular targets, but their design requires simultaneous control over non-natural monomer chemistry, ring topology, membrane permeability, and target binding. Existing SMILES- or HELM-string generative models either operate in long atom-level sequence spaces or treat monomers as symbolic tokens with limited chemical grounding. We introduce PepALD, an Autoregressive Latent Diffusion (ALD) foundation model for \textit{de novo} macrocyclic peptide generation. The model represents HELM monomers with structured chemical embeddings, generates each residue through context-conditioned diffusion in chemically informed latent space, predicts R-group-aware ring closures during autoregressive generation, and aligns the denoiser to affinity rewards using winner-protected diffusion-adapted preference optimization. In silico experiments demonstrate PepALD's generation quality and reward-optimization performance against representative peptide generation baselines.

</details>


### [155] [Behavioral Audit of Machine Unlearning Has a Privacy Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14518)
*Liou Tang,James Joshi,Ashish Kundu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度证明，在互不信任的场景下，仅依赖于查询模型行为信号的通用审计方案无法在不泄露保留集成员信息的前提下识别未充分遗忘的模型，揭示了机器学习遗忘审计中固有的隐私-审计权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习遗忘（MU）审计工作未考虑模型所有者和审计者之间互不信任的场景，且已有研究显示不诚实的模型所有者可以伪造证据规避MU执行，而好奇的审计者可以推断模型和训练数据的隐私敏感属性。

Method: 采用信息论证明方法，针对凸机器学习模型，证明仅依赖行为信号的通用审计方案存在隐私-审计权衡；并在凸模型上进行实证验证，同时在非凸模型上进一步实验。

Result: 理论证明在互不信任场景下，行为审计无法在不泄露保留集成员信息的情况下检测未充分遗忘的模型；凸模型上的实证结果有力支持了这一结论，非凸模型上也观察到类似的隐私-审计紧张关系。

Conclusion: 研究呼吁在设计隐私保护的MU审计方案时更加审慎地考虑隐私-审计权衡，为更严格地审视MU管线的隐私保护审计方案设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: The removal of learned data from Machine Learning models through Machine Unlearning (MU) has been widely studied; however, there has yet to be an agreed-upon scheme for auditing MU. Existing work has shown that a dishonest model owner can falsify evidence to avoid executing MU, while curious auditors (and adversaries) can infer the privacy-sensitive properties of the model and its training data even with limited access. Yet auditing of MU under mutual distrust between the model owner and the auditor remains unexplored. We provide an information-theoretic proof for this scenario: for convex ML models, a generic audit scheme that relies solely on querying the model for \textit{behavioral} signals cannot identify insufficiently unlearned models without revealing membership information of the retained set. Therefore, auditing MU under the assumption of a dishonest model owner and an honest-but-curious auditor faces an inherent privacy-audit tradeoff. Our empirical results on convex models strongly supports this result, while further experiments demonstrate that this privacy-audit tension persists in non-convex models. Our results call for a more careful consideration of the privacy-audit tension under a realistic auditor threat model, and serve as a foundation for more scrutiny of designs of privacy-preserving audit schemes for the MU pipeline. We also release our code implementation at https://github.com/LiouTang/Behavioral-Unlearn-Audit.

</details>


### [156] [Code Correctness Signals in LLM Hidden States: Pre-Generation Probing and Repair Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14530)
*Carlo Di Cicco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507模型上的444个LiveCodeBench任务，发现：(1) 模型首次生成代码的正确性可从最终隐藏状态中线性解码（AUC达0.931），且不受提示长度混淆；(2) 模型修复代码时隐藏状态的变化方向虽统计显著，但归因于修复上下文的协变量而非独立的修复理解特征——通过残差化控制混淆变量后，前一结果稳健，后一结果被推翻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型的隐藏状态是否编码代码正确性信息，特别是在模型生成代码之前以及修复失败代码的过程中，以理解模型内部表征与代码正确性的可解释关系。

Method: 使用线性探针（linear probe）从隐藏状态解码正确性，采用嵌套交叉验证选择探针层；引入残差化（residualization）工具去除提示长度等混淆变量的线性效应；对修复过程采用对比方向分析（contrastive direction），并通过条件残差化控制修复上下文协变量；使用标签打乱（label-shuffled）零假设进行显著性检验。

Result: (1) 首次生成代码的正确性线性可解码AUC为0.931±0.008，残差化后仍达0.911±0.010，远超提示长度基线0.754±0.014。(2) 修复过程的隐藏状态变化方向在统计上显著，但在条件残差化控制修复上下文协变量后不显著，表明该方向是修复成功相关协变量的相关物，而非独立的修复理解特征。

Conclusion: 模型隐藏状态确实编码代码正确性信息，但修复过程中的隐藏状态变化主要反映修复上下文差异而非独立的修复理解机制；方法贡献在于同一残差化工具既能确认正向结果（预生成正确性解码），也能推翻负向结果（修复方向解释），体现了诊断性严谨。

Abstract: Large language models encode rich information in their hidden states. This work asks whether code correctness is legible in the hidden states of Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, before it generates and as it repairs a failed attempt, studied on 444 LiveCodeBench tasks. It reports two findings connected by a single confound-control tool: residualization. First, the correctness of the model's first-attempt code is linearly decodable from the prompt-final hidden state, with a leakage-free held-out AUC of 0.931 +/- 0.008 across 50 outer splits. After the linear effect of prompt length is removed from each hidden state dimension, the probe still reaches 0.911 +/- 0.010, well above a prompt-length baseline of 0.754 +/- 0.014. Second, on 236 cleaned cases where the model attempts to repair a failed first attempt, the hidden state shift from the failing attempt to its repair carries a statistically detectable contrastive direction, significant on both a magnitude and a split-half test against label-shuffled nulls. This direction does not survive a conditional residualization against repair-context covariates that differ between successful and failed repairs, marking it as a correlate of repair success driven by the repair context rather than an isolated repair-comprehension feature. The probe layer is selected by nested cross-validation, and the same residualization approach that upholds the pre-generation correctness result overturns the repair-direction interpretation. The contribution is as much methodological as empirical: a diagnostic honest enough to report a negative result alongside a positive one.

</details>


### [157] [The Risk Shadow of Principal Component Analysis: When 99.9999% Variance Preservation Causes Catastrophic Decision Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14533)
*Hamidou Tembine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明PCA在降维时可能完全抹除罕见高风险事件信号（即使保留99.9999%方差），并提出ExPCA和TP-PCA两种基于重新加权协方差的方法来保留尾部风险信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCA通过最大化方差保留主要变异，但高影响罕见事件（如欺诈、灾难）的信号可能集中在低方差方向，导致降维后完全丢失关键信息。这在高风险决策场景中存在严重隐患。

Method: 理论证明PCA存在"风险阴影"现象：即使保留极高比例方差，也能完全消除罕见事件信号。提出ExPCA（使用expectile重新加权协方差矩阵）和TP-PCA（保留尾部信息）两种方法，从理论上证明ExPCA在保留罕见事件信息上严格优于PCA。

Result: 在合成数据和真实信用卡欺诈检测基准上验证了风险阴影的存在以及所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 高维数据降维时，方差最大化与尾部风险感知存在根本性矛盾，在高风险场景中应重新审视基于方差的降维方法。

Abstract: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) preserves variance, not the information needed to detect rare catastrophic events. This paper proves the existence of a {\it Risk Shadow}: PCA can retain over 99.9999 percent of total variance while completely erasing all signal about rare, high-impact failures. When this happens, even the best possible classifier operating on the PCA representation reduces to a constant predictor. The root cause is a fundamental mismatch between variance maximization and tail risk awareness. To break the shadow, we introduce Expectile PCA (ExPCA) and Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA), two methods that reweight the data covariance toward high-impact events. We prove theoretically that ExPCA strictly outperforms PCA in retaining rare-event information, and we validate our claims on synthetic data and a real-world credit card fraud detection benchmark. Our results call for a fundamental rethinking of variance-based dimensionality reduction in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [158] [CARE: Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14581)
*Guanyu Liu,Weiyi Kong,Zeyu Wang,Boer Zhang,Baiqing Li,Peiyu Zhang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARE框架，通过可审计控制器让LLM辅助而非主导科学实验优化，在保持安全性同时提升性能，在Minerva/Olympus和ChemLex基准测试上分别从80.0提升至88.5、从83.9提升至92.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接让LLM控制昂贵不可逆的科学实验会导致不安全探索和不稳定性能，但完全舍弃LLM的创造力会损失显著优化潜力，因此需要在安全性和创造力之间取得平衡。

Method: CARE框架包含一个非LLM的现有优化器作为默认路径，LLM用于修改挑战者排序策略；在每次结果揭晓前，公共证据干预门基于已有证据比较挑战者与现有方案，仅当证据支持时才授权选择挑战者，所有决策记录在审计日志中。

Result: 在Minerva/Olympus基准上最终最佳值从80.0提升至88.5，在ChemLex基准上从83.9提升至92.1，优于所有其他评估方法。

Conclusion: LLM自我进化在可审计控制器下扩展提议空间比直接选择实验更可靠。

Abstract: Granting LLMs direct control over costly, irreversible scientific experiments leads to unsafe exploration and unstable performance, but discarding LLM creativity entirely sacrifices significant optimization potential. We introduce CARE (Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation), an auditable controller for high-throughput experimentation (HTE) optimization that keeps a non-LLM incumbent optimizer as the default action path while using LLMs to revise challenger ranking policies. Before each outcome is revealed, a public-evidence intervention gate compares the challenger with the incumbent. It authorizes the challenger's selection only when the evidence available before selection supports the change, with the decision recorded in the audit log. CARE outperforms all other evaluated methods on Minerva/Olympus and ChemLex benchmarks, with final-best improving from 80.0 to 88.5 on Minerva/Olympus and from 83.9 to 92.1 on ChemLex, relative to the public incumbent. Our experiments indicate that LLM self-evolution is more reliable when it expands the proposal space under an auditable controller, rather than directly choosing experiments.

</details>


### [159] [Zero-shot generalization of transformer neural operators to larger domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14597)
*Armand de Villeroché,Sibo Cheng,Vincent Le Guen,Marc Bocquet,Rem-Sophia Mouradi,Patrick Armand,Alban Farchi,Patrick Massin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过可分解的注意力局部性偏置和旋转位置编码的方法，使得基于Transformer的神经算子能够在零样本下推广到比训练时大得多的空间域上求解PDE，显著提升了域扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的神经算子在求解复杂几何上的偏微分方程时表现优异，但它们隐含地假设了固定的计算域大小，导致在推理时无法泛化到更大的域上。本文旨在解决这一域扩展问题，实现零样本下对更大空间域的推理。

Method: 提出在注意力logits计算中引入可分解的局部性偏置，实现精细可控的局部性同时保持与查询-键内积的完全可分解性，兼容优化后的注意力核；结合旋转位置编码（RoPE），在不改变Transformer架构的前提下实现具有可控空间支持的表达性嵌入。

Result: 在两个PDE基准测试和一个3D工业大气流动应用中，所提方法显著提升了向更大域零样本泛化的性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入可分解的注意力局部性偏置与旋转位置编码，可以有效解决基于Transformer的神经算子在域扩展场景下的零样本泛化问题，且不改变原始Transformer架构。

Abstract: Transformer-based neural operators have shown remarkable performance for approximating solution operators of partial differential equations on complex geometries. However, existing approaches implicitly assume a fixed domain size, which limits their ability to generalize at inference. In this work, we investigate domain extension, namely zero-shot inference on spatial domains that are significantly larger than those encountered during training. We argue that this setting fundamentally requires spatial locality and translation equivariance. We propose to implement this locality via a decomposable bias in the attention logits computation, enabling finely controllable locality while remaining fully decomposable into query-key inner products and directly compatible with optimized attention kernels. Combined with rotary positional embeddings, it enables expressive embeddings with controllable spatial support without altering the transformer architecture. We empirically show that our approach substantially improves zero-shot generalization to larger domains across two PDE benchmarks and a 3D industrial atmospheric flow application. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/cerea-daml/domain-extension.

</details>


### [160] [Realizing Native INT8 Compute for Diffusion Transformers on Consumer GPUs: A Fused INT8 GEMM Kernel for Ideogram 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14598)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对消费级Ampere GPU的INT8量化优化方案，通过融合Triton INT8 GEMM核函数，解决了现有INT8量化因软件实现缺陷（先量化再反量化回bf16）导致速度反而不如FP8/NF4的问题，在RTX 3090上实现2.8-4.2x单GEMM加速，端到端约1.1x加速，使INT8从最慢变最快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散Transformer的INT8量化在消费级Ampere GPU上速度反而慢于FP8和NF4，原因是软件实现缺陷：INT8量化后立即反量化为bf16再执行bf16矩阵乘，未利用GPU的INT8张量核心，硬件计算优势完全未被发挥。

Method: 设计了一个融合Triton INT8 GEMM核函数（在Ampere张量核心上执行int8xint8->int32计算，并在epilogue阶段融合per-token x per-channel反量化和bias），替换Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中原有的dequantize-to-bf16路径，每个GEMM形状自动调优。

Result: 单GEMM上比bf16快2.8-4.2x；端到端768px分辨率加速~1.1x（~9-10%）；1024px在单张RTX 3090上156.5秒，快于NF4（164.5秒）和FP8（172.9秒），且无质量损失（PickScore/CLIPScore一致）。

Conclusion: INT8从最慢变最快，1024px成为单GPU可行；但该优化仅适用于消费级Ampere GPU，在A100和B200上不如其原生bf16/FP8路径。

Abstract: Post-training INT8 (W8A8) quantization of diffusion transformers is widely deployed as a speed optimization, yet on consumer Ampere GPUs it is frequently slower than the FP8 and NF4 alternatives it is meant to beat. We trace this to a software artifact: the production "INT8" forward quantizes weights and activations only to immediately dequantize them back to bf16 and run a bf16 matrix multiply, never engaging the GPU's INT8 tensor cores, so the hardware's compute advantage is left entirely unrealized. We close this gap with a single fused Triton INT8 GEMM (int8xint8->int32 on Ampere tensor cores, with per-token x per-channel dequantization and bias folded into the epilogue, autotuned per GEMM shape) dropped into the Ideogram 4.0 diffusion transformer's linear layers in place of the dequantize-to-bf16 path. In the kernel, the int8xint8->int32 accumulation is bit-exact against torch._int_mm and the dequantized output matches the reference at cosine similarity 1.0 with no NaNs, running 2.8-4.2x faster than bf16 per GEMM. End to end it delivers a ~1.1x (~9-10%) speedup at 768px, and at 1024px it generates an image in 156.5 s on a single RTX 3090, faster than the single-card NF4 (164.5 s) and FP8 (172.9 s) baselines, at no measurable quality cost on these point estimates (PickScore/CLIPScore). INT8 thus goes from the slowest variant to the fastest, and 1024px becomes single-GPU feasible. The primary speed criterion (beat FP8, by ~9.5%) is comfortably met; the NF4 margin (~4.9%, single-run n=4) is within run-to-run variance we did not quantify and is best read as consistent with meeting the stretch target. We close with an honest deployment map: the win is specific to consumer Ampere, and on A100 and B200 the same kernel loses to those cards' fast native bf16/FP8 paths.

</details>


### [161] [A Comparative Study of Deep Learning Architectures for Multi-Horizon Behavioural Forecasting for Mobile Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14604)
*Pavlos Nicolaou,Kleanthis Malialis,Artemis Kontou,Panayiotis Kolios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六种深度学习架构、两种零样本基础模型(FM)及统计基线在可穿戴设备行为时间序列预测上的表现，涵盖800多名参与者的步数、屏幕使用时间和睡眠时长三个数据集，评估了1-8天的多步预测性能及个性化微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备和智能手机生成的行为时间序列可用于主动健康干预，但缺乏对现代预测架构的系统性比较研究，特别是在跨人群泛化、个性化微调响应以及多天预测精度退化方面尚不明确。

Method: 基准测试六种深度学习架构（包括PatchTST、TCN、MLP、Transformer等）、两种零样本基础模型(TimesFM等)及统计基线，在三个公开数据集（800+参与者）上按特征（步数、屏幕时间、睡眠时长）和预测时长（1-8天）报告指标，并进行个性化微调研究和FM迁移性评估。

Result: (i) PatchTST在训练模型中表现最佳，但亚军(TCN, MLP, Transformer)之间无显著差异；(ii) TimesFM在零样本场景下匹配或超越训练模型，尤其在数据稀缺时；(iii) 参与者级微调使RMSE降低16-60%，睡眠改善最大，步数改善最小。

Conclusion: 该研究为移动健康预测中的架构选择、FM适用性和个性化策略提供了实用指导，是首个联合评估现代深度学习、基础模型和个性化策略用于可穿戴设备多步行为预测的研究。

Abstract: Wearable devices and smartphones generate rich behavioural time series that can support proactive health interventions, yet systematic comparisons of modern forecasting architectures for these data are lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how models generalise across populations, how different architectures respond to participant-level fine-tuning and how forecasting accuracy degrades across multi-day horizons. We benchmark six deep learning architectures, two zero-shot Foundation Models (FM) and statistical baselines on three public datasets encompassing over 800 participants, reporting per-feature metrics for step counts, screen time and sleep duration across 1-8 day horizons. We further conduct a per-feature personalisation study across all six architectures and assess FM transferability across dataset sizes and temporal granularities. Our key findings are: (i) no single architecture dominates, PatchTST leads among trained models while the three runners-up (TCN, MLP, Transformer) show no meaningful performance difference; (ii) the FM TimesFM matches or exceeds trained models zero-shot, especially in low-data regimes and (iii) participant-level fine-tuning reduces per-feature RMSE by 16-60\%, with sleep benefiting most and step counts least. These results provide practical guidance on architecture selection, FM applicability and personalisation strategies for mobile health forecasting. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to jointly evaluate modern deep learning, FMs and personalisation for multi-horizon behavioural forecasting from wearables.

</details>


### [162] [Expert-Driven Survival Machines: Improving Stratification and Interpretability in Multiple Clinical Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14608)
*Farica Zhuang,Zixuan Wen,Christos Davatzikos,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于混合专家(MoE)的自适应深度聚类生存框架(AdaCSM)，通过路由专家机制实现患者动态分配至特化风险预测器，在保留生存预测和亚型聚类目标的同时，提升生存分析的预测性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生存模型为所有患者学习单一共同特征表示，忽略了患者亚组间的重要差异，无法捕捉异质性生存模式。

Method: 提出AdaCSM框架，引入基于路由的专家机制，在参数化生存建模框架内实现条件特化，动态分配患者至特化风险预测器，同时优化生存预测和亚型聚类目标。

Result: 在多个真实世界纵向临床队列（涵盖多种疾病领域）上与SOTA生存模型和深度聚类模型对比，该方法展现出更优的预测性能，并产生可解释的生存分析结果。

Conclusion: MoE框架能够有效建模异质性生存模式，通过路由机制实现患者个性化风险分层，提高预测精度和模型可解释性。

Abstract: Survival prediction plays a central role for healthcare providers and clinical researchers. Accurate risk stratification enables early intervention and improved patient management. Most existing deep survival models learn one common feature representation for all patients, which may hide important differences between patient subgroups. In contrast, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework allows different parts of the model to focus on different patient patterns, leading to more individualized representations. Therefore, in this work, we propose a mixture-of-experts enhanced adaptive deep clustering survival framework (AdaCSM) for modeling such heterogeneous survival patterns. We introduce a routing-based expert mechanism that enables conditional specialization within a parametric survival modeling framework. The proposed architecture allocates patients to specialized risk predictors dynamically while preserving the patient survival and subtype clustering objectives. We compare our method with state-of-the-art survival and deep clustering models on multiple real-world longitudinal clinical cohorts spanning diverse disease domains. The proposed method demonstrates improved predictive performance and leads to interpretable results in survival analysis.

</details>


### [163] [Neither Parallel Nor Sequential: How DiffusionGemma Actually Commits Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14620)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统测量了DiffusionGemma 26B模型的解码顺序，发现其既非并行也非块自回归，而是具有自左向右的部分提交偏差；这种偏差的强度取决于分析粒度，且模型在大型批次内同步提交token，使得批次内顺序本质上是未定义的。行为具有任务依赖性，并提出了方法论贡献：诚实测量解码顺序需要处理多种混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源扩散语言模型声称是并行的非自回归解码器，但训练好的检查点实际提交token的顺序几乎从未被测量过。本文旨在填补这一空白，揭示扩散模型真实解码行为与理论声称之间的差距。

Method: 对DiffusionGemma 26B模型进行instrumentation，在采样器的accept步骤中记录每个画布位置提交的时间、顺序和置信度。在686个提示、6种设置下进行探针实验，系统分析解码顺序，并处理EOS填充、域内混淆、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性和大型提交批次平局等混淆因素。

Result: 解码既非并行也非块自回归，而是呈现部分左到右提交偏差；按token看顺序很弱，随分析粒度粗化而平滑增强，因此"块大小"是测量尺度的产物而非架构特性。模型在大型同步批次内提交token，批次内顺序本质上是未定义的。结构化的JSON以任意顺序提交；提交置信度在数学推理中跟踪正确性，但在事实回忆中无信号。提交过程激进，在步数预算内以短暂爆发完成，任务准确率与自回归版本的Gemma-4兄弟模型相当。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的实际解码行为与市场宣传存在显著差异，需要严谨的测量方法才能揭示真实行为。本文提供了方法论框架，确保未来对解码顺序的测量不会被人为制造的结果所误导。

Abstract: Open diffusion language models are marketed as parallel, non-autoregressive decoders, yet the order in which a shipped checkpoint actually commits its tokens is almost never measured. We instrument DiffusionGemma 26B, a masked discrete-diffusion mixture-of-experts model built on Gemma 4, hooking its sampler's accept step to record which canvas positions commit, when, and at what confidence. Across a 686-prompt, six-regime probe suite we find that its decoding is neither parallel nor block-autoregressive: it follows a partial left-to-right commit bias whose apparent strength depends almost entirely on the granularity at which you look. Order is weak token by token and strengthens smoothly as the analysis is coarsened, so the model's "block size" turns out to be an artifact of the measuring ruler rather than the architecture. The model commits in large simultaneous batches, leaving much of the within-batch order genuinely undefined rather than merely unobserved. The behaviour is regime-dependent: structured JSON is committed in essentially arbitrary order, and a position's commit confidence tracks correctness on mathematical reasoning but carries no signal on factual recall. Commitment is aggressive, finishing in a short late burst well inside the step budget, while task accuracy matches the model's autoregressive Gemma-4 sibling. Beyond these findings, our central contribution is methodological: measuring decoding order honestly demands handling trailing-EOS padding, within-regime confounding, commit non-monotonicity, block-size sensitivity, and large commit-batch ties, each of which can otherwise manufacture a decoding-order result that is not really there.

</details>


### [164] [Graph Diffusion Residuals for Control-Function Instrumental Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14636)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie,Defu Lian,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应各向异性工具热流（A-IHF），一种基于图扩散的残差提取器，用于高维控制函数IV估计。A-IHF通过检测处理变量的跳跃并调整图传导性来生成保留足够信息的残差，在54个合成基准测试中优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制函数IV估计器需要一阶段残差而非仅预测值，但高容量一阶段模型可能过拟合处理变量，导致残差中信息不足，影响第二阶段估计。需要一种能从高维特征中有效提取残差信息的方法。

Method: 提出自适应各向异性工具热流（A-IHF），一种确定性图扩散残差提取器。将处理变量视为一阶段特征图上的信号，通过引导扩散检测处理跳跃，衰减跨跳跃的传导性，利用稀疏图预解式计算生成控制函数。观测选择规则仅使用(Z,X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性及图可容许性过滤。

Result: 在54个合成基准测试中，经过防护观测选择的A-IHF在结构响应均方误差上平均最低；A-IHF族在32个单元中优于最佳非A-IHF基线。当图捕捉到分段光滑一阶段结构时性能最强。

Conclusion: A-IHF通过图扩散机制有效提取控制函数所需的残差信息，在分段光滑结构下表现最优，为高维IV估计提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Control-function instrumental variable estimators need a first-stage residual, not merely a first-stage prediction. High-capacity first stages can interpolate treatment and leave too little residual information for the outcome equation. We study Adaptive Anisotropic Instrumental Heat Flow (A-IHF), a deterministic graph-diffusion residual extractor for flexible control functions. A-IHF treats treatment as a signal on a graph of first-stage features, uses pilot diffusion to detect large treatment jumps, attenuates conductance across those jumps, and computes the generated control with a sparse graph resolvent. Its observational selection rule uses only $(Z,X)$, combining graph generalized cross-validation, roughness, residualized-treatment relevance, and graph-admissibility filtering. The analysis decomposes error into structural leakage, residual attenuation, and residualized treatment variation, yielding finite-sample bounds, graph-admissibility rates under latent piecewise-smooth geometry, and finite-path selection calibration. Across 54 synthetic benchmark cells with tuned graph, kernel, tree, boosting, series, and neural control-function baselines, guarded observational A-IHF has the lowest average structural-response MSE; the A-IHF family beats the best non-A-IHF baseline in 32 cells. Performance is strongest when the graph captures piecewise-smooth first-stage structure.

</details>


### [165] [Online Convex Optimization with Sublinear Noisy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14640)
*Simone Di Gregorio,Anupam Gupta,Stefano Leonardi,Matteo Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线凸优化（OCO）中，通过引入带噪声的成对探测（pairwise probes）预算来提升最坏情况下的遗憾界。主要结果表明，即使预算为次线性且带噪声，成对探测也能显著降低遗憾，达到了关于参数 $T$、$k$ 和 $δ$ 的最优（除对数因子外）界。分析通过方差缩减效应和连续指数权的二阶分析实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将两个近期研究方向统一到成对探测框架下：专家设定中的次线性最佳专家查询，以及OCO中每轮可用的成对（基于比较）反馈；探究有限预算的带噪声成对探测是否能改进OCO的遗憾界。

Method: 提出统一的成对探测模型，学习者有 $k \le T$ 次探测预算；在探测轮次中可查询两个点并获知损失较小的一个，响应可能带有 $δ$ 噪声。利用方差缩减效应量化探测收益，结合连续指数权（Continuous Exponential Weights）的二阶（基于方差）分析推导遗憾上界。

Result: 获得遗憾界 $\text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right)$，该界在 $T$、$k$ 和 $δ$ 上紧（除对数因子外）。当 $δ$ 接近 $1/2$（即噪声接近抛硬币）时，遗憾保证平滑退化。在有限集合 $K$（专家设定）中，所有参数（包括 $d$）的界完全紧。

Conclusion: 证明了即使带有噪声的次线性成对探测预算也能在完整反馈OCO中显著提升最坏情况遗憾；方法的统一性和紧性表明该框架具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: We study Online Convex Optimization (OCO) over a convex set $K\subseteq \mathbb R^d$, where in each round $t$ the learner selects $x_t\in K$ and then observes a convex loss $f_t:K\to[0,1]$, with the goal of minimizing regret to the best fixed decision in hindsight. We introduce a unified probing model that generalizes two recent lines of work: sublinear best-expert queries in the experts setting, and pairwise (comparison-based) feedback available every round in OCO. In our framework, the learner has a budget of $k\le T$ pairwise probes; on a probed round it may query two points and learn which one has smaller loss.
  Our main result shows that even a sublinear and noisy probe budget can provably improve worst-case regret in the full feedback OCO regime. With $k$ $δ$-noisy pairwise probes, we obtain: $ \text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right) $, which is tight (up to logarithmic factors in $T$) across $T$, $k$ and $δ$. Specifically regarding the noise parameter $δ\in [0,1]$, the regret guarantee smoothly degrades as the oracle response approaches a coin flip, i.e., $δ$ is close to $\frac{1}{2}$. When applying the same techniques to a finite $K$ for the prediction with $d$ experts setting, the resulting rates are instead completely tight in all parameters, including $d$.
  Our analysis gives a streamlined treatment of pairwise probing in OCO by quantifying the benefit of probing via a variance reduction effect, combined with a second-order (variance-based) analysis of Continuous Exponential Weights.

</details>


### [166] [Which Directions Matter? Sparse Design for Affine Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14648)
*Pedro Chumpitaz-Flores,My Duong,Juan S. Borrero,Kaixun Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了当不确定性由有限字典和预算约束定义时，如何选择原子不确定性集子集，提出基于覆盖目标的数据驱动选择规则，并证明该目标是单调且子模的，支持贪心算法具有(1-1/e)近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒机器学习与优化依赖于不确定性模型的选择，需要探究在有限字典和预算约束下，模型必须覆盖哪些不确定性方向。

Method: 提出基于评估方向覆盖目标的数据驱动子集选择规则，包括梯度、对抗扰动或留出数据上的偏移；证明目标函数具有单调性和子模性，支持贪心方法并提供(1-1/e)近似保证及匹配的硬度障碍；还提供了子集损失界证书和带样本外控制的半径校准规则。

Result: 证明了所提覆盖目标是单调且子模的，贪心算法具有(1-1/e)近似保证，并存在匹配的硬度障碍。

Conclusion: 提出的数据驱动选择规则能够有效地构建原子不确定性集，为鲁棒优化提供可处理且具有理论保证的不确定性建模方法。

Abstract: Robust machine learning and optimization rely on the uncertainty model choice. We investigate which uncertainty directions a model must cover when defined by a finite dictionary and a budget constraint. Selecting a subset forms an atomic uncertainty set with a closed form support function, yielding tractable robust programs for affine objectives. We propose a data driven selection rule based on a coverage objective over evaluation directions, including gradients, adversarial perturbations, or shifts observed on held out data. We prove this objective is monotone and submodular, supporting a greedy method with a $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantee and a matching hardness barrier. We also provide a certificate bounding the loss from the selected subset and a radius calibration rule with out of sample control.

</details>


### [167] [Graph Structured Combinatorial Semi-Bandit with Nonlinear Reward Associations through Separable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14650)
*Christoph Bauschmann,Setareh Maghsudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图的因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法和泰勒函数过程逼近的新型通用自适应策略，用于高效识别大规模互联数据中的最优结构，并建立了随时间亚线性和随数据量线性的理论性能保证，在合成与真实交通数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模互联数据中识别最优结构需要大量采样和计算资源，而学习信号依赖关系虽可提升效率，但非线性统计关系的普遍性使问题复杂度显著增加。

Method: 开发了结合图基因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法和泰勒函数过程逼近的通用自适应策略，并建立了亚线性时间复杂度和线性数据量复杂度的理论保证，同时分析了噪声干扰、模型收敛和解空间失配等不确定性下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界交通数据集上的数值实验验证了框架的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架仅依赖极少的先验假设或条件即可广泛适用，并可通过多种修改适应特定或扩展场景。

Abstract: The identification of optimal structures within vast arrays of interconnected data necessitates significant sampling- and computational effort. Learning and leveraging underlying signal dependencies can improve efficiency and predictive capabilities considerably, but the ubiquity of nonlinear statistical relations amplifies the complexity of such undertakings. In this paper, we develop novel generic and adaptive strategies equipped with routines for graph-based causal reward modeling, analytic reproducing kernel methods, and Taylor approximation of functional processes. We establish theoretical performance guarantees sublinear in time and linear in data volume over time. Our analyses cover robustness to a multitude of uncertainties arising from noise interference, gradual model convergence, and solution space mismatch. The framework's general appeal is substantiated by a minimalistic set of conditions or reliance on prior estimates, while various outlined modifications address specific or extended settings. To demonstrate practical effectiveness, we conduct numerical experiments using both benchmarked synthetic and real-world transportation datasets.

</details>


### [168] [Beyond task performance: Decoding bioacoustic embeddings with speech features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14662)
*Ines Nolasco,Jules Cauzinille,Marius Miron,Gagan Narula,Milad Alizadeh,Emmanuel Fernandez,Matthieu Geist,Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon,Olivier Pietquin,Emmanuel Chemla,Sara Keen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过线性与非线性回归探针，揭示了预训练音频嵌入在生物声学中编码的声学特征（如响度、基频等），发现不同模型覆盖互补的声学空间，拼接嵌入表现最佳，并基于特征可恢复性与物种特征显著性（NMI）提供了数据驱动的模型选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练音频嵌入在生物声学中被广泛使用，但人们对这些模型编码了哪些声学特征、哪些特征对特定任务有用知之甚少，这阻碍了透明性并限制了向稀有物种或数据稀缺领域的扩展。

Method: 使用88个eGeMAPS声学特征，跨越六个分类群，应用线性与非线性回归探针来量化每个模型捕获的声学属性，并通过交叉引用特征可恢复性与每物种特征显著性（NMI）来指导模型选择。

Result: 发现“没有免费午餐”模式：没有单一模型能覆盖全部特征空间；拼接嵌入达到最高性能；响度特征编码最好（R²=0.76），基频（F0）最难恢复（R²=0.33）。

Conclusion: 不同模型在声学空间覆盖上具有互补性，通过特征可恢复性与物种特征显著性的交叉分析，可为生物声学任务提供数据驱动的模型选择指导。

Abstract: Pretrained audio embeddings are standard in bioacoustics, yet little is known about which acoustic features these models encode, nor which are useful for a given task. This hinders transparency and limits extension to rare species or data-scarce domains. Here we reveal which speech-like features are encoded in bioacoustic representations. Using the 88~eGeMAPS features across six taxonomic groups, we apply linear and nonlinear regression probes to quantify which acoustic properties each model captures. Results confirm a ``no free lunch'' pattern: no single model captures the full feature space. A concatenated embedding achieves the highest performance, suggesting complementary acoustic space coverage across models. Loudness features are best encoded ($R^2 = 0.76$) while F0 is hardest to recover ($R^2 = 0.33$). By cross-referencing recoverability with per-species feature salience (NMI), we derive data-driven model selection guidance for bioacoustics.

</details>


### [169] [When to Write and When to Suppress: Route-Specialized Dual Adapters for Memory-Assisted Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14668)
*Yining Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种路由专用双适配器编辑器(METHOD)，通过相关性路由器决定是否接收编辑记忆，对路由提示用编辑适配器更新对象偏好，对非路由提示用局部适配器保持原始偏好，在三个基准测试上取得了最佳概率偏好准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑系统需要在更新选定事实的同时保持邻近但不相关行为不变，核心设计问题不仅是如何写入编辑，还包括何时抑制编辑。

Method: 提出\method{}，一种路由专用双适配器编辑器。包含一个相关性路由器决定提示是否应接收编辑记忆；路由提示使用编辑适配器训练为偏好新对象而非原始对象；非路由非直接提示使用单独的局部适配器训练为保持或恢复原始对象偏好。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，在CF(0.8180)、ZsRE(0.8946)和MQuAKE(0.9922)三个基准测试上均获得最佳概率偏好准确率；Qwen3-8B上趋势一致。路由器消融实验显示不同数据集的相关记忆边界不同；组件和模块消融显示增益主要来自编辑注入与关闭路由抑制的分离。

Conclusion: Memory-assisted知识编辑中，编辑的抑制与写入同等重要，通过路由专用双适配器分离编辑注入和离路抑制能显著提升性能。

Abstract: Knowledge editing systems must update selected facts while preserving nearby but irrelevant behavior. This paper studies this problem in a memory-assisted setting where an edit memory is retrieved at inference time and a parameter-efficient adapter corrects the model's object preference. We argue that the central design question is not only how to write an edit, but also when to suppress it. We introduce \method{}, a route-specialized dual-adapter editor. A relevance router first decides whether a prompt should receive an edit memory. Routed prompts use an edit adapter trained to prefer the new object over the original object; unrouted non-direct prompts use a separate locality adapter trained to preserve or restore the original-object preference. We evaluate \method{} on three 1,000-case protocols, \cf{}, \zsre{}, and \mquake{}, under the same memory protocol and two 7B/8B base models. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, \method{} obtains the best overall probability-preference accuracy on all three benchmarks: 0.8180 on \cf{}, 0.8946 on \zsre{}, and 0.9922 on \mquake{}. The same trend holds on Qwen3-8B. Router ablations show that the relevant memory boundary differs across datasets: a lexical neural router is safest on \cf{}, while BGE embedding routing is better on \zsre{} and \mquake{}. Component and module ablations show that the gain mainly comes from separating edit injection from off-route suppression rather than from simply increasing LoRA capacity.

</details>


### [170] [Compressed Computation is (probably) not Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14673)
*Jai Bhagat,Sara Molas-Medina,Giorgi Giglemiani,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究压缩计算(CC)玩具模型是否属于叠加计算实例，发现CC模型通过噪声残差流混合输入，性能提升源于标签混合矩阵而非叠加计算，因此CC不是叠加计算的合适玩具模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证CC玩具模型(Braun et al., 2025)是否是叠加计算(computation in superposition)的一个实例。

Method: 分析CC模型的噪声残差流导致的输入混合效应；将训练目标分解为ReLU项和混合项；检查神经元方向与混合矩阵特征子空间的关系；构建半非负矩阵分解(SNMF)基线进行对比。

Result: 性能提升与混合矩阵的幅度相关，移除混合矩阵后性能消失；学习到的神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间中；SNMF基线能够复现定性损失曲线并优于先前基线，但仍不及训练模型。

Conclusion: CC模型的表现由标签混合矩阵主导，而非真正的叠加计算，因此CC不是叠加计算的合适玩具模型。

Abstract: We study whether the Compressed Computation (CC) toy model (Braun et al., 2025) is an instance of computation in superposition. The CC model appears to compute 100 ReLU functions with just 50 neurons, achieving a better loss than expected from only representing 50 ReLU functions. We show that the model mixes inputs via its noisy residual stream, corresponding to an unintended mixing matrix in the labels. Splitting the training objective into the ReLU term and the mixing term, we find that performance gains scale with the magnitude of the mixing matrix and vanish when the matrix is removed. The learned neuron directions concentrate in the subspace associated with the top 50 eigenvalues of the mixing matrix, suggesting that the mixing term governs the solution. Finally, a semi-non-negative matrix factorization (SNMF) baseline derived solely from the mixing matrix reproduces the qualitative loss profile and improves on prior baselines, though it does not match the trained model. These results suggest CC is not a suitable toy model of computation in superposition.

</details>


### [171] [Flood and Harvest: The Provable Necessity of Trivia for Generating Valuable Mathematics via the Lens of Language Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14688)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Dai Shi,Zheng Gao,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI系统在证明助手下生成形式化数学时的价值问题，建立了嵌套语言生成模型，形式化了“有价值数学”的概念，并通过四个核心结论阐明了验证器（proof checker）与品味（taste）之间的根本区别：验证器无法替代品味，它能保证正确性但无法避免产生无限但渐近可忽略的正确而无价值的陈述（trivia），其核心在于一个尖锐二分法——有限个trivia时最优覆盖率为α/2，无限个trivia（即使速率趋零）则跳升至1-α/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统结合证明助理生成形式化数学的规模日益增大，但验证器能验证的内容与数学家认为有价值的内容之间存在根本性差距，这一差距已成为瓶颈。本文试图从理论上刻画这一差距的本质，并回答验证器能否替代品味这一核心问题。

Method: 将有价值数学的生成建模为极限情况下的嵌套语言生成问题：可验证的形式语言F通过成员查询（proof checker）访问，其中包含一个未知的有价值语言H，通过对手枚举核心C（密度α，即文献）来揭示。每个输出分为有价值（∈H）、琐碎（∈F\H）、或幻觉（∉F）。在此框架下研究四个问题：可覆盖性、可靠性、最优覆盖率与压缩模型实例化。

Result: （1）验证器不能替代品味：具有广度的生成所能覆盖的集合与无验证器模型相同，由Angluin条件刻划；（2）验证器提供可靠的覆盖：能覆盖所有未见的陈述同时只断言有效语句，将错误从假阳性转移到琐碎；（3）核心二分法：有限trivia时最优覆盖率为α/2，无限trivia（任意低速率）跳升至1-α/2，间隙1-α为未记录的价值；（4）两种模式均在数学的压缩模型中有实例。

Conclusion: 完美验证器不能替代品味：无限但渐近可忽略的正确但无价值的陈述流并非工程事故，而是可证明的必然——覆盖未记录的有价值数学必然需要无限但渐近可忽略的认证trivia流。

Abstract: AI systems coupled to proof assistants now generate formal mathematics at scale, and the gap between what a checker can verify and what a mathematician would value has become the binding constraint. We model the generation of valuable mathematics as nested language generation in the limit: a verifiable formal language $F$, accessed through a membership oracle (the proof checker), contains an unknown valuable language $H \in \mathcal{H}$ revealed only through an adversarial enumeration of a core $C \subseteq H$ of exact density $α$ (the literature). Every output is valuable ($\in H$), trivial ($\in F \setminus H$), or a hallucination ($\notin F$). We settle four questions. First, the verifier is not taste: the collections admitting generation with breadth are exactly those of the oracle-free model, characterized fiber-wise by Angluin's condition. Second, the verifier does buy sound coverage, covering all unseen valuable statements while asserting only valid ones: possible with it, impossible without it; it relocates unavoidable errors from false to trivial. Third, and centrally, a sharp dichotomy on the tight family: generators emitting finitely many trivia achieve optimal coverage $α/2$, while any infinite trivia allowance, even at vanishing rate, jumps the optimum to $1-α/2$ (both tight, for cores presented as the candidate intersection), and one generator attains both ends. The transition is in trivia count, not rate; the gap $1-α$ is the unrecorded mass. Fourth, both regimes instantiate in a compression model of mathematics. A perfect verifier cannot substitute for taste: the unbounded stream of correct-but-worthless statements is not an engineering accident but a provable necessity, since covering unrecorded valuable mathematics requires an infinite, but asymptotically negligible, stream of certified trivia.

</details>


### [172] [A Complexity Measure for Active Learning in Multi-group Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14690)
*Abdellah Aznag,Rachel Cummings,Adam N. Elmachtoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多组均值估计d臂赌博机中的最大风险主动学习目标，提出了局部极小极大下界，将难度分解为预算项、异方差指数和方差局部曲率三个正交因子，并通过损失诱导的ℓ1几何和基于表示的实例生成器证明了近优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多组均值估计的d臂赌博机问题中，现有主动学习方法缺乏针对最大风险目标的一般性下界，难以刻画不同因子对学习难度的影响。

Method: 建立了局部极小极大框架，引入损失诱导的ℓ1几何和基于表示的实例生成器，将困难实例构造转化为显式随机矩阵计算。

Result: 证明了首个一般性下界，将难度分解为预算项、异方差指数和方差局部曲率（VLC）三个正交因子，VLC在光滑类中是方差-费希尔信息量的重新参数化。

Conclusion: 该下界与最强上界相比在宽泛条件下达到对数因子的近优性，并指出了高异质性实例中的系统差距。

Abstract: We study a \emph{max-risk} objective for active learning in a multi-group mean estimation $d$-armed bandits: a learner adaptively allocates a budget of $T$ samples across $d$ groups to minimize the worst-case uncertainty index $\max_{k\in[d]}σ_k^2/n_k$, where $σ_k$ is the standard deviation of the distribution of arm $d$, and $n_k$ is the number of times arm $d$ is sampled. We develop a local minimax framework and prove the first general lower bound for this objective, valid for any finite-variance hypothesis class. The bound separates difficulty into three orthogonal factors: a \emph{budget} term, a \emph{heteroscedasticity} index measuring how unevenly the uncertainty is spread across arms, and a model-dependent complexity measure, the \emph{Variance Local Curvature} ($\mathrm{VLC}$), which captures how much information a local change of variance creates inside the hypothesis class. For smooth classes, the $\mathrm{VLC}$ is a reparametrization of a variance--Fisher information, with closed-form values for common families. Benchmarking against the strongest available upper bound shows near-optimality up to logarithmic factors in broad regimes, and pinpoints a systematic gap in highly heterogeneous instances. Our proof introduces two key ingredients: a loss-induced $\ell_1$ geometry on the decision space, and a representation-based instance generator that reduces hard-instance construction to an explicit random matrix calculation.

</details>


### [173] [Persona-Pruner: Sculpting Lightweight Models for Role-Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14695)
*Jinsu Kim,Jihoon Tack,Noah Lee,Jongheon Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Persona-Pruner框架，通过从大语言模型中裁剪出角色特定的子网络，实现轻量级角色扮演模型，在显著降低计算成本的同时保持角色扮演性能，相比现有剪枝方法性能下降减少最高93.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型作为角色扮演聊天机器人虽表现出色，但在多角色同时交互等实际应用中，为每个角色部署完整的大模型计算成本过高，需要探索更高效的角色专用模型。

Method: 提出Persona-Pruner框架，从单一角色描述中隔离出角色特定的子网络，通过针对性剪枝保留角色特质而非冗余知识，避免直接剪枝对角色扮演性能的严重损害。

Result: 在RoleBench上，相比最强基线，Persona-Pruner将性能下降减少高达93.8%（LLM-as-a-judge评分），同时保持通用LLM能力。

Conclusion: 角色身份仅依赖模型总容量的一小部分，通过人物特定子网络裁剪可以有效实现轻量级角色扮演模型，兼顾效率与性能。

Abstract: Language Models (LMs) have shown remarkable potential as role-playing chatbots, delivering consistent, stylized interactions when given a specification of a character or user persona. However, applying these capabilities to real-world applications (e.g., ecosystems with numerous NPCs interacting simultaneously) exposes a critical inefficiency due to the excessive computational cost. In this paper, we question the necessity of dedicating a full, generalist model to a single persona, hypothesizing that a specific character identity relies on only a fraction of the model's total capacity. We observe that naively pruning LMs often severely degrades the role-playing performance for a specific persona; it does not distinguish between redundant knowledge and essential character traits. We propose Persona-Pruner, a framework that sculpts a lightweight role-playing model by isolating persona-specific sub-networks from a single description. Our experiments consistently show that Persona-Pruner preserves role-playing performance substantially more effectively than existing state-of-the-art LLM pruning techniques, reducing the performance drop from the dense model by up to 93.8% over the strongest baseline on RoleBench in LLM-as-a-judge score, while still maintaining general LLM capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/jsu-kim/Persona-Pruner.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Co-Evolved Spiking Neural Network Ensembles via Marginal Contribution Fitness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13985)
*Catherine Rodriquez,James Ghawaly*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于合作博弈论和差异评估函数的协同进化集成框架，通过边际贡献分配适应度，在进化过程中鼓励SNN互补专业化，显著提升了分类、回归和控制任务性能，尤其在控制任务中实现了从进化失败到近最优的质变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）进化优化的难度随任务复杂度增加而急剧上升，因为拓扑与参数的联合搜索空间随网络规模超指数增长，现有方法难以有效应对这一扩展性挑战。

Method: 提出协同进化集成框架：种群中候选SNN的适应度由每个网络对集成性能的边际贡献定义，基于合作博弈论和多智能体系统中的差异评估函数进行信用分配，奖励持续提升集成性能的网络并惩罚冗余，在进化过程中鼓励互补专业化，而非事后组合独立训练的网络。

Result: 在μCaspian神经形态硬件约束下的分类、回归和控制任务中，协同进化集成在所有任务上均显著优于单网络进化和事后集成，控制任务中标准进化无法发现有效策略，而协同进化实现了近最优性能的质变。

Conclusion: 协同进化集成框架通过进化过程中的互补专业化有效解决了SNN扩展性挑战，在多种任务上取得显著改进，尤其在控制任务中实现了从失败到近最优的突破性性能提升。

Abstract: Evolutionary optimization of spiking neural networks (SNNs) becomes increasingly difficult as task complexity grows because they must search a combined topology--parameter space that grows super-exponentially with network size. We address this scaling challenge through a co-evolutionary ensemble framework in which a population of candidate SNNs is evolved with fitness defined by each network's marginal contribution to group performance. Grounded in cooperative game theory and difference evaluation functions from multiagent systems, this credit assignment rewards networks that consistently improve ensemble performance and penalizes redundancy, encouraging complementary specialization during evolution rather than relying on post-hoc combination of independently trained networks. We evaluate the approach on classification, regression, and control tasks under $μ$Caspian neuromorphic hardware constraints. Co-evolved ensembles achieve statistically significant improvements over both single-network evolution and post-hoc ensembles across all tasks, with the most pronounced gains in control, where standard evolution fails to discover effective policies and co-evolution enables a qualitative transition to near-optimal performance.

</details>


### [175] [A Programmer's Guide to Cascaded Adaptive Combiners: Online Learning by Biologically Accurate Models of Multilayer Neuron Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14146)
*Martin Nilsson,Denis Kleyko*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更具生物学合理性的多层神经元网络模型，该模型采用简化的机制性神经元，能够在线流式学习，并在图像分类任务中取得了与反向传播相竞争的性能，为统一生物神经元模型与机器学习提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工神经网络使用的加权求和神经元模型忽略了生物神经元的真实计算特性，作者希望提出一种更贴近生物计算机制的模型，同时保持简单高效的学习能力。

Method: 构建了一个机制性的多层神经元网络模型，该模型支持在线流式学习，避免了传统反向传播的离线批处理需求。

Result: 在图像分类任务中取得了与反向传播相竞争的分类性能。

Conclusion: 该方法的简洁性、生物基础性和广泛适用性为统一机制性神经元模型与机器学习算法开辟了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Learning in biological multilayer neuronal networks offers insights that extend beyond the classical weighted-sum neuron model commonly used in artificial neural networks. This article presents an accessible guide to a mechanistic neuronal network model that more accurately captures aspects of biological computation while enabling a simple yet powerful mechanism for learning in multilayer neural networks. The proposed approach supports efficient online streamed learning and provides a practical alternative to backpropagation. We demonstrate its potential in an image classification task, achieving competitive classification performance. The approach's simplicity, biological grounding, and broad applicability highlight a promising path toward algorithms that unify mechanistic neuron models and machine learning.

</details>


### [176] [MeEvo: Metacognitive Evolution Combined with Natural Evolution for Automatic Heuristic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14202)
*Zishang Qiu,Xinan Chen,Rong Qu,Ruibin Bai*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出MeEvo，一种双层次自动启发式设计框架，通过循环耦合自然进化（代码探索）和元认知进化（推理反思），利用共享历史记录实现种群探索与反思优化的相互增强，在五个优化问题中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种LLM驱动的自动启发式设计范式存在局限：自然进化丢弃推理轨迹，削弱知识继承与利用；元认知进化缺乏种群级重组，限制探索且易早熟收敛。两者均降低了复杂问题的搜索效率、稳定性和解质量。

Method: 提出MeEvo双层次框架：(1) 自然进化层负责探索启发式代码，同时将推理轨迹、适应度值和错误记录到共享历史中；(2) 元认知进化层基于共享历史进行反思，生成改进的启发式并重新进入父代池进行下一轮循环。两层次循环耦合，实现种群驱动的探索与反思驱动的细化相互强化。

Result: 在五个优化问题、两种LLM骨干模型上的实验表明，MeEvo相比现有LLM-based AHD方法取得了更强且更稳定的性能，尤其在复杂约束任务上优势显著。

Conclusion: MeEvo通过双层次循环耦合的设计，有效融合了种群探索与反思优化的优势，为自动启发式设计提供了一种更高效、更稳定的框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced Automatic Heuristic Design (AHD) by enabling heuristic generation through reasoning and code synthesis. Existing LLM-based AHD architectures mainly follow two paradigms: Natural Evolution, which uses crossover and mutation to explore heuristic programs, and Metacognitive Evolution, which refines reasoning through reflection. However, Natural Evolution discards reasoning traces, weakening knowledge inheritance and exploitation, while Metacognitive Evolution lacks population-level recombination, limiting exploration and increasing the risk of premature convergence. These limitations reduce search efficiency, stability, and solution quality on complex problems. To address this gap, we propose MeEvo, a dual-layer AHD framework that cyclically couples Natural Evolution and Metacognitive Evolution. Natural Evolution explores heuristic code while recording reasoning traces, fitness values, and errors into a shared history; Metacognitive Evolution then reflects on this history to generate improved heuristics that re-enter the parent pool for the next cycle. This design enables population-driven exploration and reflection-driven refinement to reinforce each other. Experiments on five optimization problems with two LLM backbones show that MeEvo achieves stronger and more stable performance than existing LLM-based AHD architectures, especially on complex constrained tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Do programming languages still matter to your AI coding agent teammate? Evidence at scale from chess engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13763)
*Mathieu Acher,Jean-Marc Jézéquel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿编码智能体（Claude Code和Codex）在17种编程语言中从零构建国际象棋引擎的能力。结果表明，智能体确实具备多语言编程能力，能在所有目标语言中生成可工作的系统，包括没有先验开源成果的语言（如LaTeX）。然而语言选择仍显著影响系统性能：仅在主流编译型语言中能达到强棋力，随着语言变得小众，成本和工程努力急剧增加，功能选择在不同语言族系间存在差异。智能体会自动验证自身工作，但其强度自评估存在偏差，部分引擎通过调用现成库"作弊"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究前沿编码智能体是否能在任意编程语言中生成完整的软件系统，以及语言选择是否仍然影响生成工件的质量、成本和特征，从而回答"编程语言在AI时代的角色"这一根本问题。

Method: 以国际象棋引擎作为多组件非平凡系统的测试案例，使用两个前沿编码智能体（Claude Code和Codex），在无象棋知识或实现指导的条件下，生成跨越17种主要编程语言的34个引擎。结合各引擎特征分析、独立Elo强度评估、会话轨迹以及代码和对话记录的定性分析进行综合研究。

Result: 所有17种语言均生成了至少一个功能完备的引擎，部分语言（如LaTeX）此前无同类开源成果；但仅在主流编译型语言中能达到强棋力，小众语言成本显著增加，功能选择呈现语言族系差异，部分引擎会通过调用现成库走捷径，且智能体自我评估存在偏差。

Conclusion: 编程语言已不再是AI能否构建可工作系统的问题，而是关乎性能、成本、构建内容以及需要多少人工监督验证的问题。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents now promise end-to-end authorship of complete software systems. Two empirical questions follow: can AI coding-agent teammates program in any target language, including ones with no comparable prior open-source artefact? If so, does language choice still shape the artefact, and along which dimensions? We study both through a polyglot case study built around chess engines: non-trivial multi-component systems that admit a hierarchy of language-agnostic oracles, from exact move-generation correctness to a strength scale (Elo), observable from Rust to Brainfuck. We prompted two frontier agents (Claude Code and Codex) at the capability level, without chess knowledge or implementation guidance, under a documented intervention and stopping policy. The agents produced 34 chess engines spanning 17 primary programming languages, from mainstream to specialised, domain-specific, legacy, and esoteric targets. We combine per-engine feature analysis, independent Elo assessment, and session trajectories with qualitative analysis of code and transcripts.
  Frontier coding agents are genuinely polyglot: every language we tried produced at least one feature-rich working engine, several with no prior open-source counterpart of comparable scope (e.g., LaTeX), and the code is synthesised from scratch rather than copied. Yet language choice still matters: strong playing strength is only reachable in mainstream compiled languages, cost and engineering effort grow sharply as the language becomes more exotic, and feature choices shift across language families. Agents validate their own work unprompted, but their strength self-estimates are biased and a few engines cheated by calling a chess library. Programming language is no longer about whether AI teammates can build a working system, but about performance, cost, what gets built, and how much human supervision validation still needs.

</details>


### [178] [I'm Sorry Driver, I'm Afraid I Can't Do That: Appraising the Safety of LLMs within Automotive Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14327)
*Shaun Feakins,Ibrahim Habli,Kim Littler,Robert Palin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了当前AI开发框架中，将大语言模型集成到汽车控制任务时面临的安全保障挑战，包括概念性挑战（上游通用模型与下游特定车辆架构的适配）和具体工程约束（如ISO21448的延迟问题、ISO/PAS8800的对齐问题），并通过Talk2Drive案例研究论证现有方案的局限性，最后提出了针对LLM相关危险事件的潜在保证机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在汽车领域的应用正在快速推进，但现有框架在实时安全关键场景下的有效性存在显著不足，亟需从安全保证角度系统评估这些挑战并探索可行的保障机制。

Method: 采用概念分析、现有标准梳理（ISO21448和ISO/PAS8800）与实验案例研究（基于开源项目Talk2Drive）相结合的方式，通过构建安全论证（safety argument）来明确现有解决方案的局限性。

Result: 发现了双重挑战：（1）概念层面：部署者需同时保证上游开发的通用模型在下游特定车辆架构中的安全性；（2）具体工程层面：存在ISO21448中的延迟等基础工程约束，以及ISO/PAS8800中LLM特有的对齐问题。

Conclusion: 尽管LLM在汽车场景中的应用面临显著安全挑战，但本文提出了面向LLM相关危险事件的潜在保证机制，为未来在安全关键领域部署LLM提供了方向性指引。

Abstract: This paper appraises recent frameworks within AI development to integrate LLMs into control tasks in automotive contexts from the perspective of safety assurance. This work has built upon the rapid integration of LLMs across automotive settings. However, we find that at present, these frameworks face significant challenges, limiting their efficacy in real-time safety-critical contexts. Firstly, we consider conceptual challenges, including the fact that deployers are faced with a dual challenge, wherein they must assure a model which has been developed upstream, i.e. as general-purpose tools by the large AI labs, in a downstream context, i.e. into specific vehicle architectures. Secondly, we consider concrete challenges from across existing standards. We show that there are currently both fundamental engineering constraints covered in ISO21448, such as latency, and novel LLM-specific issues, such as alignment-related issues covered in ISO/PAS8800. We ground both examples in a concrete introductory, experimental case study exploring an existing open-source repository, Talk2Drive. We present a safety argument in order to make explicit the limitations of existing solutions. Nonetheless, given that the use of LLMs in automotive contexts is being explored at a technical level and operationalised, we propose potential assurance mechanisms for LLM-related hazardous events going forward.

</details>


### [179] [A Benchmark and Framework for Evaluating Next Action Predictions in Spreadsheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13802)
*Tejas Agrawal,Vu Le,Sumit Gulwani,Gust Verbruggen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于电子表格预测代码补全的基准测试，包含人工策划的52个动作序列（12K个动作），并设计在线评估方法，分析多种基线模型在不同维度上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格中缺乏预测性代码补全功能，现有公共电子表格语料库缺少编辑历史记录，且电子表格动作具有空间、时间和组合上的复杂性。

Method: 人工策划52个序列（12K动作），通过参数化启发式和LLM精炼重建电子表格；提出在线评估方法：每步预测后接受或拒绝，更新后续动作，重复直到目标表格达成。使用零样本LLM、微调小模型和经典模型作为基线。

Result: 分析了已保存动作与误报、效率、用户画像影响、触发影响、上下文影响等多个属性。

Conclusion: 提出的基准测试有效评估了电子表格环境中的预测代码补全系统，揭示了不同模型在该任务上的性能特性。

Abstract: Predictive code completion greatly accelerates how quickly developers work. In spreadsheets, despite being much more common, such auto-completion features are virtually non-existent. To address this gap, we introduce a benchmark for systems that observe a sequence of user actions in a spreadsheet and predict future actions. Two challenges are (1) the absence of edit histories in public spreadsheet corpora and (2) the complex space of spreadsheet actions (spatial, temporal, composite). To address (1), we manually curate 52 sequences of 12K actions that recreate spreadsheets from public corpora, seeded by parametrized heuristics and LLM refinement. To address (2), we propose an online evaluation that expects a prediction after each user action, accepts or rejects that prediction, updates the future actions upon acceptance, and repeats this until the target spreadsheet is obtained. We use multiple baseline predictors (including zero-shot LLMs, fine-tuned SLMs, and classical models) and analyze different properties that our benchmark teaches us, including but not limited to: properties of saved actions and false positives, efficiency, effect of user profiles, effect of triggers, and effect of context.

</details>


### [180] [An Empirical Study of Gemini 3 for Detecting Natural Language Test Smells in Manual Test Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13804)
*Keila Lucas,Rohit Gheyi,Márcio Ribeiro,Fabio Palomba,Luana Martins,Elvys Soares*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探究了使用大型语言模型（GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW）通过基于提示的完整测试用例分析策略，检测自然语言手工测试用例中的测试坏味。相比之前使用小型语言模型（如GEMMA-3-4B、LLAMA-3.2-3B、PHI-4-14B）逐步骤分析的方法，本文的整用例分析策略能够考虑步骤间的依赖关系。实验基于100个Ubuntu测试用例，覆盖7种坏味类型，结果表明GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW在检测性能上优于SLM，并能提供可操作的解释。同时研究发现测试坏味普遍存在，平均每步骤检测到近一个坏味，凸显了对手工测试工件进行自动化质量支持的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工测试用例中存在大量测试坏味（如歧义、冗余、缺失检查等），降低测试的可靠性、可维护性和可复现性。现有检测方法依赖人工规则，难以泛化和扩展至异构测试套件。此前研究仅评估了小型语言模型逐步骤检测的可行性，尚未探索大型语言模型在考虑步骤间依赖关系的整用例分析策略下的检测能力。

Method: 采用基于提示的完整测试用例分析策略，将整个测试用例作为输入，利用GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW模型识别7种测试坏味类型。实验在100个Ubuntu手工测试用例上进行评估，并与之前评估的三种SLM（GEMMA-3-4B、LLAMA-3.2-3B、PHI-4-14B）进行性能对比。

Result: GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW在检测性能上优于所有对比的小型语言模型，同时能生成可操作的解释以帮助实践者改进测试用例。研究发现测试坏味普遍存在，平均每测试步骤中检测到近一个坏味。

Conclusion: LLM（特别是GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW）结合整用例分析策略在手工艺测试用例的坏味检测中具有显著优势，能提供更好的性能和实用解释。手工测试用例的质量问题普遍且严重，亟需可扩展的自动化质量支持工具。

Abstract: Manual testing, in which testers follow natural language instructions to validate system behavior, remains essential for uncovering issues that are difficult to capture with automation. However, manual test cases often contain test smells, quality issues such as ambiguity, redundancy, or missing checks that reduce reliability, maintainability, and reproducibility. Existing detection approaches largely depend on manually engineered rules and thus struggle to generalize and scale across heterogeneous test suites. In our previous work, we assessed the feasibility of using Small Language Models (SLMs) for test smell detection by evaluating GEMMA-3-4B, LLAMA-3.2-3B, and PHI-4-14B on test steps from 143 real-world Ubuntu test cases, covering seven smell types. PHI-4-14B achieved the best performance. In this article, we investigate whether a contemporary Large Language Model (GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW) available at the time of the study can identify test smells in natural language manual test cases using a prompt-based, whole-test-case analysis strategy. Unlike approaches that analyze individual test steps in isolation, our approach evaluates complete test cases, enabling the model to consider relationships and dependencies among test steps. We evaluate the approach on 100 Ubuntu test cases covering seven test smell types and compare its performance against previously evaluated SLMs, including GEMMA-3-4B, LLAMA-3.2-3B, and PHI-4-14B. Our results show that GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW outperforms the SLMs, while producing actionable explanations that can help practitioners revise manual test cases for greater clarity and consistency. We also find that test smells are pervasive in practice, with nearly one detected test smell per step on average, highlighting the need for scalable and automated quality support for manual testing artifacts.

</details>


### [181] [A Principled Framework for Safe Algorithm Updates in Automated Insulin Delivery Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13882)
*Thomas Screven,Ziqiang "Joe" Zhu,Deniz Cengiz,Rayhan A. Lal,Korey K. Hood,Samuel T. King*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个两阶段框架，用于分类AID算法中的bug（事实性、启发式、计算性）并评估软件更新的临床等价性。通过在Trio系统的Javascript与Swift实现上的验证，包括影子执行（736,480次调用）和多种仿真，证明更新满足临床等效阈值（>99%的配对血糖处于Parkes误差网格A/B区）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AID算法需要持续更新和修复bug，但在共适应系统中（用户已围绕现有算法行为调整设置），bug修复可能反而破坏血糖控制，而缺乏原则性的框架来评估AID算法更新的安全性。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 将bug分为事实性、启发式、计算性三类，各有不同管理策略；2) 通过影子执行（736,480次调用）和多种仿真（机制性计算机模拟、数据驱动重放仿真）评估临床等价性。

Result: 机制性计算机模拟中Swift与Javascript实现的时间范围（84.9% vs 84.9%）和血糖风险指数（23.5% vs 23.9%）几乎相同，>99%配对血糖在Parkes误差网格A/B区；影子执行中oref组件失配率极低（iob 0.43%, autosens 1.22%, determineBasal 0.07%, meal 0.01%），临床显著差异仅占0.03%。

Conclusion: 该框架将bug修复原则与多方法临床评估相结合，可评估AID算法更新安全性，适用于所有主流开源AID系统。

Abstract: Background: AID algorithms require ongoing software updates and bug fixes. In co-adapted systems, where users tune settings around existing algorithmic behavior, bug fixes can paradoxically disrupt glycemic control. No principled framework evaluates the safety of AID algorithm updates.
  Methods: Our two-part framework classifies bugs and evaluates the clinical equivalence of AID system software updates. Bugs are classified as factual, heuristic, or computational, each with distinct management strategies. Classifications were validated from porting Trio's oref algorithm from Javascript to a bug-fixed Swift implementation. We compared implementations using shadow execution on 736,480 invocations from eight Trio users. The second component assesses clinical equivalence with error analysis on paired glucose values, applied to both Trio implementations using mechanistic in silico and data-driven replay simulation.
  Results: In mechanistic in silico simulation, the Swift and Javascript implementations produced nearly identical Time in Range (84.9% vs. 84.9%) and Glycemia Risk Index (23.5% vs. 23.9%), with more than 99% of paired glucose in Parkes Error Grid Zones A and B, meeting our clinical equivalence threshold. Shadow execution showed low mismatch rates in oref components (iob 0.43%, autosens 1.22%, determineBasal 0.07%, meal 0.01%), with clinically meaningful differences in 0.03% of iob invocations. Data-driven replay simulations of bugs revealed more than 99% of downstream paired glucose in Parkes Error Grid Zones A and B, also meeting our clinical equivalence threshold.
  Conclusions: Our framework integrates bug-fixing principles with multi-method clinical evaluation to assess AID algorithm update safety. It is system-agnostic and applicable to all widely used OS-AID systems, with case studies highlighting the need for systematic remediation of factual and computational bugs.

</details>


### [182] [Bayesian-Calibrated Detection of Hallucinated Package Imports in AI-Assisted Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13918)
*Lom M. Hillah,Jean-Marc Richard,Ryan Hasnaoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种贝叶斯校准层，用于LLM代码中幻觉包导入检测器，将二元决策升级为基于贝塔后验概率的风险感知输出，并通过PyPI元数据通道发现注册但可疑的包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有slopsquat检测器只能输出二元决策（标记/不标记），缺乏概率校准能力，且无法识别注册但可疑的包（这是LLM代码攻击的真实场景）。

Method: 构建贝叶斯校准层，输出Beta后验概率；采用3类认知分类（经验校准、构造论证、工程判断追踪）；除200/404注册表通道外，增加PyPI元数据信号通道（包年龄、发布次数、作者描述、摘要）；使用McNemar配对检验和ECE/Brier分数评估。

Result: 校准层复现了严格注册检测结果，并在元数据通道上引入了良好校准的额外标记。对比基线为退化上界而非真正竞争对手。

Conclusion: 提出的校准层可直接用于下游CI门控，支持基于阈值的检测规则决策，实现了从二元检测到风险感知概率输出的升级。

Abstract: We present a Bayesian calibration layer for slopsquat detectors -- those that flag hallucinated package imports in code produced by large language models (LLMs). Where existing pipelines emit binary decisions (flag / do-not-flag), our layer emits a Beta-posterior probability per detection, derived from a 3-category epistemic taxonomy that explicitly classifies each prior as empirically calibrated, constructively argued, or engineering-judgement-traced. Beyond the primary 200/404 registry channel, the calibrated layer exploits PyPI metadata signals -- package age, release count, author descriptor, summary -- to surface registered-but-suspicious packages that a binary registry detector misses, which is the realistic post-LLM-emission attacker regime. The resulting risk-aware primitive is directly consumable by downstream CI gates and supports principled threshold decisions across detection rules. We evaluate the calibration on a merged corpus of 1,734 Python snippets -- a stratified 189-prompt BigCodeBench slice plus a 100-prompt niche-library stress-test set, generated across a six-model panel spanning four cloud models (Claude-Sonnet-4.6, Mistral-Large, DeepSeek-v4-pro, DeepSeek-R1) and two local open-weight code models (Mistral Codestral, Meta CodeLlama). Against a re-implemented binary baseline inspired by Mahmud et al. -- which shares its registry oracle with our ground truth and therefore serves as a degenerate upper bound rather than a genuine competitor -- the calibrated layer reproduces the strict-registry detections and introduces well-calibrated additional flags on the metadata channel. We assess detector asymmetry with a McNemar paired test and calibration with both a flagged-subset Expected Calibration Error and a strictly proper full-corpus Brier score.

</details>


### [183] [LLM Agents Can See Code Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14061)
*Dongjian Ma,Silin Chen,Yufei Yang,Yulin Shi,Yanfu yan,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究多模态大模型在仓库级代码问题修复中使用视觉表示的效果，发现纯视觉方案效果不佳，但将视觉仓库结构图作为文本的补充模态可节省最多26%的输入token并保持或提升修复准确率，视觉信息在故障定位和自主控制探索深度时最有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体几乎完全依赖文本形式处理代码仓库，忽略了人类开发者利用文件夹层次结构、依赖关系等视觉结构来定位和理解大型代码库的方式。随着多模态大模型的发展，探索视觉表示是否能为编码智能体带来益处成为一个待解决的关键问题。

Method: 对基于LLM的编码智能体在仓库级问题修复任务上，系统性地对比纯文本、纯视觉以及文本+视觉混合三种模态设置的效果。评估了四种最新的多模态模型，并分析视觉信息在不同任务阶段（如故障定位）和不同控制策略下的贡献。
