Result: 纯视觉方案因缺乏足够的符号细节导致准确率下降且token消耗增加；将视觉仓库结构图作为文本的补充模态，输入token消耗降低最多26%，同时问题修复准确率维持或提升；视觉信息在故障定位阶段以及智能体自主控制探索深度时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 提出了面向下一代编码智能体的实用混合文本-视觉设计范式，即视觉表示不应替代文本，而应作为补充模态以提升结构理解效率和降低计算成本。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models have demonstrated strong performance on software engineering tasks. Yet most agents consume repositories almost entirely as text, which differs from how human developers use visual structure such as folder hierarchies and dependency relationships to orient themselves in large codebases. With multimodal large language models (MLLMs), it is an open question whether agents can effectively benefit from visual representations of repositories. This paper presents the first systematic empirical study of visual repository representations for LLM-based agents on repository-level issue resolution. We evaluate four recent multimodal models. Our results show that a strictly vision-only setup degrades accuracy and increases token cost, because agents lack sufficient symbolic detail and compensate with repeated visual queries. In contrast, integrating visual graphs of repository structure as a supplementary modality alongside standard text interfaces helps agents understand structure more efficiently: input token consumption decreases by up to 26% while issue-resolution accuracy is maintained or improved. Visualization is most useful during fault localization and when the agent autonomously controls exploration depth. These findings point to a practical hybrid text-and-vision design for next-generation coding agents.

</details>


### [184] [FastContext: Training Efficient Repository Explorer for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14066)
*Shaoqiu Zhang,Maoquan Wang,Yuling Shi,Yuhang Wang,Xiaodong Gu,Yongqiang Yao,Rao Fu,Shengyu Fu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FastContext是一个专用的探索子代理，将仓库探索与任务解决分离，通过并行工具调用和专门的探索模型（4B-30B参数）提供精准的文件路径和行范围作为上下文，在多个基准测试上提升代码解决率最高5.5%，同时减少解码代理高达60%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理在软件工程任务中面临仓库探索瓶颈：定位相关代码消耗大量token预算，且无关片段污染代理上下文；现有方法中同一模型既负责探索又负责解决，导致探索读写留在求解器历史中，效率低下。

Method: 提出FastContext专用探索子代理，与求解代理分离；按需调用时发出并行工具调用，返回简洁的文件路径和行范围；使用4B-30B参数的专业探索模型，从强参考模型轨迹引导训练，并用任务级奖励优化首次搜索、多轮证据收集和精确引用生成。

Result: 在SWE-bench Multilingual、SWE-bench Pro和SWE-QA上，集成FastContext至Mini-SWE-Agent后，端到端解决率提升最高5.5%，编码代理token消耗减少高达60%，额外开销极小。

Conclusion: 仓库探索可以与任务解决分离，并由专门的模型高效处理，验证了分离式探索架构的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) coding agents have achieved strong results on software engineering tasks, yet repository exploration remains a major bottleneck: locating relevant code consumes substantial token budget and pollutes the agent's context with irrelevant snippets. In most agents, the same model explores the repository and solves the task, leaving exploratory reads and searches in the solver's history. We present FastContext, a dedicated exploration subagent that separates repository exploration from solving. Invoked on demand, FastContext issues parallel tool calls and returns concise file paths and line ranges as focused context. FastContext is powered by specialized exploration models spanning 4B--30B parameters. We bootstrap them from strong reference-model trajectories and refine them with task-grounded rewards for broad first-turn search, multi-turn evidence gathering, and precise citation generation. Across SWE-bench Multilingual, SWE-bench Pro, and SWE-QA, integrating FastContext into Mini-SWE-Agent improves end-to-end resolution rates up to 5.5\% while reducing coding-agent token consumption up to 60\%, with marginal overhead. These results show that repository exploration can be separated from solving and handled effectively by specialized models. Code and data: https://github.com/microsoft/fastcontext

</details>


### [185] [Simulating Students' Java Programming Errors with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14113)
*Ali Keramati,Jie Cao,Iman Mohammadi,Mark Warschauer,Yang Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探索了使用大语言模型（LLMs）作为学生代理，通过三种提示策略（IO、CoT、Self-Refine）生成编程逻辑错误。基于CodeWorkout数据集（37个问题、7.4万+ Java提交），从多样性和与真实学生错误的对齐度两个维度评估了五种LLM。结果表明，Claude Sonnet 4表现最均衡；合成错误与真实错误在功能上无法区分；高难度任务产生更多样但更不像学生的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取新编程任务的学生错误集成本高、周期长，需要大量课堂部署后才能积累真实提交；因此希望利用LLM作为可扩展的代理来模拟学生逻辑错误，加速教育数据收集。

Method: 使用CodeWorkout数据集（37道编程题，74,000+份学生Java提交），在五种LLM上采用三种提示策略（Input-Output、Chain-of-Thought、Self-Refine），从多样性（不同错误模式的覆盖范围）和对齐度（与真实学生错误的匹配程度）两个维度进行评估，并分析错误随任务困难程度的变化。

Result: 所有模型都能生成多样的错误，但对齐度存在差异，Claude Sonnet 4表现最均衡；盲审专家标注（N=401）表明合成错误与真实错误功能上不可区分；高难度任务产生更多样但与学生错误相似度更低的错误。

Conclusion: 揭示了用LLM模拟人类学习者的权衡，为将合成错误集成到可教学代理、智能辅导系统和学习分析中提供了设计考量。

Abstract: Understanding student errors in the programming is a cornerstone of programming education, yet obtaining a representative set of student errors for any newly designed task remains slow and costly, since authentic submissions only accumulate after extensive classroom deployment. This paper explores whether large language models (LLMs) can serve as scalable proxies for students by simulating realistic logical errors in code submissions. Using the CodeWorkout dataset of 74,000+ unique student Java submissions across 37 problems, we evaluate five LLMs under three mainstream prompting strategies: Input-Output (IO), Chain-of-Thought (CoT), and iterative Self-Refine. We assess performance along two key dimensions: diversity (the range of distinct error patterns) and alignment (alignment with authentic student mistakes), and examine how these vary by struggling level of programming tasks. Our quantitative findings reveal that while all models generate diverse errors, their alignment to human submissions diverges: Claude Sonnet 4 achieves the most balanced performance. In addition, we conducted a blinded expert annotation study (N = 401) comparing synthetic and authentic errors. This qualitative analysis confirms that the generated errors are functionally indistinguishable from authentic student errors. Moreover, higher-struggling-level problems elicit more diverse but less student-like errors. These results highlight trade-offs in using LLMs to simulate human learners and suggest design considerations for integrating synthetic errors into teachable agents, intelligent tutoring systems, and large-scale learning analytics.

</details>


### [186] [tap: A File-Based Protocol for Heterogeneous LLM Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14445)
*Minseo Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 tap，一种基于文件的协作协议，允许不同供应商的 LLM 代理（如 Claude 和 Codex）在无需共享内存或相同运行时的条件下，通过文件优先设计（markdown 消息持久化 + 文件检查路径 + 实时通知路径 + git worktree 隔离）协作开发同一代码库。在 27 天自我应用中收集了 717 个操作工件，发现异质模型对审查缺陷率达 69.8%，高于同质模型对的 53.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体软件开发系统通常假设共享运行时、统一 API 或中央对话服务器，限制了不同供应商的 LLM 代理直接协作。本文旨在破除这些假设，实现跨供应商、跨执行环境的多 LLM 代理协作。

Method: tap 协议核心为文件优先设计：1) 使用 markdown 文件保存元数据作为原始消息，确保持久化；2) 分层通信架构——Tier 1 文件检查路径（基于文件的异步通信）和 Tier 2 实时通知路径（支持 Claude 和 Codex 的实时通知）；3) 通过分离的 git worktree 隔离各代理工作空间。

Result: 在 27 天自我应用中收集 209 个 pull request 和 717 个操作工件。375 个审查工件的分析显示：异质模型对（Claude+Codex）审查缺陷发现率 69.8%，同质模型对 53.1%。

Conclusion: tap 协议在真实生产仓库中成功运作，结合文件消息持久化与实时通知；异质模型与执行环境的组合能拓展审查视角，提升缺陷发现率。协议已开源为 npm 包 @hua-labs/tap v0.5.2。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent software development systems have proposed many forms of agent collaboration, including role-based collaboration and automated code review. However, many systems assume a common runtime, a central conversation server, or the same API family. Under these assumptions, LLM agents from different vendors cannot easily exchange messages directly from their own execution environments while dividing development and review work on a shared codebase. This paper presents tap, a file-based collaboration protocol that allows Claude (Anthropic) and Codex (OpenAI) to collaborate on one codebase without shared memory or an identical runtime. The core of tap is a file-first design that preserves markdown files with metadata as original messages, combines a file inspection path (file communication, Tier 1) with real-time notification paths for Claude and Codex (real-time communication, Tier 2), and isolates work through separate git worktrees. Even if real-time notification fails or a receiver restarts, the message file remains available and the same content can be inspected again. In a 27-day, 37-generation self-applied operation where tap was used to develop and review itself, we collected 209 tap-related pull requests and 717 operational artifacts. An analysis of 375 review artifacts showed that the share of reviews recording at least one defect or requested change was 69.8% for heterogeneous model pairs and 53.1% for homogeneous model pairs. These results show that tap, which combines file-based message preservation with real-time notification, operates in a real production repository, and that combining heterogeneous models and execution environments can broaden review perspectives. tap is distributed as the open-source npm package @hua-labs/tap (v0.5.2).

</details>


### [187] [Investigating Metamorphic Fuzz Oracle Enhancement via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14164)
*Ruixiang Qian,Ding Yang,Zengxu Chen,Yuxuan Gao,Chunrong Fang,Chao Zhang,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaFOE框架，利用大语言模型自动为灰盒模糊测试生成蜕变关系驱动的测试预言增强，在OSS-Fuzz驱动上实现18.7%的边覆盖提升和1,528个独特崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊测试驱动通常依赖基于崩溃的测试预言，忽视库的功能正确性，导致漏洞检测能力受限。手动构建和整合蜕变关系需要大量领域知识，因此需要自动化方法。

Method: 提出MetaFOE框架，基于LLM自动生成蜕变关系并将其集成到现有模糊测试驱动中，形成蜕变驱动的测试预言增强。

Result: 在OSS-Fuzz驱动上评估，使用三种现代LLM和五种提示策略，生成3,475个MR（77.3%可应用），实现12,351个元驱动（6,228个有效）。3小时模糊测试后，有效元驱动使边覆盖平均提升18.7%，触发1,528个独特崩溃。

Conclusion: 蜕变驱动的测试预言增强是有效的，使用LLM自动化MFOE是可行的，为推进灰盒模糊测试提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Fuzz drivers are essential components of greybox fuzzing, as they encapsulate target interfaces, define test spaces, and largely determine fuzzing effectiveness. Existing fuzz drivers typically rely on crash-based oracles for security testing, overlooking library functionality and limiting bug detection capability.
  In this paper, we present the first study on metamorphic-based fuzz oracle enhancement (MFOE), which augments existing fuzz drivers with metamorphic-based oracles derived from metamorphic relations (MRs). Since constructing and integrating such oracles requires substantial domain knowledge, automating MFOE is challenging. To address this challenge, we propose MetaFOE, an LLM-based framework that automatically generates and integrates metamorphic-based oracles.
  We evaluate MetaFOE on OSS-Fuzz drivers using three modern LLMs and five prompt strategies. MetaFOE generates 3,475 MRs, of which 77.3% are applicable, and implements 12,351 meta drivers, with 6,228 being valid. After three hours of fuzzing, the valid meta drivers improve edge coverage by an average of 18.7% and trigger 1,528 unique crashes. Our results demonstrate both the effectiveness of metamorphic-based oracle enhancement and the feasibility of using LLMs to automate MFOE, providing valuable insights for advancing greybox fuzzing.

</details>


### [188] [Evaluating LLMs for Obfuscation Detection and Classification in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14233)
*Luca Ferrari,Marco Alecci,Jordan Samhi,Tegawende' F. Bissyande',Jacques Klein,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究，评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）在无需手工规则、预定义签名或专门训练的情况下，通过语义推理检测Android应用代码混淆的能力，并与现有静态分析工具进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android应用开发者越来越多地使用代码混淆技术来阻碍逆向工程和保护知识产权，但混淆也降低了静态分析和漏洞检测工具的有效性。现有检测方法依赖手工启发式规则或特定任务学习管道，难以泛化到不断演化的混淆策略，因此需要探索更通用的检测方法。

Method: 在包含多种混淆技术的受控基准数据集和从Google Play收集的真实世界Android应用数据集上，评估了多种开源和闭源LLMs在零样本设置下检测混淆代码的能力。研究了提示设计、模型选择、决策阈值等因素的影响，并与基于SAST的混淆检测方法进行了比较。

Result: LLMs能够在无需手工规则或专门训练的情况下识别混淆代码；提示设计、模型选择和决策阈值对检测性能有显著影响；基于LLM的语义推理方法与现有SAST方法相比具有互补优势。

Conclusion: LLMs在Android安全分析中具有潜力，特别是通过语义推理检测混淆的能力，但其应用存在局限性，未来需要进一步探索将LLMs集成到安全分析流程中的有效方式。

Abstract: Android applications (apps) developers increasingly rely on code obfuscation techniques to hinder reverse engineering and protect intellectual property. However, obfuscation also reduces the effectiveness of static analysis and vulnerability detection tools, creating challenges for Android security analysis. Existing approaches for detecting obfuscation in Android apps predominantly rely on handcrafted heuristics, engineered features, or task-specific learning pipelines, which may struggle to generalize across evolving obfuscation strategies.
  This paper presents a large-scale empirical study investigating the capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to detect obfuscation in Android apps through semantic reasoning. Our study evaluates whether off-the-shelf LLMs can identify obfuscated code without relying on handcrafted rules, predefined signatures, or dedicated model training. The empirical evaluation is conducted on both a controlled benchmark containing an app obfuscated with multiple techniques and a real-world dataset of Android apps collected from Google Play. The study further examines the impact of prompt design, model selection, and decision thresholds across several open-weight and proprietary LLMs. Finally, the analysis compares LLM-based reasoning with existing SAST-based obfuscation-detection approaches and discusses the broader implications and limitations of applying LLMs to Android security analysis.

</details>


### [189] [No Accidental Software Agent First Canonical Code for Human Code Entropy Reduction and 30 to 500 times Lower Frontier Model Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14357)
*Jepson Taylor*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体优先的规范代码"（agent-first canonical code）概念，这是一种承载证明的代码基板，旨在通过行为等价商化将日常产品软件重写为规范行为剖面，并通过类型化变更代数、证明通道、约束编辑文法、语义补丁单元、运行时负记忆和承载证明的变更对象，实现验证正确变更的分摊成本降低。初步QLoRA实验显示64,088条规范轨迹可学习并抑制禁用语言标记，但未建立行为保持或规模经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码模型可能花费大量容量学习人类代码仓库中的偶然熵（如框架更替、命名漂移、CI方言等），而非仅学习程序行为。这些偶然熵与有价值的信号（测试、事故、迁移、边界案例等）纠缠在一起，造成资源浪费。

Method: 提出智能体优先的规范代码框架，核心机制是通过行为等价商化（quotienting software by behavior equivalence），在声明预言（declared oracle）下将等价编码坍缩为受治理的代表，附带显式证据和证明义务。具体包括规范行为剖面、类型化变更代数、证明通道、约束编辑文法、语义补丁单元、运行时负记忆、承载证明的变更对象等组件。

Result: 提出"无事故地平线"（No-Accident Horizon）概念作为规划目标，预估日常产品软件可达成约100倍的全成本降低（是假设性估算而非实测结果）。QLoRA实验显示Qwen2.5-Coder-14B上64,088条规范轨迹可学习并抑制测试的禁用语言标记，但未验证行为保持、规模经济或验证变更成本。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于提出一个可证伪的程序（falsifiable program），围绕最小功能描述长度和验证变更成本构建。核心论点是，通过行为等价商化可显著降低验证正确变更的摊还成本，但实验仅初步验证了可学习性，尚未建立完整的有效性证据。

Abstract: Frontier coding models may spend substantial capacity learning not only program behavior, but also accidental entropy in human repositories. Such repositories contain valuable signals: tests, incidents, migrations, edge cases, product judgment, and operational history. These signals are entangled with framework churn, naming drift, generated-source ambiguity, dependency rituals, CI dialects, weak proof routes, and human-oriented review customs. We propose agent-first canonical code, a proof-carrying substrate that rewrites routine product software into canonical behavior profiles, typed change algebra, proof lanes, constrained edit grammars, semantic patch cells, runtime negative memory, and proof-carrying change objects.
  The core hypothesis is that quotienting software by behavior equivalence under a declared oracle can collapse equivalent encodings into governed representatives with explicit evidence and proof obligations. The endpoint is amortized cost per verified correct change, including source, context, reasoning, tools, verification, security, provenance, review, failed loops, defects, and foundry cost under a common oracle. Reported reduction bands are hypotheses, not measured frontier results. The proposed limit is a No-Accident Horizon: removable accident decreases until residual novelty, evidence, governance, risk, and future optionality dominate. For supported routine-product distributions, this gives a defensible planning target near 100-fold all-in cost reduction, not a guarantee for all software. Preliminary QLoRA experiments on Qwen2.5-Coder-14B show that 64,088 canonical trajectories are learnable and suppress tested forbidden-language markers, but do not establish behavior preservation, scaling economics, or verified-change cost. The contribution is a falsifiable program centered on minimum functional description length and verified-change cost.

</details>


### [190] [Security Threats and Their Impact on Blockchain Interoperability: Identification and Countermeasures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14554)
*Shawn M. Reynolds,Hassan Reza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统识别并分类了区块链互操作性面临的安全威胁，将其分为五大类（核心区块链攻击、网络攻击、互操作性特定攻击、社会工程学和代码漏洞），并针对每类威胁提出了相应的防御策略，为设计安全的区块链互操作方案提供了结构化的分类基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链互操作性系统缺乏全面的安全机制，攻击者已利用这些漏洞造成了数亿美元的资产损失，亟需系统性的威胁识别与防御方案。

Method: 采用系统化的方法对互操作性区块链系统中的安全威胁进行识别和分类，将威胁组织为五个类别，并对每类威胁分析其攻击面并提出防御策略。

Result: 形成了一套包含五类安全威胁的结构化分类体系，并为每类威胁提供了相应的攻击面分析和防御对策。

Conclusion: 该分类法为设计和评估安全的区块链互操作性解决方案提供了结构化基础。

Abstract: Blockchain interoperability enables independent blockchain systems to communicate and exchange assets across heterogeneous networks. However, the lack of comprehensive security mechanisms remains a critical weakness -- one that attackers have already exploited to cause hundreds of millions of dollars in asset losses. This paper presents a systematic identification and classification of security threats facing interoperable blockchain systems, along with corresponding countermeasures for each. We organize threats into five categories: (1) core blockchain attacks, (2) network attacks, (3) interoperability-specific attacks, (4) social engineering, and (5) code vulnerabilities, with particular attention to smart contract weaknesses. For each identified threat, we analyze its attack surface and propose effective defensive strategies. The resulting taxonomy provides a structured foundation for designing and evaluating secure blockchain interoperability solutions.

</details>


### [191] [When Errors Become Narratives: A Longitudinal Taxonomy of Silent Failures in a Production LLM Agent Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14589)
*Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对持续运行的LLM个人助手代理系统进行八周纵向研究，记录了22起静默故障事件。核心发现是"故障-可信"模式——系统不仅未报告错误，LLM反而将其转化为流畅、可信的叙述输出给用户。约70%的静默故障由用户视角观察发现，审计对事前预防无效但可阻止87%的回归。提出五类机制导向的故障分类法，并推导出防御框架与设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理系统越来越多地作为长期运行的自主运行时存在，但静默故障（错误信号无法以可操作形式到达人类）在文献和实践中缺乏系统研究。作者基于真实生产环境连续运行的系统，填补这一实证空白。

Method: 对自2026年3月起持续生产运行的个人助手代理系统进行八周长时段观测，记录并事后剖析22起事件，从中识别出至少28次静默故障实例，进而提炼出五类机制导向的分类法。

Result: (1) 约70%静默故障由人类用户视角观察发现，而非测试或审计；(2) 回顾性审计对15起事件的预防率为0%，但回归阻断率达87%；(3) 故障潜伏期（13小时至60天）与故障机制相关，而非代码复杂度——最长寿的故障存在于组件间的缝隙处（无测试覆盖的区域）。

Conclusion: 提出"故障-可信"概念（LLM将故障转化为流畅叙事），建立五类分类法，并基于实证总结防御框架与设计原则，使代理系统的故障变得响亮、可归因且乏味。

Abstract: LLM agent systems increasingly run as long-lived autonomous runtimes: scheduling jobs, calling tools, maintaining memory, and pushing results to humans. We present a longitudinal study of silent failures in one such system: a personal-assistant agent runtime in continuous production since March 2026, with roughly 40 scheduled jobs, 8 LLM providers, a tool-governance proxy, and a knowledge-base memory plane, defended by 4,286 unit tests and 827 governance checks. Over eight weeks we documented 22 incidents with full root-cause postmortems, in which one meta-pattern -- a failure whose error signal never reaches a human in actionable form -- manifested at least 28 times. We derive a five-class, mechanism-oriented taxonomy: (A) environment and platform quirks, (B) design-assumption mismatches, (C) error swallowing and dilution, (D) chained hallucination and fabrication, (E) operational omission and forensic blind spots. Class D is unique to LLM systems and the most dangerous: the system does not merely fail to report an error -- the LLM transforms it into fluent, plausible narrative delivered to the user. We term this fail-plausible: gray failure's differential observability escalated -- the observer is not just blind, it is convincingly lied to by the failure itself. Three findings: about 70% of silent failures were caught by human user-view observation, not tests or audits; a retrospective audit of 15 incidents found 0% ex-ante prevention but 87% regression blocking -- audits are regression engines, not prediction engines; incident latency (13 hours to 60 days) tracks failure mechanism, not code complexity -- the longest-lived failures lived in the seams between components, where no test runs. We describe the resulting defense framework and distill design principles for agent systems whose failures are loud, attributable, and boring. All postmortems and artifacts are public.

</details>


### [192] [Regulating the Machine Contributor: Governance and Policy Alignment in Open Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14594)
*Jassem Manita,Aziz Amari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本论文分析了开源社区在应对AI自主/半自主贡献者时面临的政策缺口，通过对六个组织的比较研究，提出了六维分类法、政策成熟度评分，并识别了与现有AI治理框架之间的重叠空白，最后描绘了分层协调框架的轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源软件贡献流程基于人类参与者的法律和道德假设（贡献者协议、行为准则、审查规范），而自主和半自主AI贡献者正在挑战这些假设。2025-2026年间AI代理引发的事件、AI生成的噪音量以及平台级关闭事件表明，这一缺口已具有操作层面的严重后果。多个开源组织已制定响应政策，但结果碎片化，且与新兴AI治理框架（EU AI Act、NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC标准）的映射关系尚未在贡献层面建立。

Method: 采用最相似系统设计（Most-Similar Systems Design），对六个组织（SymPy、LLVM、matplotlib、OpenInfra、Apache软件基金会、Linux基金会）的政策进行比较分析，使用基于指标的编码和过程追踪方法（针对SymPy和LLVM）。由此导出六维分类法、序数政策成熟度评分，并将已记录的AI代理事件映射到各政策未能治理的维度上。

Result: 提出了六维分类法（披露、责任、人工监督、许可、执行、维护者工作量）、政策成熟度评分，以及将AI代理事件映射到政策空白。发现当前政策与EU AI Act、NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001和23894等治理框架之间存在重叠空白，双方均未覆盖。

Conclusion: 需要构建一个协调的分层框架，并开展实证评估以校准该框架，从而弥合开源贡献政策与AI治理框架之间的现有空白。

Abstract: AI-assisted software development has moved from line-level autocomplete to agents that can plan changes, edit files, and submit pull requests with limited human supervision. Open-source software, however, evolves through a process designed for humans: contributor agreements, codes of conduct, and review norms all assume a legally accountable person who can attest to provenance and answer reviewer questions. Autonomous and semi-autonomous AI contributors strain those assumptions, and the 2025-2026 record of agent-driven incidents, AI-generated nuisance volume, and platform-level shutdowns shows that the gap is operationally consequential. Several open-source organisations have responded with contribution policies, but the result is fragmented, and its alignment with emerging AI governance frameworks (EU AI Act, NIST AI RMF with the UC Berkeley Agentic AI Profile, ISO/IEC 42001 and 23894) is unmapped at the contribution level. We compare policies across six organisations (SymPy, LLVM, matplotlib, OpenInfra, the Apache Software Foundation, and the Linux Foundation) using Most-Similar Systems Design with indicator-based coding and process tracing for SymPy and LLVM. From this we derive a six-dimensional taxonomy (disclosure, responsibility, human oversight, licensing, enforcement, maintainer workload), an ordinal Policy Maturity Score, and a mapping of documented agent incidents onto the dimensions each policy fails to govern. Aligning the dimensions with the regulatory frameworks above identifies overlapping gaps neither side currently closes, and we close by sketching the shape of a harmonised tiered framework and the empirical evaluation needed to calibrate it.

</details>


### [193] [Empowering Student Debugging in Parallel Programming with Execution Traces and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14607)
*God'salvation F. Oguibe,Vinodh Kumaran Jayakumar,Tongping Liu,Andrew Lan,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ParaView是一个教育工具，通过执行记录和可视化帮助学生学习C/C++并发编程中的调试和实现，17名学生参与实验表明调试和实现成功率显著提升。同时探索了LLM辅助分析并发bug，虽能有效识别但修复正确性因复杂度而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发编程是计算机科学课程的核心组成部分，但由于其固有复杂性和死锁、竞态条件等并发bug的不确定性，学生难以掌握。现有教学工具有限，需要更有效的工具帮助学生理解和调试并发问题。

Method: 开发ParaView工具，提供透明的执行记录和可视化功能，使并行执行过程可观察和可理解。通过17名学生的调试和实现任务实验评估工具效果，并探索使用LLM分析并发bug及建议修复。

Result: 与以往课程相比，学生的调试和实现成功率显著提升。学生调查显示大多数参与者认为ParaView有帮助。LLM在识别bug和解释执行轨迹方面非常有效，但在复杂同步模式下的bug修复正确性存在差异。

Conclusion: 记录-可视化工具（如ParaView）结合人工智能（AI）辅助能够改善并发编程的教学与学习效果。

Abstract: Concurrent programming is a core component of Computer Science curricula, yet remains notoriously difficult for students to master due to its inherent complexity and the nondeterministic nature of concurrency bugs such as deadlocks and race conditions. In this work, we present ParaView, an educational tool designed to help students understand, debug, and correct concurrency issues in parallel programs written in C/C++. ParaView provides transparent execution recording and visualization to make parallel execution observable and comprehensible. We evaluated ParaView through a series of debugging and implementation tasks, with 17 students participating. Results showed a significant improvement in debugging and implementation successes compared to previous course iterations. A student survey confirmed that most participants found ParaView helpful. To further support learning outside the classroom, we explored using Large Language Models (LLMs) to analyze concurrency bugs and suggest fixes. While LLMs were highly effective in identifying bugs and explaining execution traces, the correctness of their bug fixes varied, especially for more complex synchronization patterns. Our findings suggest that recording-visualization tools like ParaView, complemented by artificial intelligence (AI), can improve teaching and learning of concurrent programming.

</details>


### [194] [GitHub Template Repositories: Served Domains, Maintenance, and Practitioner Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14616)
*Leuson Da Silva,Altaf Allah Abbassi,Imen Trabelsi,Paulo Borba,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对GitHub模板仓库进行了大规模实证研究，涵盖五种主流编程语言，分析了模板的领域分布、维护特征、质量问题，并提出了模板设计与管理的实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GitHub自2019年便提供了模板仓库功能用于代码复用，但学界对其支持的领域、维护特性以及有效模板设计的实践指南知之甚少，因此有必要进行系统性研究以填补这一空白。

Method: 首先通过挖掘和分类模板仓库分析其服务领域（采用LLM-as-a-judge策略）；然后通过统计分析评估仓库特征、活动与质量问题（代码异味、漏洞、安全热点）之间的关联；最后对代表性模板子集进行定性分析，提炼模板设计与管理的最佳实践和常见陷阱。

Result: Web开发是所有生态系统中主导领域；维护与质量问题因编程语言而异；高质量的模板倾向于采用成熟的软件工程实践，并提供全面的文档和清晰的使用指南。

Conclusion: 研究结果为从业者设计和采用高质量模板仓库提供了经验性见解和可操作的指导建议。

Abstract: Over time, GitHub has introduced different strategies for sharing reusable code artifacts. In addition to fork-based reuse, template repositories provide a distinct feature for generating new projects from scaffolding. Although this feature has been available since 2019, little is known about the domains it supports, its maintenance characteristics, or the practices that guide practitioners for effective template design. To address this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study of GitHub template repositories across the five most used programming languages. First, we mine and categorize templates to analyze the domains they serve, exploring the LLM-as-a-judge strategy. Next, we explore the reliability of templates by evaluating the associations between repository characteristics and activity, and quality-related issues (e.g., code smells, vulnerabilities, and security hotspots) through statistical analysis. Finally, we qualitatively analyze a representative subset of templates to derive practical guidelines and recurring pitfalls for template design and management. Our results show that Web Development is the predominant domain across ecosystems, while maintenance and quality issues vary by programming language. We further find that high-quality templates tend to adopt established software engineering practices, while providing comprehensive documentation and clear guidance for use. Overall, our findings offer empirical insights and actionable guidance to support practitioners in designing and adopting high-quality template repositories.

</details>


### [195] [Teaching Machine Learning to Software Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14618)
*Nafiseh Kahani,Jason Jaskolka*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对本科软件工程课程中缺乏AI/ML内容系统教学的问题，定义了一个AI/ML相关主题清单，映射到现有课程中识别覆盖缺口，通过调查教师确定优先级，最终提出将高优先级主题嵌入现有SE课程的指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML组件越来越多地嵌入软件产品，但本科SE课程缺乏对构建、测试、部署和维护AI/ML系统的系统准备。

Method: 1) 编译定义AI/ML相关主题清单；2) 映射到代表性SE课程中识别覆盖缺口；3) 调查SE教师评估重要性和整合约束；4) 基于上述交叉分析推导嵌入指南。

Result: 确定了AI/ML相关主题清单、现有课程覆盖缺口、教师对主题重要性和整合约束的评估，以及高优先级主题的嵌入指南。

Conclusion: 提出了将高优先级AI/ML主题嵌入现有SE课程的循证指南，以弥补课程内容与行业实践之间的差距。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) components are increasingly embedded in software products, yet undergraduate software engineering (SE) curricula rarely provide systematic preparation for building, testing, deploying, and maintaining AI/ML-based software systems. This paper aims to provide evidence-based guidance for integrating AI/MLrelevant content into core SE education. We compile and define a structured inventory of topics relevant to SE practice in AI/MLbased software, then map these topics against required courses in a set of representative SE curricula to identify coverage gaps. To assess educational priorities and feasibility, we survey SE instructors on topic importance and integration constraints. Based on the crosswalk between topic definitions, curriculum coverage, and instructor prioritization, we derive a guideline that recommends where and how high-priority topics can be embedded within existing SE courses.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [An Agentic Retrieval Framework for Autonomous Context-Aware Data Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13692)
*Hadi Fadlallah,Ibrahim Dhaini,Fatima Mubarak,Rima Kilany*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于智能体检索的统一框架，通过多智能体工作流实现自主、上下文感知的数据质量评估，并引入可行性验证机制确保生成的评估规则可执行、可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据质量评估依赖上下文，现有静态规则或人工评估方法难以适应多样化场景且缺乏自动化能力；大语言模型虽有潜力但存在可靠性、可溯源性和执行安全性问题。

Method: 构建统一的智能体-检索框架，包括：解释自然语言描述的数据使用意图、推导上下文感知的评估策略、通过多智能体工作流生成可执行验证逻辑；引入可行性验证阶段评估生成规范的现实性与可执行性，迭代优化后确定性执行。

Result: 在多个使用场景下对同一数据集进行评估，结果表明评估结果能根据不同使用意图自适应调整，可行性门控执行有效减少了不现实或不可执行的规则生成。

Conclusion: 该框架为现代数据驱动环境中部署自主且可控的数据质量评估提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Data quality assessment is a critical prerequisite for effective data analytics and data-driven decision-making, yet it remains a challenging task due to the inherently context-dependent nature of data quality. Existing approaches often rely on static rules or manual assessment strategies, limiting their adaptability to diverse usage scenarios and constraining automation at scale. Recent advances in artificial intelligence, particularly large language models, offer new opportunities for automating data quality assessment, but raise concerns related to reliability, grounding, and execution safety.
  In this paper, we propose a unified agentic-retrieval framework for autonomous context-aware data quality assessment. The framework interprets natural-language descriptions of intended data usage, derives context-aware assessment strategies, and generates executable validation logic through a multi-agent workflow. To ensure operational reliability, the framework introduces a feasibility validation stage that evaluates the realism and executability of generated assessment specifications before execution, enabling iterative refinement when necessary. Accepted validation logic is executed deterministically to guarantee reproducible and auditable results.
  We implement the proposed framework as an end-to-end prototype and evaluate it across multiple usage scenarios applied to the same dataset. The results demonstrate that assessment outcomes adapt meaningfully to different intended uses, while feasibility-gated execution reduces unrealistic or non-executable rule generation. The proposed approach provides a practical foundation for deploying autonomous yet controlled data quality assessment in modern data-driven environments.

</details>


### [197] [A Multimodal Machine Learning Framework for Enterprise Database Workload-Aware Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13699)
*Ruchi Pakhle,Siddhant Pawar*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多模态机器学习框架，通过结合工作负载特征、系统遥测数据和操作信号，对企业数据库事件进行根因分析。实验表明，LightGBM在工作负载分类中表现最佳，结合多模态数据能为自动化和可解释的根因分析提供实用基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据库事件的根因分析目前依赖人工检查日志、性能指标和工作负载行为，过程耗时且容易出错。现有方法通常只使用单一证据来源，无法全面捕捉CPU饱和、I/O瓶颈、锁竞争、死锁和慢查询等事件背后的操作上下文。

Method: 提出了一个多模态机器学习框架，融合工作负载特征、系统遥测数据以及来自计算、存储和加速器数据集的操作信号。通过工程化的工作负载感知特征对工作负载行为进行分类，并支持下游根因诊断。评估了随机森林、LightGBM和前馈神经网络三种模型。

Result: 工作负载感知的特征工程提升了工作负载的可分离性，LightGBM在预测性能和可解释性之间取得了最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 将多模态遥测数据与工作负载上下文相结合，可以为自动化和可解释的根因分析系统提供实用基础。

Abstract: Root cause analysis for enterprise database incidents is often a manual and time consuming process that requires operators to inspect logs, performance metrics, and workload behavior. Existing approaches commonly focus on a single source of evidence, which limits their ability to capture the broader operational context behind incidents such as CPU saturation, I/O bottlenecks, lock contention, deadlocks, and slow query execution.
  This paper presents a multimodal machine learning framework for workload-aware root cause analysis in enterprise database environments. The proposed approach combines workload characteristics, system telemetry, and operational signals from compute, storage, and accelerator oriented datasets. Engineered workload aware features are used to classify workload behavior and support downstream diagnosis of likely incident causes.
  The framework evaluates Random Forest, LightGBM, and feedforward neural network models for workload classification and root cause analysis support. Experimental results show that workload aware feature engineering improves workload separability, with LightGBM providing the strongest balance of predictive performance and interpretability. The results suggest that combining multimodal telemetry with workload context can provide a practical foundation for automated and explainable root cause analysis systems.

</details>


### [198] [Towards an open registry of Earth observation instruments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13923)
*David Montero,César Aybar,Miguel D. Mahecha,Luis Gómez-Chova*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了Awesome Earth Observation Instruments，一个开源、标准化、社区驱动的地球观测仪器注册目录，提供机器可读的元数据，解决现有数据目录缺乏仪器级元数据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管地球观测仪器数量快速增长，用户缺乏描述其存在和关键特征的单一可靠来源。现有数据目录主要描述数据产品，而非提供关于产生这些数据的仪器的持久、策展元数据。

Method: 在GitHub上托管开源目录，采用通用模式（轻量级核心+模块化扩展涵盖光谱、几何和数据访问元数据），通过自动化模式验证和人工审查确保质量，支持社区贡献，并计划提供API以支持程序化访问和互操作性。

Result: 建立了一个开放、可版本化、可扩展的仪器元数据注册目录，支持多种地球观测仪器（卫星、机载、地面/原位系统），能够随着新仪器和元数据需求的出现而持续演进。

Conclusion: 该目录通过提供标准化的仪器元数据，促进EO数据的发现、解释和分析，提升仪器特性层面的互操作性，并计划通过API进一步集成到常见EO分析环境中。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) is essential to understanding the Earth system, enabling the transformation of planetary properties into measurable variables that can be analysed, compared, and modelled. In recent decades, EO capabilities have grown rapidly, accompanied by an even faster expansion in the number and variety of available EO instruments. Today, EO includes instruments deployed on satellites, airborne platforms, and terrestrial or in-situ systems. However, despite this proliferation of instruments, users often lack a single, reliable source describing their existence and key characteristics. Although existing data catalogues have substantially improved dataset discovery, they primarily describe data products rather than providing persistent, curated metadata about the instruments that produced them. Here we present Awesome Earth Observation Instruments, an open, standardized, and community-oriented registry providing machine-readable metadata for EO instruments. The catalogue is hosted on GitHub and allows contributors to submit instrument metadata following a common schema. The schema combines a lightweight core with modular extensions covering spectral, geometric, and data access-related metadata, enabling both standardization and flexibility across diverse EO systems. All submissions undergo automated schema validation and human review. Because the schema is open, versioned, and extensible, the catalogue can continuously evolve as new instruments and metadata requirements emerge. This facilitates the discovery, interpretation, and analysis of EO data in light of instrument characteristics. To support programmatic access and interoperability, we further envisage an API for integration within common EO analysis environments. The catalogue is openly available at https://github.com/awesome-spectral-indices/awesome-earth-observation-instruments.

</details>


### [199] [Vivace: Exact Temporal OLAP over Interval Histories via Independent Serverless Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14069)
*Woohyeok Park,Taeyoon Kim,Hyunjoon Kim,Kungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Vivace 是一个基于无服务器架构的精确时间OLAP系统，通过预查询布局（复制跨边界区间）和查询时合并（聚合、排序等操作符特定规则）两阶段方法，解决了无服务器函数拆分计算导致的数据不完整和部分结果求和错误两个问题，在AWS Lambda上相比SQL基线降低高达82%延迟和84%成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间OLAP分析通常以不频繁、间歇性的突发方式到达，无服务器执行仅在查询时启动函数，比常开集群更具成本优势。但将单进程计算拆分到独立无服务器函数时，存在两个正确性问题：函数可能无法接收到确定其时间范围状态的行，以及简单累加部分结果对持续时间加权和累积阈值查询会得出错误答案。现有SQL引擎和无服务器分析系统未能同时解决这两个问题。

Method: Vivace 采用两阶段方法：(1) 预查询布局阶段——对区间历史进行分区，复制跨边界的区间，使每个函数能从一个完整文件中计算其时间范围；(2) 查询时合并阶段——根据操作符特定规则合并部分结果：结合性聚合合并中间值，排序则在每个时间范围内重新排序候选对象。论文证明这种分区执行在规范形式上等价于单进程计算。

Result: 在AWS Lambda上使用真实数据集进行评估，与直接查询历史的等效SQL基线相比，Vivace将延迟和货币成本分别降低高达82%和84%，展现出鲁棒的通用性和效率。

Conclusion: Vivace通过预查询分区复制和查询时操作符特定合并的无服务器架构，实现了对区间历史的精确时间OLAP分析，在保证正确性的同时显著降低了延迟和成本。

Abstract: Temporal online analytical processing (OLAP) analyzes past states of data whose values change over time. Such histories are naturally stored as interval histories, in which each row records the period during which a value remained valid. Because temporal analyses typically arrive in infrequent, intermittent bursts, serverless execution that launches functions only at query time offers a cost advantage over always-on clusters. Splitting a computation that a single process performs as a whole across independent serverless functions, however, breaks correctness in two ways. A function may not receive the rows that determine the state of its time range, and naively summing partial results yields incorrect answers for duration-weighted and cumulative-threshold queries. Existing SQL engines and serverless analytics do not address both problems together.
  This paper presents Vivace, a serverless system for exact temporal OLAP over interval histories. Vivace resolves the two problems in separate stages. Before any query arrives, a pre-query layout step partitions the interval history, replicating boundary-crossing intervals so each function computes its range completely from a single file. At query time, a merge step combines partial results under operator-specific rules. Associative aggregates merge intermediate values, and ranking re-orders candidates within each time range. We prove that this partitioned execution matches single-process computation up to canonical form. Evaluated on AWS Lambda with real-world datasets, Vivace reduces latency and monetary cost by up to 82% and 84%, respectively, against an equivalent SQL baseline that queries the history directly, demonstrating robust generality and efficiency.

</details>


### [200] [Revisiting Filtered ANN Benchmarks: A Hardness-Controlled Benchmark Generator for Realistic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14193)
*Mintaek Lim,Dogeun Kim,Minwoo Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于执行驱动查询难度的FANN评估指标α-Hardness，并设计了硬度可控的基准生成器HCBGen。实验表明现有基准位于较易的硬度区间，掩盖了鲁棒性问题，而匹配硬度分布可构建隐私保护的代理工作负载，缩小研究基准与真实评估之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FANN评估方法存在局限性：真实混合工作负载难以共享，而现有基准依赖于临时性的合成或半合成构造，导致评估结果不可靠。核心问题在于缺乏对查询执行驱动难度的刻画——早期过滤失败会触发过度抓取，直接影响延迟、吞吐量和召回率。

Method: 提出了α-Hardness度量，通过过抓取因子（over-fetch factor）建模条件执行链，并扩展到策略条件场景。基于此设计了HCBGen，一个硬度可控的基准生成器，能够通过粗粒度偏置模式或匹配目标硬度分布来合成工作负载。

Result: α-Hardness在不同数据集和混合策略下与经验性能呈现强单调对齐，而选择性、属性-向量相关性等常见代理指标常不稳定或策略不一致。现有基准占据窄且较易的硬度范围，掩盖了硬查询下的鲁棒性差距。匹配硬度分布可构建隐私保护代理工作负载，准确复现性能趋势。

Conclusion: α-Hardness是一种有效的查询级硬度度量，HCBGen可生成更真实的基准。通过控制硬度分布，能够构建隐私保护的代理工作负载，弥合研究基准与真实评估之间的鸿沟，提升FANN评估的现实性和可靠性。

Abstract: Filtered approximate nearest neighbor (FANN) search must satisfy both vector similarity and structured predicates, yet evaluations remain brittle because real hybrid workloads are rarely shareable and existing benchmarks rely on ad-hoc synthetic or semi-real constructions. We argue that realism hinges on execution-driven query difficulty: failures in early filtering trigger over-fetching of additional candidates, shaping latency, throughput, and recall. Building on this insight, we propose $α$-Hardness, a query-level hardness metric that models the conditional execution chain via the over-fetch factor and extends naturally to strategy-conditioned settings. Across diverse datasets and hybrid strategies, $α$-Hardness exhibits strong monotonic alignment with empirical performance, while common proxies such as selectivity or attribute-vector correlation are frequently unstable or strategy-inconsistent. We further introduce HCBGen, a hardness-controlled benchmark generator that uses $α$-Hardness as an explicit control signal to synthesize workloads under coarse bias modes or to match a target hardness profile. Our experiments show that widely used benchmarks occupy a narrow, relatively easy portion of the hardness spectrum, masking robustness gaps that emerge under harder queries. Finally, we demonstrate that matching hardness distributions enables privacy-preserving proxy workloads that closely reproduce performance trends, bridging research benchmarks and real evaluation.

</details>


### [201] [TACO: A Benchmark for Open-Domain Text-to-SQL with Ambiguous and Cross-Database Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14201)
*Chao Deng,Ju Fan,Yuyu Luo,Qinliang Xue,Meihao Fan,Yuxin Zhang,Min Zhang,Xiaofeng Jia,Jing Zhang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出TACO基准，用于开放域文本到SQL任务，包含1500个真实世界示例和13000个高质量合成示例，覆盖交通、医疗、金融等领域。实验表明现有方法距离人类水平仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL基准主要针对封闭域场景，无法应对开放域中问题模糊、数据库未指定、跨数据库查询等挑战。

Method: 构建了基于智慧城市数据服务的1500个真实示例和基于开放数据门户的13000个合成示例；开发了保持查询复杂性的数据合成流水线；提出了包含问题重写、表链接和查询规划的TACO-SQL基线。

Result: TACO-SQL在基准上取得最佳结果，但现有方法（包括TACO-SQL）与人工编写的SQL之间仍存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 开放域Text-to-SQL具有很高的难度，TACO可作为推动该领域未来研究的重要基准。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL aims to translate natural language questions into executable SQL queries over structured databases. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on closed-domain settings with predefined database schemas and well-specified questions, but they fall short in addressing the challenges of open-domain scenarios, such as ambiguous questions, unspecified databases, and cross-database querying. To bridge this gap, we introduce TACO, a benchmark for open-domain Text-to-SQL with Ambiguous and Cross-database queries. TACO consists of 1,500 real-world Text-to-SQL examples based on a smart city data service and 13,000 high-quality synthetic examples generated based on large-scale open data portals, covering diverse domains such as transportation, healthcare, and finance. To construct the synthetic examples, we develop an effective data synthesis pipeline that preserves the complexity of real-world queries. To demonstrate the utility of TACO, we introduce a baseline TACO-SQL composed of question rewriting, table linking, and query planning, to illustrate the challenges posed by TACO and to better understand the limitations of existing Text-to-SQL approaches. Extensive experiments on TACO using a variety of recent Text-to-SQL approaches show that, while TACO-SQL achieves the best results, a significant gap still remains between the existing approaches and human-written SQL. These findings highlight the difficulty of open-domain Text-to-SQL and position TACO as a valuable benchmark to drive future research.

</details>


### [202] [WikiKV: Schema-Evolving Path-Indexed Storage for Hierarchical Knowledge Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14275)
*Feifei Li,Haoliang Ming,Zihan Li,Hang Liao,Xingyu Fan,Xiaoqing Wu,Chenggong Wang,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出WikiKV，一种专为层级化知识库设计的路径索引键值存储模型，通过数据驱动模式归纳、一致性协议和预算导向导航算子，在微信公众平台AI助手实际部署中优于多种RAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有存储模型无法同时原生支持层级化知识库工作负载的三种核心特性：层级性、查询密集性和持续演化性，存储层仍被视为实现细节而缺乏专门优化。

Method: 提出WikiKV三组件：1) 基于Intent-Anchored Schema Induction的数据驱动模式归纳与Continuous Evolution Operators的持续优化；2) 路径索引存储模型的一致性协议，支持并发离线重写且无需读路径锁定即可避免部分读现象；3) 预算导向导航算子，通过搜索加速路由将LLM辅助下降步数从d降至O(1)，同时保持随时语义。

Result: 在AuthTrace数据集上，WikiKV在四种查询算子上实现了均衡的低延迟，端到端答案正确率达63.2%，超过多个RAG基线，在低扇入和高扇入多文档问题上差距更大。消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: WikiKV作为首个为层级化知识库工作量专门设计的存储模型，在真实部署中展现了优越性能，证明了路径索引键值存储在该场景下的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-curated hierarchical knowledge bases, namely a tree-structured wiki whose nodes summarize an underlying corpus, have become a dominant substrate for retrieval-augmented applications, yet their storage layer is still treated as an implementation detail. This workload is hierarchical, query-intensive, and continuously evolving, and no existing storage model natively captures all three properties at once. We present WikiKV, a path-indexed key-value storage model purpose-built for this workload, comprising three components: (i) a data-driven schema that bootstraps the hierarchy via Intent-Anchored Schema Induction and refines it through Continuous Evolution Operators; (ii) a consistency protocol for the path-indexed storage model that precludes partial-read observations under concurrent offline rewrites without read-path locking; and (iii) a budgeted navigation operator whose search-accelerated routing reduces the expected number of LLM-assisted descent steps from d to O(1) while preserving anytime semantics with progressively refined answers. We evaluate WikiKV through real-world deployment for the WeChat Official Account AI Assistant and benchmark it against diverse baselines on the AuthTrace dataset, where it achieves balanced low per-operator latency across four query operators against relational, graph, and FS backends, and reaches 63.2% end-to-end answer correctness, exceeding multiple RAG baselines, with the gap widening on low- and high-fan-in multi-document questions. Ablation study further confirms the effectiveness of WikiKV's components.

</details>


### [203] [Transforming Shape Schemas with Composable Property-Graph Queries (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14309)
*Philipp Seifer,Daniel Hernández,Ralf Lämmel,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通过映射到RDF/SHACL和SPARQL CONSTRUCT来推断属性图模式约束的方法，从而在给定输入模式（ProGS）和转换查询（G-CORE）时自动推导输出模式，并确保对所有符合输入模式的图实例都有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性图可以用模式（schema）约束数据形状，但经过可组合的查询转换后，输出图应遵循何种模式尚不清楚。现有工作虽对SPARQL CONSTRUCT查询有所研究，但属性图包含标签、属性注解和一等边等特性，直接编码到描述逻辑中存在困难，因此需要新的推理方法。

Method: 提出一种映射族方案：(i) 将属性图重化为RDF；(ii) 将ProGS模式映射到SHACL；(iii) 将G-CORE查询映射到SPARQL CONSTRUCT。通过这一额外映射层，将属性图模式推断问题分解为可管理的子问题，并利用高效的描述逻辑推理器进行推理。

Result: 建立了推断模式约束的可靠性元理论，证明了映射后的模式与查询在语义上等价，且推断出的输出模式对所有源自符合输入模式的输入图的图实例均适用。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了属性图场景下的模式自动推断，通过映射层将问题降维到现有DL推理工具可处理的范畴，为属性图查询的静态分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Property graphs may be constrained by schemas that inform both query engines and human users about the shape of valid data, enforcing a contract between data provider and consumer. Composable property-graph queries transform input graphs into output graphs. Then, the question arises of which schema can be expected after one (or several) transformation steps. We investigate how schema constraints can be inferred given an input schema and a transforming query. Specifically, we propose a reasoning procedure that, given an input schema in ProGS and a query in G-CORE infers an output schema. Since graph updates will happen frequently, our inference procedure does not rely on graph instances, such that the computed output schema applies to all graphs originating from any input graph complying with the input schema. Related work has addressed this problem for SPARQL CONSTRUCT queries, encoding it in Description Logics (DLs) so that the output schema is entailed by axioms inferred from input schema and queries. Property graphs and their queries, however, complicate the matter, as property graphs feature label and property annotations as well as first-class edges. Thus, reification has to be used in one way or another, though available DLs lack the means to encode such features directly. We approach this novel challenge via a family of mappings for i) property graphs reified in RDF, aligned with ii) a mapping from ProGS to SHACL and iii) a mapping from G-CORE to SPARQL CONSTRUCT queries. In this manner, schema inference for property graphs becomes manageable, as we break apart the problem through the extra mapping layer and utilize efficient DL reasoners. We develop the metatheory regarding the soundness of inferred schema constraints and the semantic equivalence of mapped schemas and queries.

</details>


### [204] [PLRTune: Importance Pre-Sampling and LLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Automatic Database Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14312)
*Xinyue Yang,Chen Zheng,Yaoyang Hou,Renhao Zhang,Yinyan Zhang,Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PLRTune 是一个分阶段数据库调优系统，通过工作负载特定的领域知识识别缩减搜索空间，并在此区域内进一步优化。它包含重要性预采样与重排序、执行引导的提示精炼以及基于TD3的后调优精炼三个阶段。在MySQL和PostgreSQL上的实验表明，PLRTune相比最先进方法平均提升9.50%的性能，并快9.03倍达到最优基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据库配置调优至关重要，但现有自动调优面临高维旋钮空间、高昂在线调优成本、LLM或社区文档提供的不可靠文本提示，以及初始化后难以利用剩余优化空间等挑战。

Method: 提出PLRTune分阶段调优系统：(1) 重要性预采样与重排序策略，以工作负载特定方式识别主导旋钮子集并生成紧凑状态表示；(2) 执行引导的提示精炼技术，提升文档引导调优的初始化质量；(3) 基于TD3的后调优精炼阶段，探索主导旋钮子集并进一步挖掘剩余优化空间。

Result: 在MySQL和PostgreSQL上多种基准工作负载下评估，PLRTune在所有测试工作负载上均达到最佳最终结果，相比最优替代方案平均提升9.50%；平均快9.03倍达到最强基线的性能水平。

Conclusion: PLRTune通过分阶段设计有效解决了高维空间、不可靠提示和剩余优化空间利用等挑战，兼顾了最终调优质量和运行时效率，是一种实用的数据库自动调优方案。

Abstract: Configuration tuning is critical to database performance, yet automatic database tuning remains challenging due to high-dimensional knob spaces, substantial online tuning cost, unreliable textual hints derived from Large Language Models (LLMs) or community documents, and the difficulty of exploiting the remaining optimization room after initialization.
  Hence, we propose PLRTune, a staged database tuning system that leverages workload-specific domain knowledge to identify a reduced search space and further optimize within this promising region. First, we develop an importance pre-sampling and reranking strategy to identify the dominant knob subset in a workload-specific manner and derive a compact state representation. Second, we design an execution-guided hint refinement technique to improve the initialization quality of documentation-guided tuning. Finally, we propose a post-tuning refinement stage that leverages Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) to explore the dominant knob subset and further exploit the remaining optimization room.
  We evaluate PLRTune on MySQL and PostgreSQL across diverse benchmark workloads. Compared with state-of-the-art approaches, PLRTune achieves the best final result on all tested workloads, improving over the corresponding best-performing alternative by 9.50% on average. Moreover, PLRTune reaches the strongest baseline's best performance level 9.03 times faster on average across workloads, demonstrating its practical runtime efficiency without sacrificing final tuning quality.

</details>


### [205] [Evaluating and Generating Query Workloads for High Dimensional Vector Similarity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14511)
*Matteo Ceccarello,Alexandra Levchenko,Ioana Ileana,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文指出相似性搜索的基准测试缺乏对查询难度控制的问题，提出两种查询负载合成方法HephAnn和HephGrad，用于生成指定难度目标的查询负载，以更全面地评估算法的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相似性搜索基准测试中，查询负载的难度通常不受控制，导致算法评估不够全面和严格。作者希望解决这一缺陷，使基准测试能更有针对性地检测算法在不同难度下的表现。

Method: 首先评估多种查询难度度量与经验查询难度（即索引结构为回答查询所付出的计算努力）之间的相关性。然后提出两种合成方法：HephGrad（更快但假设更多）和HephAnn（更通用但假设更少），用于生成满足用户指定难度目标的查询负载。

Result: 两种方法均能有效生成具有目标难度的查询负载。HephGrad速度更快，HephAnn对目标难度度量的假设更少，适用范围更广。

Conclusion: 生成的查询负载可用于深入分析相似性搜索算法在不同难度下的行为，为基准测试提供更精细的控制手段。

Abstract: Similarity search lies at the heart of many modern applications, ranging from databases to deep learning to data series analysis. As such, a vast effort has been invested in developing algorithms, data structures and implementations to speed up this crucial subroutine. To empirically validate these approaches, several benchmarking efforts have been initiated covering a wide array of datasets. In this paper, we observe that usually little control is exercised on the hardness of the workloads with which methods are tested and compared. To address this issue, we first evaluate several query hardness measures with respect to their ability to capture the empirical hardness of a query, i.e. the effort invested by an index data structure to provide an answer. Then, we propose two methods, deemed \HephAnn and \HephGrad, for synthesizing query workloads so that they meet a user-specified hardness target. Both methods allow to produce workloads with the desired hardness: we find that \HephGrad is faster, while \HephAnn makes fewer assumptions on the target hardness measure. The resulting workloads can be used to gain insights into the behavior of similarity search algorithms.

</details>
